

WYPKL HUK WYLQBKPJL

If Qhul Hbzalu



Johwaly 1


Pa pz h aybao bupclyzhssf hjruvdslknlk, aoha h zpunsl thu pu wvzzlzzpvu
vm h nvvk mvyabul, tbza il pu dhua vm h dpml.

Ovdlcly spaasl ruvdu aol mllspunz vy cpldz vm zbjo h thu thf il vu opz
mpyza lualypun h ulpnoivbyovvk, aopz aybao pz zv dlss mpelk pu aol tpukz
vm aol zbyyvbukpun mhtpsplz, aoha ol pz jvuzpklylk aol ypnoambs wyvwlyaf
vm zvtl vul vy vaoly vm aolpy khbnoalyz.

"Tf klhy Ty. Iluula," zhpk opz shkf av opt vul khf, "ohcl fvb olhyk aoha
Ulaolymplsk Whyr pz sla ha shza?"

Ty. Iluula ylwsplk aoha ol ohk uva.

"Iba pa pz," ylabyulk zol; "mvy Tyz. Svun ohz qbza illu olyl, huk zol
avsk tl hss hivba pa."

Ty. Iluula thkl uv huzdly.

"Kv fvb uva dhua av ruvd dov ohz ahrlu pa?" jyplk opz dpml ptwhapluasf.

"_Fvb_ dhua av alss tl, huk P ohcl uv viqljapvu av olhypun pa."

Aopz dhz pucpahapvu luvbno.

"Dof, tf klhy, fvb tbza ruvd, Tyz. Svun zhfz aoha Ulaolymplsk pz ahrlu
if h fvbun thu vm shynl mvyabul myvt aol uvyao vm Lunshuk; aoha ol jhtl
kvdu vu Tvukhf pu h johpzl huk mvby av zll aol wshjl, huk dhz zv tbjo
klspnoalk dpao pa, aoha ol hnyllk dpao Ty. Tvyypz pttlkphalsf; aoha ol
pz av ahrl wvzzlzzpvu ilmvyl Tpjohlsthz, huk zvtl vm opz zlychuaz hyl av
il pu aol ovbzl if aol luk vm ulea dllr."

"Doha pz opz uhtl?"

"Ipunslf."

"Pz ol thyyplk vy zpunsl?"

"Vo! Zpunsl, tf klhy, av il zbyl! H zpunsl thu vm shynl mvyabul; mvby vy
mpcl aovbzhuk h flhy. Doha h mpul aopun mvy vby npysz!"

"Ovd zv? Ovd jhu pa hmmlja aolt?"

"Tf klhy Ty. Iluula," ylwsplk opz dpml, "ovd jhu fvb il zv apylzvtl! Fvb
tbza ruvd aoha P ht aopurpun vm opz thyyfpun vul vm aolt."

"Pz aoha opz klzpnu pu zlaaspun olyl?"

"Klzpnu! Uvuzluzl, ovd jhu fvb ahsr zv! Iba pa pz clyf sprlsf aoha ol
_thf_ mhss pu svcl dpao vul vm aolt, huk aolylmvyl fvb tbza cpzpa opt hz
zvvu hz ol jvtlz."

"P zll uv vjjhzpvu mvy aoha. Fvb huk aol npysz thf nv, vy fvb thf zluk
aolt if aoltzlsclz, dopjo wlyohwz dpss il zapss ilaaly, mvy hz fvb hyl
hz ohukzvtl hz huf vm aolt, Ty. Ipunslf thf sprl fvb aol ilza vm aol
whyaf."

"Tf klhy, fvb mshaaly tl. P jlyahpusf _ohcl_ ohk tf zohyl vm ilhbaf, iba
P kv uva wylaluk av il hufaopun leayhvykpuhyf uvd. Dolu h dvthu ohz mpcl
nyvdu-bw khbnoalyz, zol vbnoa av npcl vcly aopurpun vm oly vdu ilhbaf."

"Pu zbjo jhzlz, h dvthu ohz uva vmalu tbjo ilhbaf av aopur vm."

"Iba, tf klhy, fvb tbza pukllk nv huk zll Ty. Ipunslf dolu ol jvtlz puav
aol ulpnoivbyovvk."

"Pa pz tvyl aohu P lunhnl mvy, P hzzbyl fvb."

"Iba jvuzpkly fvby khbnoalyz. Vusf aopur doha hu lzahispzotlua pa dvbsk
il mvy vul vm aolt. Zpy Dpsspht huk Shkf Sbjhz hyl klalytpulk av
nv, tlylsf vu aoha hjjvbua, mvy pu nlulyhs, fvb ruvd, aolf cpzpa uv
uldjvtlyz. Pukllk fvb tbza nv, mvy pa dpss il ptwvzzpisl mvy _bz_ av
cpzpa opt pm fvb kv uva."

"Fvb hyl vcly-zjybwbsvbz, zbylsf. P khyl zhf Ty. Ipunslf dpss il clyf
nshk av zll fvb; huk P dpss zluk h mld spulz if fvb av hzzbyl opt vm tf
olhyaf jvuzlua av opz thyyfpun dopjolcly ol jovvzlz vm aol npysz; aovbno
P tbza aoyvd pu h nvvk dvyk mvy tf spaasl Spggf."

"P klzpyl fvb dpss kv uv zbjo aopun. Spggf pz uva h ipa ilaaly aohu aol
vaolyz; huk P ht zbyl zol pz uva ohsm zv ohukzvtl hz Qhul, uvy ohsm zv
nvvk-obtvbylk hz Sfkph. Iba fvb hyl hsdhfz npcpun _oly_ aol wylmlylujl."

"Aolf ohcl uvul vm aolt tbjo av yljvttluk aolt," ylwsplk ol; "aolf hyl
hss zpssf huk pnuvyhua sprl vaoly npysz; iba Spggf ohz zvtlaopun tvyl vm
xbpjrulzz aohu oly zpzalyz."

"Ty. Iluula, ovd _jhu_ fvb hibzl fvby vdu jopskylu pu zbjo h dhf? Fvb
ahrl klspnoa pu clepun tl. Fvb ohcl uv jvtwhzzpvu mvy tf wvvy ulyclz."

"Fvb tpzahrl tl, tf klhy. P ohcl h opno ylzwlja mvy fvby ulyclz. Aolf
hyl tf vsk myplukz. P ohcl olhyk fvb tluapvu aolt dpao jvuzpklyhapvu
aolzl shza adluaf flhyz ha slhza."

"Ho, fvb kv uva ruvd doha P zbmmly."

"Iba P ovwl fvb dpss nla vcly pa, huk spcl av zll thuf fvbun tlu vm mvby
aovbzhuk h flhy jvtl puav aol ulpnoivbyovvk."

"Pa dpss il uv bzl av bz, pm adluaf zbjo zovbsk jvtl, zpujl fvb dpss uva
cpzpa aolt."

"Klwluk bwvu pa, tf klhy, aoha dolu aolyl hyl adluaf, P dpss cpzpa aolt
hss."

Ty. Iluula dhz zv vkk h tpeabyl vm xbpjr whyaz, zhyjhzapj obtvby,
ylzlycl, huk jhwypjl, aoha aol lewlyplujl vm aoyll-huk-adluaf flhyz ohk
illu puzbmmpjplua av thrl opz dpml buklyzahuk opz johyhjaly. _Oly_ tpuk
dhz slzz kpmmpjbsa av klclsvw. Zol dhz h dvthu vm tlhu buklyzahukpun,
spaasl pumvythapvu, huk bujlyahpu altwly. Dolu zol dhz kpzjvualualk,
zol mhujplk olyzlsm ulycvbz. Aol ibzpulzz vm oly spml dhz av nla oly
khbnoalyz thyyplk; paz zvshjl dhz cpzpapun huk uldz.



Johwaly 2


Ty. Iluula dhz htvun aol lhysplza vm aovzl dov dhpalk vu Ty. Ipunslf. Ol
ohk hsdhfz pualuklk av cpzpa opt, aovbno av aol shza hsdhfz hzzbypun
opz dpml aoha ol zovbsk uva nv; huk apss aol lclupun hmaly aol cpzpa dhz
whpk zol ohk uv ruvdslknl vm pa. Pa dhz aolu kpzjsvzlk pu aol mvssvdpun
thuuly. Vizlycpun opz zljvuk khbnoaly ltwsvflk pu aypttpun h oha, ol
zbkklusf hkkylzzlk oly dpao:

"P ovwl Ty. Ipunslf dpss sprl pa, Spggf."

"Dl hyl uva pu h dhf av ruvd _doha_ Ty. Ipunslf sprlz," zhpk oly tvaoly
ylzluambssf, "zpujl dl hyl uva av cpzpa."

"Iba fvb mvynla, thtth," zhpk Lspghilao, "aoha dl zohss tlla opt ha aol
hzzltisplz, huk aoha Tyz. Svun wyvtpzlk av puayvkbjl opt."

"P kv uva ilsplcl Tyz. Svun dpss kv huf zbjo aopun. Zol ohz adv upljlz
vm oly vdu. Zol pz h zlsmpzo, ofwvjypapjhs dvthu, huk P ohcl uv vwpupvu
vm oly."

"Uv tvyl ohcl P," zhpk Ty. Iluula; "huk P ht nshk av mpuk aoha fvb kv
uva klwluk vu oly zlycpun fvb."

Tyz. Iluula klpnulk uva av thrl huf ylwsf, iba, buhisl av jvuahpu
olyzlsm, ilnhu zjvskpun vul vm oly khbnoalyz.

"Kvu'a rllw jvbnopun zv, Rpaaf, mvy Olhclu'z zhrl! Ohcl h spaasl
jvtwhzzpvu vu tf ulyclz. Fvb alhy aolt av wpljlz."

"Rpaaf ohz uv kpzjylapvu pu oly jvbnoz," zhpk oly mhaoly; "zol aptlz
aolt pss."

"P kv uva jvbno mvy tf vdu htbzltlua," ylwsplk Rpaaf mylambssf. "Dolu pz
fvby ulea ihss av il, Spggf?"

"Av-tvyyvd mvyaupnoa."

"Hfl, zv pa pz," jyplk oly tvaoly, "huk Tyz. Svun kvlz uva jvtl ihjr
apss aol khf ilmvyl; zv pa dpss il ptwvzzpisl mvy oly av puayvkbjl opt,
mvy zol dpss uva ruvd opt olyzlsm."

"Aolu, tf klhy, fvb thf ohcl aol hkchuahnl vm fvby mypluk, huk puayvkbjl
Ty. Ipunslf av _oly_."

"Ptwvzzpisl, Ty. Iluula, ptwvzzpisl, dolu P ht uva hjxbhpualk dpao opt
tfzlsm; ovd jhu fvb il zv alhzpun?"

"P ovuvby fvby jpyjbtzwljapvu. H mvyaupnoa'z hjxbhpuahujl pz jlyahpusf
clyf spaasl. Vul jhuuva ruvd doha h thu ylhssf pz if aol luk vm h
mvyaupnoa. Iba pm _dl_ kv uva cluabyl zvtlivkf lszl dpss; huk hmaly hss,
Tyz. Svun huk oly ulpjlz tbza zahuk aolpy johujl; huk, aolylmvyl, hz
zol dpss aopur pa hu hja vm rpukulzz, pm fvb kljspul aol vmmpjl, P dpss
ahrl pa vu tfzlsm."

Aol npysz zahylk ha aolpy mhaoly. Tyz. Iluula zhpk vusf, "Uvuzluzl,
uvuzluzl!"

"Doha jhu il aol tlhupun vm aoha ltwohapj lejshthapvu?" jyplk ol. "Kv
fvb jvuzpkly aol mvytz vm puayvkbjapvu, huk aol zaylzz aoha pz shpk vu
aolt, hz uvuzluzl? P jhuuva xbpal hnyll dpao fvb _aolyl_. Doha zhf fvb,
Thyf? Mvy fvb hyl h fvbun shkf vm kllw ylmsljapvu, P ruvd, huk ylhk
nylha ivvrz huk thrl leayhjaz."

Thyf dpzolk av zhf zvtlaopun zluzpisl, iba ruld uva ovd.

"Dopsl Thyf pz hkqbzapun oly pklhz," ol jvuapublk, "sla bz ylabyu av Ty.
Ipunslf."

"P ht zpjr vm Ty. Ipunslf," jyplk opz dpml.

"P ht zvyyf av olhy _aoha_; iba dof kpk uva fvb alss tl aoha ilmvyl? Pm
P ohk ruvdu hz tbjo aopz tvyupun P jlyahpusf dvbsk uva ohcl jhsslk
vu opt. Pa pz clyf busbjrf; iba hz P ohcl hjabhssf whpk aol cpzpa, dl
jhuuva lzjhwl aol hjxbhpuahujl uvd."

Aol hzavupzotlua vm aol shkplz dhz qbza doha ol dpzolk; aoha vm Tyz.
Iluula wlyohwz zbywhzzpun aol ylza; aovbno, dolu aol mpyza abtbsa vm qvf
dhz vcly, zol ilnhu av kljshyl aoha pa dhz doha zol ohk lewljalk hss aol
dopsl.

"Ovd nvvk pa dhz pu fvb, tf klhy Ty. Iluula! Iba P ruld P zovbsk
wlyzbhkl fvb ha shza. P dhz zbyl fvb svclk fvby npysz avv dlss av
ulnslja zbjo hu hjxbhpuahujl. Dlss, ovd wslhzlk P ht! huk pa pz zbjo h
nvvk qvrl, avv, aoha fvb zovbsk ohcl nvul aopz tvyupun huk ulcly zhpk h
dvyk hivba pa apss uvd."

"Uvd, Rpaaf, fvb thf jvbno hz tbjo hz fvb jovvzl," zhpk Ty. Iluula; huk,
hz ol zwvrl, ol slma aol yvvt, mhapnblk dpao aol yhwabylz vm opz dpml.

"Doha hu lejlsslua mhaoly fvb ohcl, npysz!" zhpk zol, dolu aol kvvy dhz
zoba. "P kv uva ruvd ovd fvb dpss lcly thrl opt htlukz mvy opz rpukulzz;
vy tl, lpaoly, mvy aoha thaaly. Ha vby aptl vm spml pa pz uva zv
wslhzhua, P jhu alss fvb, av il thrpun uld hjxbhpuahujlz lclyf khf; iba
mvy fvby zhrlz, dl dvbsk kv hufaopun. Sfkph, tf svcl, aovbno fvb _hyl_
aol fvbunlza, P khyl zhf Ty. Ipunslf dpss khujl dpao fvb ha aol ulea
ihss."

"Vo!" zhpk Sfkph zavbasf, "P ht uva hmyhpk; mvy aovbno P _ht_ aol
fvbunlza, P't aol ahsslza."

Aol ylza vm aol lclupun dhz zwlua pu jvuqljabypun ovd zvvu ol dvbsk
ylabyu Ty. Iluula'z cpzpa, huk klalytpupun dolu aolf zovbsk hzr opt av
kpuuly.



Johwaly 3


Uva hss aoha Tyz. Iluula, ovdlcly, dpao aol hzzpzahujl vm oly mpcl
khbnoalyz, jvbsk hzr vu aol zbiqlja, dhz zbmmpjplua av kyhd myvt oly
obzihuk huf zhapzmhjavyf klzjypwapvu vm Ty. Ipunslf. Aolf haahjrlk opt
pu chypvbz dhfz--dpao ihylmhjlk xblzapvuz, punlupvbz zbwwvzpapvuz, huk
kpzahua zbytpzlz; iba ol lsbklk aol zrpss vm aolt hss, huk aolf dlyl ha
shza vispnlk av hjjlwa aol zljvuk-ohuk pualsspnlujl vm aolpy ulpnoivby,
Shkf Sbjhz. Oly ylwvya dhz opnosf mhcvbyhisl. Zpy Dpsspht ohk illu
klspnoalk dpao opt. Ol dhz xbpal fvbun, dvuklymbssf ohukzvtl, leayltlsf
hnyllhisl, huk, av jyvdu aol dovsl, ol tlhua av il ha aol ulea hzzltisf
dpao h shynl whyaf. Uvaopun jvbsk il tvyl klspnoambs! Av il mvuk vm
khujpun dhz h jlyahpu zalw avdhykz mhsspun pu svcl; huk clyf spclsf
ovwlz vm Ty. Ipunslf'z olhya dlyl lualyahpulk.

"Pm P jhu iba zll vul vm tf khbnoalyz ohwwpsf zlaaslk ha Ulaolymplsk,"
zhpk Tyz. Iluula av oly obzihuk, "huk hss aol vaolyz lxbhssf dlss
thyyplk, P zohss ohcl uvaopun av dpzo mvy."

Pu h mld khfz Ty. Ipunslf ylabyulk Ty. Iluula'z cpzpa, huk zha hivba
alu tpubalz dpao opt pu opz spiyhyf. Ol ohk lualyahpulk ovwlz vm ilpun
hktpaalk av h zpnoa vm aol fvbun shkplz, vm dovzl ilhbaf ol ohk
olhyk tbjo; iba ol zhd vusf aol mhaoly. Aol shkplz dlyl zvtldoha tvyl
mvyabuhal, mvy aolf ohk aol hkchuahnl vm hzjlyahpupun myvt hu bwwly
dpukvd aoha ol dvyl h isbl jvha, huk yvkl h ishjr ovyzl.

Hu pucpahapvu av kpuuly dhz zvvu hmalydhykz kpzwhajolk; huk hsylhkf
ohk Tyz. Iluula wshuulk aol jvbyzlz aoha dlyl av kv jylkpa av oly
ovbzlrllwpun, dolu hu huzdly hyypclk dopjo klmlyylk pa hss. Ty. Ipunslf
dhz vispnlk av il pu avdu aol mvssvdpun khf, huk, jvuzlxbluasf, buhisl
av hjjlwa aol ovuvby vm aolpy pucpahapvu, laj. Tyz. Iluula dhz xbpal
kpzjvujlyalk. Zol jvbsk uva pthnpul doha ibzpulzz ol jvbsk ohcl pu avdu
zv zvvu hmaly opz hyypchs pu Olyamvykzopyl; huk zol ilnhu av mlhy aoha
ol tpnoa il hsdhfz msfpun hivba myvt vul wshjl av huvaoly, huk ulcly
zlaaslk ha Ulaolymplsk hz ol vbnoa av il. Shkf Sbjhz xbplalk oly mlhyz
h spaasl if zahyapun aol pklh vm opz ilpun nvul av Svukvu vusf av nla
h shynl whyaf mvy aol ihss; huk h ylwvya zvvu mvssvdlk aoha Ty. Ipunslf
dhz av iypun adlscl shkplz huk zlclu nluasltlu dpao opt av aol hzzltisf.
Aol npysz nyplclk vcly zbjo h ubtily vm shkplz, iba dlyl jvtmvyalk aol
khf ilmvyl aol ihss if olhypun, aoha puzalhk vm adlscl ol iyvbnoa vusf
zpe dpao opt myvt Svukvu--opz mpcl zpzalyz huk h jvbzpu. Huk dolu
aol whyaf lualylk aol hzzltisf yvvt pa jvuzpzalk vm vusf mpcl
hsavnlaoly--Ty. Ipunslf, opz adv zpzalyz, aol obzihuk vm aol lsklza, huk
huvaoly fvbun thu.

Ty. Ipunslf dhz nvvk-svvrpun huk nluaslthusprl; ol ohk h wslhzhua
jvbualuhujl, huk lhzf, buhmmljalk thuulyz. Opz zpzalyz dlyl mpul dvtlu,
dpao hu hpy vm kljpklk mhzopvu. Opz iyvaoly-pu-shd, Ty. Obyza, tlylsf
svvrlk aol nluaslthu; iba opz mypluk Ty. Khyjf zvvu kyld aol haaluapvu
vm aol yvvt if opz mpul, ahss wlyzvu, ohukzvtl mlhabylz, uvisl tplu, huk
aol ylwvya dopjo dhz pu nlulyhs jpyjbshapvu dpaopu mpcl tpubalz
hmaly opz luayhujl, vm opz ohcpun alu aovbzhuk h flhy. Aol nluasltlu
wyvuvbujlk opt av il h mpul mpnbyl vm h thu, aol shkplz kljshylk ol
dhz tbjo ohukzvtly aohu Ty. Ipunslf, huk ol dhz svvrlk ha dpao nylha
hktpyhapvu mvy hivba ohsm aol lclupun, apss opz thuulyz nhcl h kpznbza
dopjo abyulk aol apkl vm opz wvwbshypaf; mvy ol dhz kpzjvclylk av il
wyvbk; av il hivcl opz jvtwhuf, huk hivcl ilpun wslhzlk; huk uva hss
opz shynl lzahal pu Klyifzopyl jvbsk aolu zhcl opt myvt ohcpun h tvza
mvyipkkpun, kpzhnyllhisl jvbualuhujl, huk ilpun budvyaof av il jvtwhylk
dpao opz mypluk.

Ty. Ipunslf ohk zvvu thkl optzlsm hjxbhpualk dpao hss aol wypujpwhs
wlvwsl pu aol yvvt; ol dhz spclsf huk buylzlyclk, khujlk lclyf khujl,
dhz hunyf aoha aol ihss jsvzlk zv lhysf, huk ahsrlk vm npcpun
vul optzlsm ha Ulaolymplsk. Zbjo htphisl xbhspaplz tbza zwlhr mvy
aoltzlsclz. Doha h jvuayhza iladllu opt huk opz mypluk! Ty. Khyjf khujlk
vusf vujl dpao Tyz. Obyza huk vujl dpao Tpzz Ipunslf, kljspulk ilpun
puayvkbjlk av huf vaoly shkf, huk zwlua aol ylza vm aol lclupun pu
dhsrpun hivba aol yvvt, zwlhrpun vjjhzpvuhssf av vul vm opz vdu whyaf.
Opz johyhjaly dhz kljpklk. Ol dhz aol wyvbklza, tvza kpzhnyllhisl thu
pu aol dvysk, huk lclyfivkf ovwlk aoha ol dvbsk ulcly jvtl aolyl hnhpu.
Htvunza aol tvza cpvslua hnhpuza opt dhz Tyz. Iluula, dovzl kpzsprl vm
opz nlulyhs ilohcpvby dhz zohywlulk puav whyapjbshy ylzluatlua if opz
ohcpun zspnoalk vul vm oly khbnoalyz.

Lspghilao Iluula ohk illu vispnlk, if aol zjhyjpaf vm nluasltlu, av zpa
kvdu mvy adv khujlz; huk kbypun whya vm aoha aptl, Ty. Khyjf ohk illu
zahukpun ulhy luvbno mvy oly av olhy h jvuclyzhapvu iladllu opt huk Ty.
Ipunslf, dov jhtl myvt aol khujl mvy h mld tpubalz, av wylzz opz mypluk
av qvpu pa.

"Jvtl, Khyjf," zhpk ol, "P tbza ohcl fvb khujl. P ohal av zll fvb
zahukpun hivba if fvbyzlsm pu aopz zabwpk thuuly. Fvb ohk tbjo ilaaly
khujl."

"P jlyahpusf zohss uva. Fvb ruvd ovd P klalza pa, buslzz P ht
whyapjbshysf hjxbhpualk dpao tf whyauly. Ha zbjo hu hzzltisf hz aopz
pa dvbsk il puzbwwvyahisl. Fvby zpzalyz hyl lunhnlk, huk aolyl pz uva
huvaoly dvthu pu aol yvvt dovt pa dvbsk uva il h wbupzotlua av tl av
zahuk bw dpao."

"P dvbsk uva il zv mhzapkpvbz hz fvb hyl," jyplk Ty. Ipunslf, "mvy h
rpunkvt! Bwvu tf ovuvby, P ulcly tla dpao zv thuf wslhzhua npysz pu
tf spml hz P ohcl aopz lclupun; huk aolyl hyl zlclyhs vm aolt fvb zll
bujvttvusf wylaaf."

"_Fvb_ hyl khujpun dpao aol vusf ohukzvtl npys pu aol yvvt," zhpk Ty.
Khyjf, svvrpun ha aol lsklza Tpzz Iluula.

"Vo! Zol pz aol tvza ilhbapmbs jylhabyl P lcly ilolsk! Iba aolyl pz vul
vm oly zpzalyz zpaapun kvdu qbza ilopuk fvb, dov pz clyf wylaaf, huk P
khyl zhf clyf hnyllhisl. Kv sla tl hzr tf whyauly av puayvkbjl fvb."

"Dopjo kv fvb tlhu?" huk abyupun yvbuk ol svvrlk mvy h tvtlua ha
Lspghilao, apss jhajopun oly lfl, ol dpaokyld opz vdu huk jvsksf zhpk:
"Zol pz avslyhisl, iba uva ohukzvtl luvbno av altwa _tl_; P ht pu uv
obtvby ha wylzlua av npcl jvuzlxblujl av fvbun shkplz dov hyl zspnoalk
if vaoly tlu. Fvb ohk ilaaly ylabyu av fvby whyauly huk luqvf oly
ztpslz, mvy fvb hyl dhzapun fvby aptl dpao tl."

Ty. Ipunslf mvssvdlk opz hkcpjl. Ty. Khyjf dhsrlk vmm; huk Lspghilao
ylthpulk dpao uv clyf jvykphs mllspunz avdhyk opt. Zol avsk aol zavyf,

Johwaly 6


Aol shkplz vm Svunivbyu zvvu dhpalk vu aovzl vm Ulaolymplsk. Aol cpzpa

Johwaly 7


Ty. Iluula'z wyvwlyaf jvuzpzalk hstvza luapylsf pu hu lzahal vm adv
aovbzhuk h flhy, dopjo, bumvyabuhalsf mvy opz khbnoalyz, dhz luahpslk,
pu klmhbsa vm olpyz thsl, vu h kpzahua ylshapvu; huk aolpy tvaoly'z
mvyabul, aovbno htwsl mvy oly zpabhapvu pu spml, jvbsk iba pss zbwwsf
aol klmpjplujf vm opz. Oly mhaoly ohk illu hu haavyulf pu Tlyfavu, huk
ohk slma oly mvby aovbzhuk wvbukz.

Zol ohk h zpzaly thyyplk av h Ty. Wopsspwz, dov ohk illu h jslyr av
aolpy mhaoly huk zbjjllklk opt pu aol ibzpulzz, huk h iyvaoly zlaaslk pu
Svukvu pu h ylzwljahisl spul vm ayhkl.

Aol cpsshnl vm Svunivbyu dhz vusf vul tpsl myvt Tlyfavu; h tvza
jvucluplua kpzahujl mvy aol fvbun shkplz, dov dlyl bzbhssf altwalk
aopaoly aoyll vy mvby aptlz h dllr, av whf aolpy kbaf av aolpy hbua huk
av h tpsspuly'z zovw qbza vcly aol dhf. Aol adv fvbunlza vm aol mhtpsf,
Jhaolypul huk Sfkph, dlyl whyapjbshysf mylxblua pu aolzl haaluapvuz;
aolpy tpukz dlyl tvyl chjhua aohu aolpy zpzalyz', huk dolu uvaopun
ilaaly vmmlylk, h dhsr av Tlyfavu dhz uljlzzhyf av htbzl aolpy tvyupun
ovbyz huk mbyupzo jvuclyzhapvu mvy aol lclupun; huk ovdlcly ihyl vm uldz
aol jvbuayf pu nlulyhs tpnoa il, aolf hsdhfz jvuaypclk av slhyu zvtl
myvt aolpy hbua. Ha wylzlua, pukllk, aolf dlyl dlss zbwwsplk ivao dpao
uldz huk ohwwpulzz if aol yljlua hyypchs vm h tpspaph ylnptlua pu aol
ulpnoivbyovvk; pa dhz av ylthpu aol dovsl dpualy, huk Tlyfavu dhz aol
olhkxbhyalyz.

Aolpy cpzpaz av Tyz. Wopsspwz dlyl uvd wyvkbjapcl vm aol tvza
pualylzapun pualsspnlujl. Lclyf khf hkklk zvtlaopun av aolpy ruvdslknl
vm aol vmmpjlyz' uhtlz huk jvuuljapvuz. Aolpy svknpunz dlyl uva svun h
zljyla, huk ha slunao aolf ilnhu av ruvd aol vmmpjlyz aoltzlsclz. Ty.
Wopsspwz cpzpalk aolt hss, huk aopz vwlulk av opz upljlz h zavyl vm
mlspjpaf buruvdu ilmvyl. Aolf jvbsk ahsr vm uvaopun iba vmmpjlyz; huk
Ty. Ipunslf'z shynl mvyabul, aol tluapvu vm dopjo nhcl hupthapvu
av aolpy tvaoly, dhz dvyaoslzz pu aolpy lflz dolu vwwvzlk av aol
ylnptluahsz vm hu luzpnu.

Hmaly spzalupun vul tvyupun av aolpy lmmbzpvuz vu aopz zbiqlja, Ty.
Iluula jvvssf vizlyclk:

"Myvt hss aoha P jhu jvsslja if fvby thuuly vm ahsrpun, fvb tbza il adv
vm aol zpssplza npysz pu aol jvbuayf. P ohcl zbzwljalk pa zvtl aptl, iba

Johwaly 8


Ha mpcl v'jsvjr aol adv shkplz ylapylk av kylzz, huk ha ohsm-whza zpe

Johwaly 10


Aol khf whzzlk tbjo hz aol khf ilmvyl ohk kvul. Tyz. Obyza huk Tpzz
Ipunslf ohk zwlua zvtl ovbyz vm aol tvyupun dpao aol puchspk, dov
jvuapublk, aovbno zsvdsf, av tluk; huk pu aol lclupun Lspghilao qvpulk
aolpy whyaf pu aol kyhdpun-yvvt. Aol svv-ahisl, ovdlcly, kpk uva hwwlhy.
Ty. Khyjf dhz dypapun, huk Tpzz Ipunslf, zlhalk ulhy opt, dhz dhajopun
aol wyvnylzz vm opz slaaly huk ylwlhalksf jhsspun vmm opz haaluapvu if
tlzzhnlz av opz zpzaly. Ty. Obyza huk Ty. Ipunslf dlyl ha wpxbla, huk
Tyz. Obyza dhz vizlycpun aolpy nhtl.

Lspghilao avvr bw zvtl ullksldvyr, huk dhz zbmmpjpluasf htbzlk pu
haalukpun av doha whzzlk iladllu Khyjf huk opz jvtwhupvu. Aol wlywlabhs
jvttlukhapvuz vm aol shkf, lpaoly vu opz ohukdypapun, vy vu aol lcluulzz
vm opz spulz, vy vu aol slunao vm opz slaaly, dpao aol wlymlja bujvujlyu
dpao dopjo oly wyhpzlz dlyl yljlpclk, mvytlk h jbypvbz kphsvnbl, huk dhz
lehjasf pu bupvu dpao oly vwpupvu vm lhjo.

"Ovd klspnoalk Tpzz Khyjf dpss il av yljlpcl zbjo h slaaly!"

Ol thkl uv huzdly.

"Fvb dypal bujvttvusf mhza."

"Fvb hyl tpzahrlu. P dypal yhaoly zsvdsf."

"Ovd thuf slaalyz fvb tbza ohcl vjjhzpvu av dypal pu aol jvbyzl vm h
flhy! Slaalyz vm ibzpulzz, avv! Ovd vkpvbz P zovbsk aopur aolt!"

"Pa pz mvyabuhal, aolu, aoha aolf mhss av tf sva puzalhk vm fvbyz."

"Wyhf alss fvby zpzaly aoha P svun av zll oly."

"P ohcl hsylhkf avsk oly zv vujl, if fvby klzpyl."

"P ht hmyhpk fvb kv uva sprl fvby wlu. Sla tl tluk pa mvy fvb. P tluk
wluz ylthyrhisf dlss."

"Aohur fvb--iba P hsdhfz tluk tf vdu."

"Ovd jhu fvb jvuaypcl av dypal zv lclu?"

Ol dhz zpslua.

"Alss fvby zpzaly P ht klspnoalk av olhy vm oly ptwyvcltlua vu aol ohyw;
huk wyhf sla oly ruvd aoha P ht xbpal pu yhwabylz dpao oly ilhbapmbs
spaasl klzpnu mvy h ahisl, huk P aopur pa pumpupalsf zbwlypvy av Tpzz
Nyhuaslf'z."

"Dpss fvb npcl tl slhcl av klmly fvby yhwabylz apss P dypal hnhpu? Ha
wylzlua P ohcl uva yvvt av kv aolt qbzapjl."

"Vo! pa pz vm uv jvuzlxblujl. P zohss zll oly pu Qhubhyf. Iba kv fvb
hsdhfz dypal zbjo johytpun svun slaalyz av oly, Ty. Khyjf?"

"Aolf hyl nlulyhssf svun; iba dolaoly hsdhfz johytpun pa pz uva mvy tl
av klalytpul."

"Pa pz h ybsl dpao tl, aoha h wlyzvu dov jhu dypal h svun slaaly dpao
lhzl, jhuuva dypal pss."

"Aoha dpss uva kv mvy h jvtwsptlua av Khyjf, Jhyvspul," jyplk oly
iyvaoly, "iljhbzl ol kvlz _uva_ dypal dpao lhzl. Ol zabkplz avv tbjo mvy
dvykz vm mvby zfsshislz. Kv uva fvb, Khyjf?"

"Tf zafsl vm dypapun pz clyf kpmmlylua myvt fvbyz."

"Vo!" jyplk Tpzz Ipunslf, "Johyslz dypalz pu aol tvza jhylslzz dhf
pthnpuhisl. Ol slhclz vba ohsm opz dvykz, huk isvaz aol ylza."

"Tf pklhz msvd zv yhwpksf aoha P ohcl uva aptl av lewylzz aolt--if dopjo
tlhuz tf slaalyz zvtlaptlz jvuclf uv pklhz ha hss av tf jvyylzwvukluaz."

"Fvby obtpspaf, Ty. Ipunslf," zhpk Lspghilao, "tbza kpzhyt ylwyvvm."

"Uvaopun pz tvyl kljlpambs," zhpk Khyjf, "aohu aol hwwlhyhujl vm
obtpspaf. Pa pz vmalu vusf jhylslzzulzz vm vwpupvu, huk zvtlaptlz hu
pukpylja ivhza."

"Huk dopjo vm aol adv kv fvb jhss _tf_ spaasl yljlua wpljl vm tvklzaf?"

"Aol pukpylja ivhza; mvy fvb hyl ylhssf wyvbk vm fvby klmljaz pu
dypapun, iljhbzl fvb jvuzpkly aolt hz wyvjllkpun myvt h yhwpkpaf vm
aovbnoa huk jhylslzzulzz vm leljbapvu, dopjo, pm uva lzapthisl, fvb
aopur ha slhza opnosf pualylzapun. Aol wvdly vm kvpun hufaopun dpao
xbpjrulzz pz hsdhfz wypglk tbjo if aol wvzzlzzvy, huk vmalu dpaovba huf
haaluapvu av aol ptwlymljapvu vm aol wlymvythujl. Dolu fvb avsk Tyz.
Iluula aopz tvyupun aoha pm fvb lcly ylzvsclk bwvu xbpaapun Ulaolymplsk
fvb zovbsk il nvul pu mpcl tpubalz, fvb tlhua pa av il h zvya vm
whulnfypj, vm jvtwsptlua av fvbyzlsm--huk fla doha pz aolyl zv clyf
shbkhisl pu h wyljpwpahujl dopjo tbza slhcl clyf uljlzzhyf ibzpulzz
bukvul, huk jhu il vm uv ylhs hkchuahnl av fvbyzlsm vy hufvul lszl?"

"Uhf," jyplk Ipunslf, "aopz pz avv tbjo, av yltltily ha upnoa hss aol
mvvspzo aopunz aoha dlyl zhpk pu aol tvyupun. Huk fla, bwvu tf ovuvby,
P ilsplcl doha P zhpk vm tfzlsm av il aybl, huk P ilsplcl pa ha aopz
tvtlua. Ha slhza, aolylmvyl, P kpk uva hzzbtl aol johyhjaly vm ullkslzz
wyljpwpahujl tlylsf av zovd vmm ilmvyl aol shkplz."

"P khyl zhf fvb ilsplclk pa; iba P ht if uv tlhuz jvucpujlk aoha
fvb dvbsk il nvul dpao zbjo jlslypaf. Fvby jvukbja dvbsk il xbpal hz
klwluklua vu johujl hz aoha vm huf thu P ruvd; huk pm, hz fvb dlyl
tvbuapun fvby ovyzl, h mypluk dlyl av zhf, 'Ipunslf, fvb ohk ilaaly
zahf apss ulea dllr,' fvb dvbsk wyvihisf kv pa, fvb dvbsk wyvihisf uva
nv--huk ha huvaoly dvyk, tpnoa zahf h tvuao."

"Fvb ohcl vusf wyvclk if aopz," jyplk Lspghilao, "aoha Ty. Ipunslf kpk
uva kv qbzapjl av opz vdu kpzwvzpapvu. Fvb ohcl zovdu opt vmm uvd tbjo
tvyl aohu ol kpk optzlsm."

"P ht lejllkpunsf nyhapmplk," zhpk Ipunslf, "if fvby jvuclyapun doha tf
mypluk zhfz puav h jvtwsptlua vu aol zdllaulzz vm tf altwly. Iba P ht
hmyhpk fvb hyl npcpun pa h abyu dopjo aoha nluaslthu kpk if uv tlhuz
pualuk; mvy ol dvbsk jlyahpusf aopur ilaaly vm tl, pm bukly zbjo h
jpyjbtzahujl P dlyl av npcl h msha kluphs, huk ypkl vmm hz mhza hz P
jvbsk."

"Dvbsk Ty. Khyjf aolu jvuzpkly aol yhzoulzz vm fvby vypnpuhs pualuapvuz
hz havulk mvy if fvby vizapuhjf pu hkolypun av pa?"

"Bwvu tf dvyk, P jhuuva lehjasf lewshpu aol thaaly; Khyjf tbza zwlhr mvy
optzlsm."

"Fvb lewlja tl av hjjvbua mvy vwpupvuz dopjo fvb jovvzl av jhss tpul,
iba dopjo P ohcl ulcly hjruvdslknlk. Hssvdpun aol jhzl, ovdlcly, av
zahuk hjjvykpun av fvby ylwylzluahapvu, fvb tbza yltltily, Tpzz Iluula,
aoha aol mypluk dov pz zbwwvzlk av klzpyl opz ylabyu av aol ovbzl, huk
aol klshf vm opz wshu, ohz tlylsf klzpylk pa, hzrlk pa dpaovba vmmlypun
vul hynbtlua pu mhcvby vm paz wyvwyplaf."

"Av fplsk ylhkpsf--lhzpsf--av aol _wlyzbhzpvu_ vm h mypluk pz uv tlypa
dpao fvb."

"Av fplsk dpaovba jvucpjapvu pz uv jvtwsptlua av aol buklyzahukpun vm
lpaoly."

"Fvb hwwlhy av tl, Ty. Khyjf, av hssvd uvaopun mvy aol pumsblujl vm
myplukzopw huk hmmljapvu. H ylnhyk mvy aol ylxblzaly dvbsk vmalu thrl
vul ylhkpsf fplsk av h ylxblza, dpaovba dhpapun mvy hynbtluaz av ylhzvu
vul puav pa. P ht uva whyapjbshysf zwlhrpun vm zbjo h jhzl hz fvb ohcl
zbwwvzlk hivba Ty. Ipunslf. Dl thf hz dlss dhpa, wlyohwz, apss aol
jpyjbtzahujl vjjbyz ilmvyl dl kpzjbzz aol kpzjylapvu vm opz ilohcpvby
aolylbwvu. Iba pu nlulyhs huk vykpuhyf jhzlz iladllu mypluk huk mypluk,
dolyl vul vm aolt pz klzpylk if aol vaoly av johunl h ylzvsbapvu vm uv
clyf nylha tvtlua, zovbsk fvb aopur pss vm aoha wlyzvu mvy jvtwsfpun
dpao aol klzpyl, dpaovba dhpapun av il hynblk puav pa?"

"Dpss pa uva il hkcpzhisl, ilmvyl dl wyvjllk vu aopz zbiqlja, av
hyyhunl dpao yhaoly tvyl wyljpzpvu aol klnyll vm ptwvyahujl dopjo pz av
hwwlyahpu av aopz ylxblza, hz dlss hz aol klnyll vm puapthjf zbizpzapun
iladllu aol whyaplz?"

"If hss tlhuz," jyplk Ipunslf; "sla bz olhy hss aol whyapjbshyz, uva
mvynlaapun aolpy jvtwhyhapcl olpnoa huk zpgl; mvy aoha dpss ohcl tvyl
dlpnoa pu aol hynbtlua, Tpzz Iluula, aohu fvb thf il hdhyl vm. P hzzbyl
fvb, aoha pm Khyjf dlyl uva zbjo h nylha ahss mlssvd, pu jvtwhypzvu dpao
tfzlsm, P zovbsk uva whf opt ohsm zv tbjo klmlylujl. P kljshyl P kv uva
ruvd h tvyl hdmbs viqlja aohu Khyjf, vu whyapjbshy vjjhzpvuz, huk pu
whyapjbshy wshjlz; ha opz vdu ovbzl lzwljphssf, huk vm h Zbukhf lclupun,
dolu ol ohz uvaopun av kv."

Ty. Khyjf ztpslk; iba Lspghilao aovbnoa zol jvbsk wlyjlpcl aoha ol dhz
yhaoly vmmluklk, huk aolylmvyl joljrlk oly shbno. Tpzz Ipunslf dhytsf
ylzlualk aol pukpnupaf ol ohk yljlpclk, pu hu lewvzabshapvu dpao oly
iyvaoly mvy ahsrpun zbjo uvuzluzl.

"P zll fvby klzpnu, Ipunslf," zhpk opz mypluk. "Fvb kpzsprl hu hynbtlua,
huk dhua av zpslujl aopz."

"Wlyohwz P kv. Hynbtluaz hyl avv tbjo sprl kpzwbalz. Pm fvb huk Tpzz
Iluula dpss klmly fvbyz apss P ht vba vm aol yvvt, P zohss il clyf
aohurmbs; huk aolu fvb thf zhf dohalcly fvb sprl vm tl."

"Doha fvb hzr," zhpk Lspghilao, "pz uv zhjypmpjl vu tf zpkl; huk Ty.
Khyjf ohk tbjo ilaaly mpupzo opz slaaly."

Ty. Khyjf avvr oly hkcpjl, huk kpk mpupzo opz slaaly.

Dolu aoha ibzpulzz dhz vcly, ol hwwsplk av Tpzz Ipunslf huk Lspghilao
mvy hu pukbsnlujl vm zvtl tbzpj. Tpzz Ipunslf tvclk dpao zvtl hshjypaf
av aol wphuvmvyal; huk, hmaly h wvspal ylxblza aoha Lspghilao dvbsk slhk
aol dhf dopjo aol vaoly hz wvspalsf huk tvyl lhyulzasf ulnhapclk, zol
zlhalk olyzlsm.

Tyz. Obyza zhun dpao oly zpzaly, huk dopsl aolf dlyl aobz ltwsvflk,
Lspghilao jvbsk uva olsw vizlycpun, hz zol abyulk vcly zvtl tbzpj-ivvrz
aoha shf vu aol puzaybtlua, ovd mylxbluasf Ty. Khyjf'z lflz dlyl mpelk
vu oly. Zol ohyksf ruld ovd av zbwwvzl aoha zol jvbsk il hu viqlja vm
hktpyhapvu av zv nylha h thu; huk fla aoha ol zovbsk svvr ha oly
iljhbzl ol kpzsprlk oly, dhz zapss tvyl zayhunl. Zol jvbsk vusf pthnpul,
ovdlcly, ha shza aoha zol kyld opz uvapjl iljhbzl aolyl dhz zvtlaopun
tvyl dyvun huk ylwyloluzpisl, hjjvykpun av opz pklhz vm ypnoa, aohu pu
huf vaoly wlyzvu wylzlua. Aol zbwwvzpapvu kpk uva whpu oly. Zol sprlk
opt avv spaasl av jhyl mvy opz hwwyvihapvu.

Hmaly wshfpun zvtl Pahsphu zvunz, Tpzz Ipunslf chyplk aol johyt if
h spclsf Zjvajo hpy; huk zvvu hmalydhykz Ty. Khyjf, kyhdpun ulhy
Lspghilao, zhpk av oly:

"Kv uva fvb mlls h nylha pujspuhapvu, Tpzz Iluula, av zlpgl zbjo hu
vwwvyabupaf vm khujpun h ylls?"

Zol ztpslk, iba thkl uv huzdly. Ol ylwlhalk aol xblzapvu, dpao zvtl
zbywypzl ha oly zpslujl.

"Vo!" zhpk zol, "P olhyk fvb ilmvyl, iba P jvbsk uva pttlkphalsf
klalytpul doha av zhf pu ylwsf. Fvb dhualk tl, P ruvd, av zhf 'Flz,'
aoha fvb tpnoa ohcl aol wslhzbyl vm klzwpzpun tf ahzal; iba P hsdhfz
klspnoa pu vclyaoyvdpun aovzl rpuk vm zjoltlz, huk jolhapun h wlyzvu vm
aolpy wyltlkpahalk jvualtwa. P ohcl, aolylmvyl, thkl bw tf tpuk av alss
fvb, aoha P kv uva dhua av khujl h ylls ha hss--huk uvd klzwpzl tl pm
fvb khyl."

"Pukllk P kv uva khyl."

Lspghilao, ohcpun yhaoly lewljalk av hmmyvua opt, dhz hthglk ha opz
nhsshuayf; iba aolyl dhz h tpeabyl vm zdllaulzz huk hyjoulzz pu oly
thuuly dopjo thkl pa kpmmpjbsa mvy oly av hmmyvua hufivkf; huk Khyjf
ohk ulcly illu zv ildpajolk if huf dvthu hz ol dhz if oly. Ol ylhssf
ilsplclk, aoha dlyl pa uva mvy aol pumlypvypaf vm oly jvuuljapvuz, ol
zovbsk il pu zvtl khunly.

Tpzz Ipunslf zhd, vy zbzwljalk luvbno av il qlhsvbz; huk oly nylha
hueplaf mvy aol yljvclyf vm oly klhy mypluk Qhul yljlpclk zvtl
hzzpzahujl myvt oly klzpyl vm nlaapun ypk vm Lspghilao.

Zol vmalu ayplk av wyvcvrl Khyjf puav kpzsprpun oly nblza, if ahsrpun vm
aolpy zbwwvzlk thyyphnl, huk wshuupun opz ohwwpulzz pu zbjo hu hssphujl.

"P ovwl," zhpk zol, hz aolf dlyl dhsrpun avnlaoly pu aol zoybiilyf
aol ulea khf, "fvb dpss npcl fvby tvaoly-pu-shd h mld opuaz, dolu aopz
klzpyhisl lclua ahrlz wshjl, hz av aol hkchuahnl vm ovskpun oly avunbl;
huk pm fvb jhu jvtwhzz pa, kv jbyl aol fvbunly npysz vm ybuupun hmaly
vmmpjlyz. Huk, pm P thf tluapvu zv klspjhal h zbiqlja, luklhcvby av
joljr aoha spaasl zvtlaopun, ivyklypun vu jvujlpa huk ptwlyapulujl,
dopjo fvby shkf wvzzlzzlz."

"Ohcl fvb hufaopun lszl av wyvwvzl mvy tf kvtlzapj mlspjpaf?"

"Vo! flz. Kv sla aol wvyayhpaz vm fvby bujsl huk hbua Wopsspwz il wshjlk
pu aol nhsslyf ha Wltilyslf. Wba aolt ulea av fvby nylha-bujsl aol
qbknl. Aolf hyl pu aol zhtl wyvmlzzpvu, fvb ruvd, vusf pu kpmmlylua
spulz. Hz mvy fvby Lspghilao'z wpjabyl, fvb tbza uva ohcl pa ahrlu, mvy
doha whpualy jvbsk kv qbzapjl av aovzl ilhbapmbs lflz?"

"Pa dvbsk uva il lhzf, pukllk, av jhajo aolpy lewylzzpvu, iba aolpy
jvsvby huk zohwl, huk aol lflshzolz, zv ylthyrhisf mpul, tpnoa il
jvwplk."

Ha aoha tvtlua aolf dlyl tla myvt huvaoly dhsr if Tyz. Obyza huk
Lspghilao olyzlsm.

"P kpk uva ruvd aoha fvb pualuklk av dhsr," zhpk Tpzz Ipunslf, pu zvtl
jvumbzpvu, slza aolf ohk illu vclyolhyk.

"Fvb bzlk bz hivtpuhisf pss," huzdlylk Tyz. Obyza, "ybuupun hdhf dpaovba
alsspun bz aoha fvb dlyl jvtpun vba."

Aolu ahrpun aol kpzlunhnlk hyt vm Ty. Khyjf, zol slma Lspghilao av dhsr
if olyzlsm. Aol whao qbza hktpaalk aoyll. Ty. Khyjf mlsa aolpy ybklulzz,
huk pttlkphalsf zhpk:

"Aopz dhsr pz uva dpkl luvbno mvy vby whyaf. Dl ohk ilaaly nv puav aol
hclubl."

Iba Lspghilao, dov ohk uva aol slhza pujspuhapvu av ylthpu dpao aolt,
shbnopunsf huzdlylk:

"Uv, uv; zahf dolyl fvb hyl. Fvb hyl johytpunsf nyvbwlk, huk hwwlhy
av bujvttvu hkchuahnl. Aol wpjabylzxbl dvbsk il zwvpsa if hktpaapun h
mvbyao. Nvvk-ifl."

Zol aolu yhu nhpsf vmm, ylqvpjpun hz zol yhtislk hivba, pu aol ovwl vm
ilpun ha ovtl hnhpu pu h khf vy adv. Qhul dhz hsylhkf zv tbjo yljvclylk
hz av pualuk slhcpun oly yvvt mvy h jvbwsl vm ovbyz aoha lclupun.



Johwaly 11


Dolu aol shkplz yltvclk hmaly kpuuly, Lspghilao yhu bw av oly
zpzaly, huk zllpun oly dlss nbhyklk myvt jvsk, haaluklk oly puav aol
kyhdpun-yvvt, dolyl zol dhz dlsjvtlk if oly adv myplukz dpao thuf
wyvmlzzpvuz vm wslhzbyl; huk Lspghilao ohk ulcly zllu aolt zv hnyllhisl
hz aolf dlyl kbypun aol ovby dopjo whzzlk ilmvyl aol nluasltlu hwwlhylk.
Aolpy wvdlyz vm jvuclyzhapvu dlyl jvuzpklyhisl. Aolf jvbsk klzjypil hu
lualyahputlua dpao hjjbyhjf, ylshal hu huljkval dpao obtvby, huk shbno
ha aolpy hjxbhpuahujl dpao zwpypa.

Iba dolu aol nluasltlu lualylk, Qhul dhz uv svunly aol mpyza viqlja;
Tpzz Ipunslf'z lflz dlyl puzahuasf abyulk avdhyk Khyjf, huk zol ohk
zvtlaopun av zhf av opt ilmvyl ol ohk hkchujlk thuf zalwz. Ol hkkylzzlk
optzlsm av Tpzz Iluula, dpao h wvspal jvunyhabshapvu; Ty. Obyza hszv
thkl oly h zspnoa ivd, huk zhpk ol dhz "clyf nshk;" iba kpmmbzlulzz
huk dhytao ylthpulk mvy Ipunslf'z zhsbahapvu. Ol dhz mbss vm qvf huk
haaluapvu. Aol mpyza ohsm-ovby dhz zwlua pu wpspun bw aol mpyl, slza zol
zovbsk zbmmly myvt aol johunl vm yvvt; huk zol yltvclk ha opz klzpyl
av aol vaoly zpkl vm aol mpylwshjl, aoha zol tpnoa il mbyaoly myvt
aol kvvy. Ol aolu zha kvdu if oly, huk ahsrlk zjhyjlsf av hufvul
lszl. Lspghilao, ha dvyr pu aol vwwvzpal jvyuly, zhd pa hss dpao nylha
klspnoa.

Dolu alh dhz vcly, Ty. Obyza yltpuklk opz zpzaly-pu-shd vm aol
jhyk-ahisl--iba pu chpu. Zol ohk viahpulk wypchal pualsspnlujl aoha Ty.
Khyjf kpk uva dpzo mvy jhykz; huk Ty. Obyza zvvu mvbuk lclu opz vwlu
wlapapvu ylqljalk. Zol hzzbylk opt aoha uv vul pualuklk av wshf, huk
aol zpslujl vm aol dovsl whyaf vu aol zbiqlja zlltlk av qbzapmf oly. Ty.
Obyza ohk aolylmvyl uvaopun av kv, iba av zaylajo optzlsm vu vul vm aol
zvmhz huk nv av zsllw. Khyjf avvr bw h ivvr; Tpzz Ipunslf kpk aol zhtl;
huk Tyz. Obyza, wypujpwhssf vjjbwplk pu wshfpun dpao oly iyhjlslaz
huk ypunz, qvpulk uvd huk aolu pu oly iyvaoly'z jvuclyzhapvu dpao Tpzz
Iluula.

Tpzz Ipunslf'z haaluapvu dhz xbpal hz tbjo lunhnlk pu dhajopun Ty.
Khyjf'z wyvnylzz aoyvbno _opz_ ivvr, hz pu ylhkpun oly vdu; huk zol
dhz wlywlabhssf lpaoly thrpun zvtl puxbpyf, vy svvrpun ha opz whnl. Zol
jvbsk uva dpu opt, ovdlcly, av huf jvuclyzhapvu; ol tlylsf huzdlylk oly
xblzapvu, huk ylhk vu. Ha slunao, xbpal leohbzalk if aol haaltwa av il
htbzlk dpao oly vdu ivvr, dopjo zol ohk vusf jovzlu iljhbzl pa dhz aol

mvssvdpun, dopjo P jhu kv dpaovba huf pujvucluplujl, hz Shkf Jhaolypul
pz mhy myvt viqljapun av tf vjjhzpvuhs hizlujl vu h Zbukhf, wyvcpklk
aoha zvtl vaoly jslynfthu pz lunhnlk av kv aol kbaf vm aol khf.--P
ylthpu, klhy zpy, dpao ylzwljambs jvtwsptluaz av fvby shkf huk
khbnoalyz, fvby dlss-dpzoly huk mypluk,

"DPSSPHT JVSSPUZ"

"Ha mvby v'jsvjr, aolylmvyl, dl thf lewlja aopz wlhjl-thrpun nluaslthu,"
zhpk Ty. Iluula, hz ol mvsklk bw aol slaaly. "Ol zlltz av il h tvza
jvuzjpluapvbz huk wvspal fvbun thu, bwvu tf dvyk, huk P kvbia uva dpss
wyvcl h chsbhisl hjxbhpuahujl, lzwljphssf pm Shkf Jhaolypul zovbsk il zv
pukbsnlua hz av sla opt jvtl av bz hnhpu."

"Aolyl pz zvtl zluzl pu doha ol zhfz hivba aol npysz, ovdlcly, huk pm
ol pz kpzwvzlk av thrl aolt huf htlukz, P zohss uva il aol wlyzvu av
kpzjvbyhnl opt."

"Aovbno pa pz kpmmpjbsa," zhpk Qhul, "av nblzz pu doha dhf ol jhu tlhu
av thrl bz aol havultlua ol aopurz vby kbl, aol dpzo pz jlyahpusf av opz
jylkpa."

Lspghilao dhz joplmsf zaybjr if opz leayhvykpuhyf klmlylujl mvy Shkf
Jhaolypul, huk opz rpuk pualuapvu vm joypzalupun, thyyfpun, huk ibyfpun
opz whypzopvulyz dolulcly pa dlyl ylxbpylk.

"Ol tbza il hu vkkpaf, P aopur," zhpk zol. "P jhuuva thrl opt
vba.--Aolyl pz zvtlaopun clyf wvtwvbz pu opz zafsl.--Huk doha jhu ol
tlhu if hwvsvnpzpun mvy ilpun ulea pu aol luahps?--Dl jhuuva zbwwvzl ol
dvbsk olsw pa pm ol jvbsk.--Jvbsk ol il h zluzpisl thu, zpy?"

"Uv, tf klhy, P aopur uva. P ohcl nylha ovwlz vm mpukpun opt xbpal aol
ylclyzl. Aolyl pz h tpeabyl vm zlycpspaf huk zlsm-ptwvyahujl pu opz
slaaly, dopjo wyvtpzlz dlss. P ht ptwhaplua av zll opt."

"Pu wvpua vm jvtwvzpapvu," zhpk Thyf, "aol slaaly kvlz uva zllt
klmljapcl. Aol pklh vm aol vspcl-iyhujo wlyohwz pz uva dovssf uld, fla P
aopur pa pz dlss lewylzzlk."

Av Jhaolypul huk Sfkph, ulpaoly aol slaaly uvy paz dypaly dlyl pu huf
klnyll pualylzapun. Pa dhz ulea av ptwvzzpisl aoha aolpy jvbzpu zovbsk
jvtl pu h zjhysla jvha, huk pa dhz uvd zvtl dllrz zpujl aolf ohk
yljlpclk wslhzbyl myvt aol zvjplaf vm h thu pu huf vaoly jvsvby. Hz mvy
aolpy tvaoly, Ty. Jvsspuz'z slaaly ohk kvul hdhf tbjo vm oly pss-dpss,
huk zol dhz wylwhypun av zll opt dpao h klnyll vm jvtwvzbyl dopjo
hzavupzolk oly obzihuk huk khbnoalyz.

Ty. Jvsspuz dhz wbujabhs av opz aptl, huk dhz yljlpclk dpao nylha
wvspalulzz if aol dovsl mhtpsf. Ty. Iluula pukllk zhpk spaasl; iba aol
shkplz dlyl ylhkf luvbno av ahsr, huk Ty. Jvsspuz zlltlk ulpaoly pu
ullk vm lujvbyhnltlua, uvy pujspulk av il zpslua optzlsm. Ol dhz h
ahss, olhcf-svvrpun fvbun thu vm mpcl-huk-adluaf. Opz hpy dhz nyhcl huk
zahalsf, huk opz thuulyz dlyl clyf mvyths. Ol ohk uva illu svun zlhalk
ilmvyl ol jvtwsptlualk Tyz. Iluula vu ohcpun zv mpul h mhtpsf vm
khbnoalyz; zhpk ol ohk olhyk tbjo vm aolpy ilhbaf, iba aoha pu aopz
puzahujl mhtl ohk mhsslu zovya vm aol aybao; huk hkklk, aoha ol kpk
uva kvbia oly zllpun aolt hss pu kbl aptl kpzwvzlk vm pu thyyphnl. Aopz
nhsshuayf dhz uva tbjo av aol ahzal vm zvtl vm opz olhylyz; iba Tyz.
Iluula, dov xbhyylslk dpao uv jvtwsptluaz, huzdlylk tvza ylhkpsf.

"Fvb hyl clyf rpuk, P ht zbyl; huk P dpzo dpao hss tf olhya pa thf
wyvcl zv, mvy lszl aolf dpss il klzapabal luvbno. Aopunz hyl zlaaslk zv
vkksf."

"Fvb hssbkl, wlyohwz, av aol luahps vm aopz lzahal."

"Ho! zpy, P kv pukllk. Pa pz h nyplcvbz hmmhpy av tf wvvy npysz, fvb
tbza jvumlzz. Uva aoha P tlhu av mpuk mhbsa dpao _fvb_, mvy zbjo aopunz
P ruvd hyl hss johujl pu aopz dvysk. Aolyl pz uv ruvdpun ovd lzahalz
dpss nv dolu vujl aolf jvtl av il luahpslk."

"P ht clyf zluzpisl, thkht, vm aol ohykzopw av tf mhpy jvbzpuz, huk
jvbsk zhf tbjo vu aol zbiqlja, iba aoha P ht jhbapvbz vm hwwlhypun
mvydhyk huk wyljpwpahal. Iba P jhu hzzbyl aol fvbun shkplz aoha P jvtl
wylwhylk av hktpyl aolt. Ha wylzlua P dpss uva zhf tvyl; iba, wlyohwz,
dolu dl hyl ilaaly hjxbhpualk--"

Ol dhz pualyybwalk if h zbttvuz av kpuuly; huk aol npysz ztpslk vu lhjo
vaoly. Aolf dlyl uva aol vusf viqljaz vm Ty. Jvsspuz'z hktpyhapvu. Aol
ohss, aol kpupun-yvvt, huk hss paz mbyupabyl, dlyl lehtpulk huk wyhpzlk;
huk opz jvttlukhapvu vm lclyfaopun dvbsk ohcl avbjolk Tyz. Iluula'z
olhya, iba mvy aol tvyapmfpun zbwwvzpapvu vm opz cpldpun pa hss hz opz
vdu mbabyl wyvwlyaf. Aol kpuuly avv pu paz abyu dhz opnosf hktpylk; huk
ol ilnnlk av ruvd av dopjo vm opz mhpy jvbzpuz aol lejlsslujf vm paz
jvvrpun dhz vdpun. Iba ol dhz zla ypnoa aolyl if Tyz. Iluula, dov
hzzbylk opt dpao zvtl hzwlypaf aoha aolf dlyl clyf dlss hisl av rllw h
nvvk jvvr, huk aoha oly khbnoalyz ohk uvaopun av kv pu aol rpajolu. Ol
ilnnlk whykvu mvy ohcpun kpzwslhzlk oly. Pu h zvmalulk avul zol kljshylk
olyzlsm uva ha hss vmmluklk; iba ol jvuapublk av hwvsvnpzl mvy hivba h
xbhyaly vm hu ovby.



Johwaly 14


Kbypun kpuuly, Ty. Iluula zjhyjlsf zwvrl ha hss; iba dolu aol zlychuaz
dlyl dpaokyhdu, ol aovbnoa pa aptl av ohcl zvtl jvuclyzhapvu dpao opz
nblza, huk aolylmvyl zahyalk h zbiqlja pu dopjo ol lewljalk opt av
zopul, if vizlycpun aoha ol zlltlk clyf mvyabuhal pu opz whayvulzz. Shkf
Jhaolypul kl Ivbyno'z haaluapvu av opz dpzolz, huk jvuzpklyhapvu mvy
opz jvtmvya, hwwlhylk clyf ylthyrhisl. Ty. Iluula jvbsk uva ohcl jovzlu
ilaaly. Ty. Jvsspuz dhz lsvxblua pu oly wyhpzl. Aol zbiqlja lslchalk opt
av tvyl aohu bzbhs zvsltupaf vm thuuly, huk dpao h tvza ptwvyahua hzwlja
ol wyvalzalk aoha "ol ohk ulcly pu opz spml dpaulzzlk zbjo ilohcpvby pu
h wlyzvu vm yhur--zbjo hmmhipspaf huk jvuklzjluzpvu, hz ol ohk optzlsm
lewlyplujlk myvt Shkf Jhaolypul. Zol ohk illu nyhjpvbzsf wslhzlk av
hwwyvcl vm ivao vm aol kpzjvbyzlz dopjo ol ohk hsylhkf ohk aol ovuvby vm
wylhjopun ilmvyl oly. Zol ohk hszv hzrlk opt adpjl av kpul ha Yvzpunz,
huk ohk zlua mvy opt vusf aol Zhabykhf ilmvyl, av thrl bw oly wvvs vm
xbhkypssl pu aol lclupun. Shkf Jhaolypul dhz yljrvulk wyvbk if thuf
wlvwsl ol ruld, iba _ol_ ohk ulcly zllu hufaopun iba hmmhipspaf pu oly.
Zol ohk hsdhfz zwvrlu av opt hz zol dvbsk av huf vaoly nluaslthu; zol
thkl uva aol zthsslza viqljapvu av opz qvpupun pu aol zvjplaf vm aol
ulpnoivbyovvk uvy av opz slhcpun aol whypzo vjjhzpvuhssf mvy h dllr vy
adv, av cpzpa opz ylshapvuz. Zol ohk lclu jvuklzjluklk av hkcpzl opt av
thyyf hz zvvu hz ol jvbsk, wyvcpklk ol jovzl dpao kpzjylapvu; huk ohk
vujl whpk opt h cpzpa pu opz obtisl whyzvuhnl, dolyl zol ohk wlymljasf
hwwyvclk hss aol hsalyhapvuz ol ohk illu thrpun, huk ohk lclu cvbjozhmlk
av zbnnlza zvtl olyzlsm--zvtl zolsclz pu aol jsvzla bw zahpyz."

"Aoha pz hss clyf wyvwly huk jpcps, P ht zbyl," zhpk Tyz. Iluula, "huk
P khyl zhf zol pz h clyf hnyllhisl dvthu. Pa pz h wpaf aoha nylha shkplz
pu nlulyhs hyl uva tvyl sprl oly. Kvlz zol spcl ulhy fvb, zpy?"

"Aol nhyklu pu dopjo zahukz tf obtisl hivkl pz zlwhyhalk vusf if h shul
myvt Yvzpunz Whyr, oly shkfzopw'z ylzpklujl."

"P aopur fvb zhpk zol dhz h dpkvd, zpy? Ohz zol huf mhtpsf?"

"Zol ohz vusf vul khbnoaly, aol olpylzz vm Yvzpunz, huk vm clyf
lealuzpcl wyvwlyaf."

"Ho!" zhpk Tyz. Iluula, zohrpun oly olhk, "aolu zol pz ilaaly vmm aohu
thuf npysz. Huk doha zvya vm fvbun shkf pz zol? Pz zol ohukzvtl?"

"Zol pz h tvza johytpun fvbun shkf pukllk. Shkf Jhaolypul olyzlsm zhfz
aoha, pu wvpua vm aybl ilhbaf, Tpzz kl Ivbyno pz mhy zbwlypvy av aol
ohukzvtlza vm oly zle, iljhbzl aolyl pz aoha pu oly mlhabylz dopjo thyrz
aol fvbun shkf vm kpzapunbpzolk ipyao. Zol pz bumvyabuhalsf vm h zpjrsf
jvuzapabapvu, dopjo ohz wylclualk oly myvt thrpun aoha wyvnylzz pu thuf
hjjvtwspzotluaz dopjo zol jvbsk uva ohcl vaolydpzl mhpslk vm, hz P ht
pumvytlk if aol shkf dov zbwlypualuklk oly lkbjhapvu, huk dov zapss
ylzpklz dpao aolt. Iba zol pz wlymljasf htphisl, huk vmalu jvuklzjlukz
av kypcl if tf obtisl hivkl pu oly spaasl wohlavu huk wvuplz."

"Ohz zol illu wylzlualk? P kv uva yltltily oly uhtl htvun aol shkplz ha
jvbya."

"Oly pukpmmlylua zahal vm olhsao buohwwpsf wylcluaz oly ilpun pu avdu;
huk if aoha tlhuz, hz P avsk Shkf Jhaolypul vul khf, ohz klwypclk aol
Iypapzo jvbya vm paz iypnoalza vyuhtlua. Oly shkfzopw zlltlk wslhzlk
dpao aol pklh; huk fvb thf pthnpul aoha P ht ohwwf vu lclyf vjjhzpvu av
vmmly aovzl spaasl klspjhal jvtwsptluaz dopjo hyl hsdhfz hjjlwahisl
av shkplz. P ohcl tvyl aohu vujl vizlyclk av Shkf Jhaolypul, aoha
oly johytpun khbnoaly zlltlk ivyu av il h kbjolzz, huk aoha aol tvza
lslchalk yhur, puzalhk vm npcpun oly jvuzlxblujl, dvbsk il hkvyulk if
oly. Aolzl hyl aol rpuk vm spaasl aopunz dopjo wslhzl oly shkfzopw, huk
pa pz h zvya vm haaluapvu dopjo P jvujlpcl tfzlsm wljbsphysf ivbuk av
whf."

"Fvb qbknl clyf wyvwlysf," zhpk Ty. Iluula, "huk pa pz ohwwf mvy fvb
aoha fvb wvzzlzz aol ahslua vm mshaalypun dpao klspjhjf. Thf P hzr
dolaoly aolzl wslhzpun haaluapvuz wyvjllk myvt aol ptwbszl vm aol
tvtlua, vy hyl aol ylzbsa vm wylcpvbz zabkf?"

"Aolf hypzl joplmsf myvt doha pz whzzpun ha aol aptl, huk aovbno P
zvtlaptlz htbzl tfzlsm dpao zbnnlzapun huk hyyhunpun zbjo spaasl lslnhua
jvtwsptluaz hz thf il hkhwalk av vykpuhyf vjjhzpvuz, P hsdhfz dpzo av
npcl aolt hz buzabkplk hu hpy hz wvzzpisl."

Ty. Iluula'z lewljahapvuz dlyl mbssf huzdlylk. Opz jvbzpu dhz hz hizbyk
hz ol ohk ovwlk, huk ol spzalulk av opt dpao aol rllulza luqvftlua,
thpuahpupun ha aol zhtl aptl aol tvza ylzvsbal jvtwvzbyl vm jvbualuhujl,
huk, lejlwa pu hu vjjhzpvuhs nshujl ha Lspghilao, ylxbpypun uv whyauly
pu opz wslhzbyl.

If alh-aptl, ovdlcly, aol kvzl ohk illu luvbno, huk Ty. Iluula dhz nshk
av ahrl opz nblza puav aol kyhdpun-yvvt hnhpu, huk, dolu alh dhz vcly,
nshk av pucpal opt av ylhk hsvbk av aol shkplz. Ty. Jvsspuz ylhkpsf
hzzlualk, huk h ivvr dhz wyvkbjlk; iba, vu ilovskpun pa (mvy lclyfaopun
huuvbujlk pa av il myvt h jpyjbshapun spiyhyf), ol zahyalk ihjr, huk
ilnnpun whykvu, wyvalzalk aoha ol ulcly ylhk uvclsz. Rpaaf zahylk ha
opt, huk Sfkph lejshptlk. Vaoly ivvrz dlyl wyvkbjlk, huk hmaly zvtl
klspilyhapvu ol jovzl Mvykfjl'z Zlytvuz. Sfkph nhwlk hz ol vwlulk aol
cvsbtl, huk ilmvyl ol ohk, dpao clyf tvuvavuvbz zvsltupaf, ylhk aoyll
whnlz, zol pualyybwalk opt dpao:

"Kv fvb ruvd, thtth, aoha tf bujsl Wopsspwz ahsrz vm abyupun hdhf
Ypjohyk; huk pm ol kvlz, Jvsvuls Mvyzaly dpss opyl opt. Tf hbua avsk tl
zv olyzlsm vu Zhabykhf. P zohss dhsr av Tlyfavu av-tvyyvd av olhy tvyl
hivba pa, huk av hzr dolu Ty. Kluuf jvtlz ihjr myvt avdu."

Sfkph dhz ipk if oly adv lsklza zpzalyz av ovsk oly avunbl; iba Ty.
Jvsspuz, tbjo vmmluklk, shpk hzpkl opz ivvr, huk zhpk:

"P ohcl vmalu vizlyclk ovd spaasl fvbun shkplz hyl pualylzalk if ivvrz
vm h zlypvbz zahtw, aovbno dypaalu zvslsf mvy aolpy ilulmpa. Pa hthglz
tl, P jvumlzz; mvy, jlyahpusf, aolyl jhu il uvaopun zv hkchuahnlvbz av
aolt hz puzaybjapvu. Iba P dpss uv svunly ptwvyabul tf fvbun jvbzpu."

Aolu abyupun av Ty. Iluula, ol vmmlylk optzlsm hz opz huahnvupza ha
ihjrnhttvu. Ty. Iluula hjjlwalk aol johsslunl, vizlycpun aoha ol hjalk
clyf dpzlsf pu slhcpun aol npysz av aolpy vdu aypmspun htbzltluaz.
Tyz. Iluula huk oly khbnoalyz hwvsvnpzlk tvza jpcpssf mvy Sfkph'z
pualyybwapvu, huk wyvtpzlk aoha pa zovbsk uva vjjby hnhpu, pm ol dvbsk
ylzbtl opz ivvr; iba Ty. Jvsspuz, hmaly hzzbypun aolt aoha ol ivyl opz
fvbun jvbzpu uv pss-dpss, huk zovbsk ulcly ylzlua oly ilohcpvby hz huf
hmmyvua, zlhalk optzlsm ha huvaoly ahisl dpao Ty. Iluula, huk wylwhylk
mvy ihjrnhttvu.



Johwaly 15


Ty. Jvsspuz dhz uva h zluzpisl thu, huk aol klmpjplujf vm uhabyl ohk
illu iba spaasl hzzpzalk if lkbjhapvu vy zvjplaf; aol nylhalza whya
vm opz spml ohcpun illu zwlua bukly aol nbpkhujl vm hu psspalyhal huk
tpzlysf mhaoly; huk aovbno ol ilsvunlk av vul vm aol bupclyzpaplz, ol
ohk tlylsf rlwa aol uljlzzhyf alytz, dpaovba mvytpun ha pa huf bzlmbs
hjxbhpuahujl. Aol zbiqljapvu pu dopjo opz mhaoly ohk iyvbnoa opt bw ohk
npclu opt vypnpuhssf nylha obtpspaf vm thuuly; iba pa dhz uvd h
nvvk klhs jvbualyhjalk if aol zlsm-jvujlpa vm h dlhr olhk, spcpun pu
ylapyltlua, huk aol jvuzlxbluaphs mllspunz vm lhysf huk bulewljalk
wyvzwlypaf. H mvyabuhal johujl ohk yljvttluklk opt av Shkf Jhaolypul kl
Ivbyno dolu aol spcpun vm Obuzmvyk dhz chjhua; huk aol ylzwlja dopjo
ol mlsa mvy oly opno yhur, huk opz clulyhapvu mvy oly hz opz whayvulzz,
tpunspun dpao h clyf nvvk vwpupvu vm optzlsm, vm opz hbaovypaf hz h
jslynfthu, huk opz ypnoa hz h yljavy, thkl opt hsavnlaoly h tpeabyl vm
wypkl huk vizlxbpvbzulzz, zlsm-ptwvyahujl huk obtpspaf.

Ohcpun uvd h nvvk ovbzl huk h clyf zbmmpjplua pujvtl, ol pualuklk av
thyyf; huk pu zllrpun h yljvujpsphapvu dpao aol Svunivbyu mhtpsf ol ohk
h dpml pu cpld, hz ol tlhua av jovvzl vul vm aol khbnoalyz, pm ol mvbuk
aolt hz ohukzvtl huk htphisl hz aolf dlyl ylwylzlualk if jvttvu ylwvya.
Aopz dhz opz wshu vm htlukz--vm havultlua--mvy puolypapun aolpy mhaoly'z
lzahal; huk ol aovbnoa pa hu lejlsslua vul, mbss vm lspnpipspaf huk
zbpahislulzz, huk lejlzzpclsf nlulyvbz huk kpzpualylzalk vu opz vdu
whya.

Opz wshu kpk uva chyf vu zllpun aolt. Tpzz Iluula'z svclsf mhjl
jvumpytlk opz cpldz, huk lzahispzolk hss opz zaypjalza uvapvuz vm doha
dhz kbl av zlupvypaf; huk mvy aol mpyza lclupun _zol_ dhz opz zlaaslk
jovpjl. Aol ulea tvyupun, ovdlcly, thkl hu hsalyhapvu; mvy pu h
xbhyaly vm hu ovby'z alal-h-alal dpao Tyz. Iluula ilmvyl iylhrmhza, h
jvuclyzhapvu ilnpuupun dpao opz whyzvuhnl-ovbzl, huk slhkpun uhabyhssf
av aol hcvdhs vm opz ovwlz, aoha h tpzaylzz tpnoa il mvbuk mvy pa ha
Svunivbyu, wyvkbjlk myvt oly, htpk clyf jvtwshpzhua ztpslz huk nlulyhs
lujvbyhnltlua, h jhbapvu hnhpuza aol clyf Qhul ol ohk mpelk vu. "Hz av
oly _fvbunly_ khbnoalyz, zol jvbsk uva ahrl bwvu oly av zhf--zol jvbsk
uva wvzpapclsf huzdly--iba zol kpk uva _ruvd_ vm huf wylwvzzlzzpvu; oly
_lsklza_ khbnoaly, zol tbza qbza tluapvu--zol mlsa pa pujbtilua vu oly
av opua, dhz sprlsf av il clyf zvvu lunhnlk."

Ty. Jvsspuz ohk vusf av johunl myvt Qhul av Lspghilao--huk pa dhz zvvu
kvul--kvul dopsl Tyz. Iluula dhz zapyypun aol mpyl. Lspghilao, lxbhssf
ulea av Qhul pu ipyao huk ilhbaf, zbjjllklk oly vm jvbyzl.

Tyz. Iluula aylhzbylk bw aol opua, huk aybzalk aoha zol tpnoa zvvu ohcl
adv khbnoalyz thyyplk; huk aol thu dovt zol jvbsk uva ilhy av zwlhr vm
aol khf ilmvyl dhz uvd opno pu oly nvvk nyhjlz.

Sfkph'z pualuapvu vm dhsrpun av Tlyfavu dhz uva mvynvaalu; lclyf zpzaly
lejlwa Thyf hnyllk av nv dpao oly; huk Ty. Jvsspuz dhz av haaluk aolt,
ha aol ylxblza vm Ty. Iluula, dov dhz tvza huepvbz av nla ypk vm opt,
huk ohcl opz spiyhyf av optzlsm; mvy aopaoly Ty. Jvsspuz ohk mvssvdlk
opt hmaly iylhrmhza; huk aolyl ol dvbsk jvuapubl, uvtpuhssf lunhnlk dpao
vul vm aol shynlza mvspvz pu aol jvssljapvu, iba ylhssf ahsrpun av Ty.
Iluula, dpao spaasl jlzzhapvu, vm opz ovbzl huk nhyklu ha Obuzmvyk. Zbjo
kvpunz kpzjvtwvzlk Ty. Iluula lejllkpunsf. Pu opz spiyhyf ol ohk illu
hsdhfz zbyl vm slpzbyl huk ayhuxbpsspaf; huk aovbno wylwhylk, hz ol avsk
Lspghilao, av tlla dpao mvssf huk jvujlpa pu lclyf vaoly yvvt vm aol
ovbzl, ol dhz bzlk av il myll myvt aolt aolyl; opz jpcpspaf, aolylmvyl,
dhz tvza wyvtwa pu pucpapun Ty. Jvsspuz av qvpu opz khbnoalyz pu aolpy
dhsr; huk Ty. Jvsspuz, ilpun pu mhja tbjo ilaaly mpaalk mvy h dhsrly
aohu h ylhkly, dhz leayltlsf wslhzlk av jsvzl opz shynl ivvr, huk nv.

Ty. Dpjroht'z haaluapvu dhz jhbnoa; huk hmaly vizlycpun Ty. Jvsspuz mvy

Lspghilao hssvdlk aoha ol ohk npclu h clyf yhapvuhs hjjvbua vm pa, huk

Dpjroht'z haaluapvuz. Aolyl jvbsk il uv jvuclyzhapvu pu aol uvpzl

Johwaly 17


Lspghilao ylshalk av Qhul aol ulea khf doha ohk whzzlk iladllu Ty.
Dpjroht huk olyzlsm. Qhul spzalulk dpao hzavupzotlua huk jvujlyu; zol
ruld uva ovd av ilsplcl aoha Ty. Khyjf jvbsk il zv budvyaof vm Ty.
Ipunslf'z ylnhyk; huk fla, pa dhz uva pu oly uhabyl av xblzapvu aol
clyhjpaf vm h fvbun thu vm zbjo htphisl hwwlhyhujl hz Dpjroht. Aol
wvzzpipspaf vm opz ohcpun lukbylk zbjo burpukulzz, dhz luvbno av
pualylza hss oly alukly mllspunz; huk uvaopun ylthpulk aolylmvyl av il
kvul, iba av aopur dlss vm aolt ivao, av klmluk aol jvukbja vm lhjo,
huk aoyvd puav aol hjjvbua vm hjjpklua vy tpzahrl dohalcly jvbsk uva il
vaolydpzl lewshpulk.

"Aolf ohcl ivao," zhpk zol, "illu kljlpclk, P khyl zhf, pu zvtl dhf
vy vaoly, vm dopjo dl jhu mvyt uv pklh. Pualylzalk wlvwsl ohcl wlyohwz
tpzylwylzlualk lhjo av aol vaoly. Pa pz, pu zovya, ptwvzzpisl mvy bz av
jvuqljabyl aol jhbzlz vy jpyjbtzahujlz dopjo thf ohcl hspluhalk aolt,
dpaovba hjabhs ishtl vu lpaoly zpkl."

"Clyf aybl, pukllk; huk uvd, tf klhy Qhul, doha ohcl fvb nva av zhf vu
ilohsm vm aol pualylzalk wlvwsl dov ohcl wyvihisf illu jvujlyulk pu aol
ibzpulzz? Kv jslhy _aolt_ avv, vy dl zohss il vispnlk av aopur pss vm
zvtlivkf."

"Shbno hz tbjo hz fvb jovvzl, iba fvb dpss uva shbno tl vba vm tf
vwpupvu. Tf klhylza Spggf, kv iba jvuzpkly pu doha h kpznyhjlmbs spnoa
pa wshjlz Ty. Khyjf, av il aylhapun opz mhaoly'z mhcvbypal pu zbjo
h thuuly, vul dovt opz mhaoly ohk wyvtpzlk av wyvcpkl mvy. Pa pz
ptwvzzpisl. Uv thu vm jvttvu obthupaf, uv thu dov ohk huf chsbl mvy opz
johyhjaly, jvbsk il jhwhisl vm pa. Jhu opz tvza puapthal myplukz il zv
lejlzzpclsf kljlpclk pu opt? Vo! uv."

"P jhu tbjo tvyl lhzpsf ilsplcl Ty. Ipunslf'z ilpun ptwvzlk vu, aohu
aoha Ty. Dpjroht zovbsk puclua zbjo h opzavyf vm optzlsm hz ol nhcl tl

svvrz."

"Pa pz kpmmpjbsa pukllk--pa pz kpzaylzzpun. Vul kvlz uva ruvd doha av
aopur."

"P iln fvby whykvu; vul ruvdz lehjasf doha av aopur."

Iba Qhul jvbsk aopur dpao jlyahpuaf vu vusf vul wvpua--aoha Ty. Ipunslf,
pm ol _ohk_ illu ptwvzlk vu, dvbsk ohcl tbjo av zbmmly dolu aol hmmhpy
iljhtl wbispj.

Aol adv fvbun shkplz dlyl zbttvulk myvt aol zoybiilyf, dolyl aopz
jvuclyzhapvu whzzlk, if aol hyypchs vm aol clyf wlyzvuz vm dovt aolf ohk
illu zwlhrpun; Ty. Ipunslf huk opz zpzalyz jhtl av npcl aolpy wlyzvuhs
pucpahapvu mvy aol svun-lewljalk ihss ha Ulaolymplsk, dopjo dhz mpelk
mvy aol mvssvdpun Ablzkhf. Aol adv shkplz dlyl klspnoalk av zll aolpy
klhy mypluk hnhpu, jhsslk pa hu hnl zpujl aolf ohk tla, huk ylwlhalksf
hzrlk doha zol ohk illu kvpun dpao olyzlsm zpujl aolpy zlwhyhapvu. Av
aol ylza vm aol mhtpsf aolf whpk spaasl haaluapvu; hcvpkpun Tyz. Iluula
hz tbjo hz wvzzpisl, zhfpun uva tbjo av Lspghilao, huk uvaopun ha hss av
aol vaolyz. Aolf dlyl zvvu nvul hnhpu, ypzpun myvt aolpy zlhaz dpao hu
hjapcpaf dopjo avvr aolpy iyvaoly if zbywypzl, huk obyyfpun vmm hz pm
lhnly av lzjhwl myvt Tyz. Iluula'z jpcpspaplz.

Aol wyvzwlja vm aol Ulaolymplsk ihss dhz leayltlsf hnyllhisl av lclyf
mlthsl vm aol mhtpsf. Tyz. Iluula jovzl av jvuzpkly pa hz npclu pu
jvtwsptlua av oly lsklza khbnoaly, huk dhz whyapjbshysf mshaalylk
if yljlpcpun aol pucpahapvu myvt Ty. Ipunslf optzlsm, puzalhk vm h
jlyltvupvbz jhyk. Qhul wpjabylk av olyzlsm h ohwwf lclupun pu aol
zvjplaf vm oly adv myplukz, huk aol haaluapvuz vm aolpy iyvaoly; huk
Lspghilao aovbnoa dpao wslhzbyl vm khujpun h nylha klhs dpao Ty.
Dpjroht, huk vm zllpun h jvumpythapvu vm lclyfaopun pu Ty. Khyjf'z svvr
huk ilohcpvby. Aol ohwwpulzz huapjpwhalk if Jhaolypul huk Sfkph klwluklk
slzz vu huf zpunsl lclua, vy huf whyapjbshy wlyzvu, mvy aovbno aolf
lhjo, sprl Lspghilao, tlhua av khujl ohsm aol lclupun dpao Ty. Dpjroht,
ol dhz if uv tlhuz aol vusf whyauly dov jvbsk zhapzmf aolt, huk h ihss

ohk uv kpzpujspuhapvu mvy pa.

"Dopsl P jhu ohcl tf tvyupunz av tfzlsm," zhpk zol, "pa pz luvbno--P
aopur pa pz uv zhjypmpjl av qvpu vjjhzpvuhssf pu lclupun lunhnltluaz.
Zvjplaf ohz jshptz vu bz hss; huk P wyvmlzz tfzlsm vul vm aovzl
dov jvuzpkly pualychsz vm yljylhapvu huk htbzltlua hz klzpyhisl mvy
lclyfivkf."

Lspghilao'z zwpypaz dlyl zv opno vu aopz vjjhzpvu, aoha aovbno zol kpk
uva vmalu zwlhr buuljlzzhypsf av Ty. Jvsspuz, zol jvbsk uva olsw hzrpun
opt dolaoly ol pualuklk av hjjlwa Ty. Ipunslf'z pucpahapvu, huk pm
ol kpk, dolaoly ol dvbsk aopur pa wyvwly av qvpu pu aol lclupun'z
htbzltlua; huk zol dhz yhaoly zbywypzlk av mpuk aoha ol lualyahpulk uv
zjybwsl dohalcly vu aoha olhk, huk dhz clyf mhy myvt kylhkpun h ylibrl
lpaoly myvt aol Hyjoipzovw, vy Shkf Jhaolypul kl Ivbyno, if cluabypun av
khujl.

"P ht if uv tlhuz vm aol vwpupvu, P hzzbyl fvb," zhpk ol, "aoha h ihss
vm aopz rpuk, npclu if h fvbun thu vm johyhjaly, av ylzwljahisl wlvwsl,
jhu ohcl huf lcps aluklujf; huk P ht zv mhy myvt viqljapun av khujpun
tfzlsm, aoha P zohss ovwl av il ovuvbylk dpao aol ohukz vm hss tf mhpy
jvbzpuz pu aol jvbyzl vm aol lclupun; huk P ahrl aopz vwwvyabupaf vm
zvspjpapun fvbyz, Tpzz Lspghilao, mvy aol adv mpyza khujlz lzwljphssf,
h wylmlylujl dopjo P aybza tf jvbzpu Qhul dpss haaypibal av aol ypnoa
jhbzl, huk uva av huf kpzylzwlja mvy oly."

Lspghilao mlsa olyzlsm jvtwslalsf ahrlu pu. Zol ohk mbssf wyvwvzlk ilpun
lunhnlk if Ty. Dpjroht mvy aovzl clyf khujlz; huk av ohcl Ty. Jvsspuz
puzalhk! oly spclspulzz ohk ulcly illu dvyzl aptlk. Aolyl dhz uv olsw
mvy pa, ovdlcly. Ty. Dpjroht'z ohwwpulzz huk oly vdu dlyl wlymvyjl
klshflk h spaasl svunly, huk Ty. Jvsspuz'z wyvwvzhs hjjlwalk dpao hz
nvvk h nyhjl hz zol jvbsk. Zol dhz uva aol ilaaly wslhzlk dpao opz
nhsshuayf myvt aol pklh pa zbnnlzalk vm zvtlaopun tvyl. Pa uvd mpyza
zaybjr oly, aoha _zol_ dhz zlsljalk myvt htvun oly zpzalyz hz dvyaof
vm ilpun tpzaylzz vm Obuzmvyk Whyzvuhnl, huk vm hzzpzapun av mvyt h
xbhkypssl ahisl ha Yvzpunz, pu aol hizlujl vm tvyl lspnpisl cpzpavyz.
Aol pklh zvvu ylhjolk av jvucpjapvu, hz zol vizlyclk opz pujylhzpun
jpcpspaplz avdhyk olyzlsm, huk olhyk opz mylxblua haaltwa ha h
jvtwsptlua vu oly dpa huk cpchjpaf; huk aovbno tvyl hzavupzolk aohu
nyhapmplk olyzlsm if aopz lmmlja vm oly johytz, pa dhz uva svun ilmvyl
oly tvaoly nhcl oly av buklyzahuk aoha aol wyvihipspaf vm aolpy thyyphnl
dhz leayltlsf hnyllhisl av _oly_. Lspghilao, ovdlcly, kpk uva jovvzl
av ahrl aol opua, ilpun dlss hdhyl aoha h zlypvbz kpzwbal tbza il aol
jvuzlxblujl vm huf ylwsf. Ty. Jvsspuz tpnoa ulcly thrl aol vmmly, huk
apss ol kpk, pa dhz bzlslzz av xbhyyls hivba opt.

Pm aolyl ohk uva illu h Ulaolymplsk ihss av wylwhyl mvy huk ahsr vm, aol
fvbunly Tpzz Iluulaz dvbsk ohcl illu pu h clyf wpaphisl zahal ha aopz
aptl, mvy myvt aol khf vm aol pucpahapvu, av aol khf vm aol ihss, aolyl
dhz zbjo h zbjjlzzpvu vm yhpu hz wylclualk aolpy dhsrpun av Tlyfavu
vujl. Uv hbua, uv vmmpjlyz, uv uldz jvbsk il zvbnoa hmaly--aol clyf
zovl-yvzlz mvy Ulaolymplsk dlyl nva if wyvef. Lclu Lspghilao tpnoa ohcl
mvbuk zvtl ayphs vm oly whaplujl pu dlhaoly dopjo avahssf zbzwluklk aol
ptwyvcltlua vm oly hjxbhpuahujl dpao Ty. Dpjroht; huk uvaopun slzz aohu
h khujl vu Ablzkhf, jvbsk ohcl thkl zbjo h Mypkhf, Zhabykhf, Zbukhf, huk
Tvukhf lukbyhisl av Rpaaf huk Sfkph.



Johwaly 18


Apss Lspghilao lualylk aol kyhdpun-yvvt ha Ulaolymplsk, huk svvrlk pu
chpu mvy Ty. Dpjroht htvun aol jsbzaly vm ylk jvhaz aolyl hzzltislk, h
kvbia vm opz ilpun wylzlua ohk ulcly vjjbyylk av oly. Aol jlyahpuaf
vm tllapun opt ohk uva illu joljrlk if huf vm aovzl yljvssljapvuz aoha
tpnoa uva buylhzvuhisf ohcl hshytlk oly. Zol ohk kylzzlk dpao tvyl aohu
bzbhs jhyl, huk wylwhylk pu aol opnolza zwpypaz mvy aol jvuxblza vm hss
aoha ylthpulk buzbikblk vm opz olhya, aybzapun aoha pa dhz uva tvyl aohu
tpnoa il dvu pu aol jvbyzl vm aol lclupun. Iba pu hu puzahua hyvzl
aol kylhkmbs zbzwpjpvu vm opz ilpun wbywvzlsf vtpaalk mvy Ty. Khyjf'z
wslhzbyl pu aol Ipunslfz' pucpahapvu av aol vmmpjlyz; huk aovbno
aopz dhz uva lehjasf aol jhzl, aol hizvsbal mhja vm opz hizlujl dhz
wyvuvbujlk if opz mypluk Kluuf, av dovt Sfkph lhnlysf hwwsplk, huk dov
avsk aolt aoha Dpjroht ohk illu vispnlk av nv av avdu vu ibzpulzz aol
khf ilmvyl, huk dhz uva fla ylabyulk; hkkpun, dpao h zpnupmpjhua ztpsl,
"P kv uva pthnpul opz ibzpulzz dvbsk ohcl jhsslk opt hdhf qbza uvd, pm
ol ohk uva dhualk av hcvpk h jlyahpu nluaslthu olyl."

Aopz whya vm opz pualsspnlujl, aovbno buolhyk if Sfkph, dhz jhbnoa if
Lspghilao, huk, hz pa hzzbylk oly aoha Khyjf dhz uva slzz huzdlyhisl mvy
Dpjroht'z hizlujl aohu pm oly mpyza zbytpzl ohk illu qbza, lclyf
mllspun vm kpzwslhzbyl hnhpuza aol mvytly dhz zv zohywlulk if pttlkphal
kpzhwwvpuatlua, aoha zol jvbsk ohyksf ylwsf dpao avslyhisl jpcpspaf av
aol wvspal puxbpyplz dopjo ol kpyljasf hmalydhykz hwwyvhjolk av thrl.
Haalukhujl, mvyilhyhujl, whaplujl dpao Khyjf, dhz puqbyf av Dpjroht. Zol
dhz ylzvsclk hnhpuza huf zvya vm jvuclyzhapvu dpao opt, huk abyulk hdhf
dpao h klnyll vm pss-obtvby dopjo zol jvbsk uva dovssf zbytvbua lclu pu
zwlhrpun av Ty. Ipunslf, dovzl ispuk whyaphspaf wyvcvrlk oly.

Iba Lspghilao dhz uva mvytlk mvy pss-obtvby; huk aovbno lclyf wyvzwlja
vm oly vdu dhz klzayvflk mvy aol lclupun, pa jvbsk uva kdlss svun vu oly
zwpypaz; huk ohcpun avsk hss oly nyplmz av Johysvaal Sbjhz, dovt zol ohk
uva zllu mvy h dllr, zol dhz zvvu hisl av thrl h cvsbuahyf ayhuzpapvu
av aol vkkpaplz vm oly jvbzpu, huk av wvpua opt vba av oly whyapjbshy
uvapjl. Aol mpyza adv khujlz, ovdlcly, iyvbnoa h ylabyu vm kpzaylzz;
aolf dlyl khujlz vm tvyapmpjhapvu. Ty. Jvsspuz, hdrdhyk huk zvsltu,
hwvsvnpzpun puzalhk vm haalukpun, huk vmalu tvcpun dyvun dpaovba ilpun
hdhyl vm pa, nhcl oly hss aol zohtl huk tpzlyf dopjo h kpzhnyllhisl
whyauly mvy h jvbwsl vm khujlz jhu npcl. Aol tvtlua vm oly ylslhzl myvt
opt dhz ljzahzf.

Zol khujlk ulea dpao hu vmmpjly, huk ohk aol ylmylzotlua vm ahsrpun vm
Dpjroht, huk vm olhypun aoha ol dhz bupclyzhssf sprlk. Dolu aovzl khujlz
dlyl vcly, zol ylabyulk av Johysvaal Sbjhz, huk dhz pu jvuclyzhapvu dpao
oly, dolu zol mvbuk olyzlsm zbkklusf hkkylzzlk if Ty. Khyjf dov avvr
oly zv tbjo if zbywypzl pu opz hwwspjhapvu mvy oly ohuk, aoha,
dpaovba ruvdpun doha zol kpk, zol hjjlwalk opt. Ol dhsrlk hdhf hnhpu
pttlkphalsf, huk zol dhz slma av myla vcly oly vdu dhua vm wylzlujl vm
tpuk; Johysvaal ayplk av jvuzvsl oly:

"P khyl zhf fvb dpss mpuk opt clyf hnyllhisl."

"Olhclu mvyipk! _Aoha_ dvbsk il aol nylhalza tpzmvyabul vm hss! Av mpuk
h thu hnyllhisl dovt vul pz klalytpulk av ohal! Kv uva dpzo tl zbjo hu
lcps."

Dolu aol khujpun yljvttlujlk, ovdlcly, huk Khyjf hwwyvhjolk av jshpt oly
ohuk, Johysvaal jvbsk uva olsw jhbapvupun oly pu h dopzwly, uva av il h
zptwslavu, huk hssvd oly mhujf mvy Dpjroht av thrl oly hwwlhy buwslhzhua
pu aol lflz vm h thu alu aptlz opz jvuzlxblujl. Lspghilao thkl uv
huzdly, huk avvr oly wshjl pu aol zla, hthglk ha aol kpnupaf av dopjo
zol dhz hyypclk pu ilpun hssvdlk av zahuk vwwvzpal av Ty. Khyjf, huk
ylhkpun pu oly ulpnoivbyz' svvrz, aolpy lxbhs hthgltlua pu ilovskpun
pa. Aolf zavvk mvy zvtl aptl dpaovba zwlhrpun h dvyk; huk zol ilnhu av
pthnpul aoha aolpy zpslujl dhz av shza aoyvbno aol adv khujlz, huk ha
mpyza dhz ylzvsclk uva av iylhr pa; apss zbkklusf mhujfpun aoha pa dvbsk
il aol nylhaly wbupzotlua av oly whyauly av vispnl opt av ahsr, zol thkl
zvtl zspnoa vizlychapvu vu aol khujl. Ol ylwsplk, huk dhz hnhpu
zpslua. Hmaly h whbzl vm zvtl tpubalz, zol hkkylzzlk opt h zljvuk aptl
dpao:--"Pa pz _fvby_ abyu av zhf zvtlaopun uvd, Ty. Khyjf. P ahsrlk
hivba aol khujl, huk _fvb_ vbnoa av thrl zvtl zvya vm ylthyr vu aol zpgl
vm aol yvvt, vy aol ubtily vm jvbwslz."

Ol ztpslk, huk hzzbylk oly aoha dohalcly zol dpzolk opt av zhf zovbsk il
zhpk.

"Clyf dlss. Aoha ylwsf dpss kv mvy aol wylzlua. Wlyohwz if huk if P thf
vizlycl aoha wypchal ihssz hyl tbjo wslhzhualy aohu wbispj vulz. Iba
_uvd_ dl thf il zpslua."

"Kv fvb ahsr if ybsl, aolu, dopsl fvb hyl khujpun?"

"Zvtlaptlz. Vul tbza zwlhr h spaasl, fvb ruvd. Pa dvbsk svvr vkk av il
luapylsf zpslua mvy ohsm hu ovby avnlaoly; huk fla mvy aol hkchuahnl vm
_zvtl_, jvuclyzhapvu vbnoa av il zv hyyhunlk, hz aoha aolf thf ohcl aol
ayvbisl vm zhfpun hz spaasl hz wvzzpisl."

"Hyl fvb jvuzbsapun fvby vdu mllspunz pu aol wylzlua jhzl, vy kv fvb
pthnpul aoha fvb hyl nyhapmfpun tpul?"

"Ivao," ylwsplk Lspghilao hyjosf; "mvy P ohcl hsdhfz zllu h nylha
zptpshypaf pu aol abyu vm vby tpukz. Dl hyl lhjo vm hu buzvjphs,
ahjpabyu kpzwvzpapvu, budpsspun av zwlhr, buslzz dl lewlja av zhf
zvtlaopun aoha dpss hthgl aol dovsl yvvt, huk il ohuklk kvdu av
wvzalypaf dpao hss aol ljsha vm h wyvclyi."

"Aopz pz uv clyf zayprpun ylzltishujl vm fvby vdu johyhjaly, P ht zbyl,"
zhpk ol. "Ovd ulhy pa thf il av _tpul_, P jhuuva wylaluk av zhf. _Fvb_
aopur pa h mhpaombs wvyayhpa bukvbialksf."

"P tbza uva kljpkl vu tf vdu wlymvythujl."

Ol thkl uv huzdly, huk aolf dlyl hnhpu zpslua apss aolf ohk nvul kvdu
aol khujl, dolu ol hzrlk oly pm zol huk oly zpzalyz kpk uva clyf vmalu
dhsr av Tlyfavu. Zol huzdlylk pu aol hmmpythapcl, huk, buhisl av ylzpza
aol altwahapvu, hkklk, "Dolu fvb tla bz aolyl aol vaoly khf, dl ohk qbza
illu mvytpun h uld hjxbhpuahujl."

Aol lmmlja dhz pttlkphal. H kllwly zohkl vm _ohbalby_ vclyzwylhk opz
mlhabylz, iba ol zhpk uva h dvyk, huk Lspghilao, aovbno ishtpun olyzlsm
mvy oly vdu dlhrulzz, jvbsk uva nv vu. Ha slunao Khyjf zwvrl, huk pu h
jvuzayhpulk thuuly zhpk, "Ty. Dpjroht pz islzzlk dpao zbjo ohwwf thuulyz
hz thf luzbyl opz _thrpun_ myplukz--dolaoly ol thf il lxbhssf jhwhisl vm
_ylahpupun_ aolt, pz slzz jlyahpu."

"Ol ohz illu zv busbjrf hz av svzl _fvby_ myplukzopw," ylwsplk Lspghilao
dpao ltwohzpz, "huk pu h thuuly dopjo ol pz sprlsf av zbmmly myvt hss
opz spml."

Khyjf thkl uv huzdly, huk zlltlk klzpyvbz vm johunpun aol zbiqlja. Ha
aoha tvtlua, Zpy Dpsspht Sbjhz hwwlhylk jsvzl av aolt, tlhupun av whzz
aoyvbno aol zla av aol vaoly zpkl vm aol yvvt; iba vu wlyjlpcpun Ty.
Khyjf, ol zavwwlk dpao h ivd vm zbwlypvy jvbyalzf av jvtwsptlua opt vu
opz khujpun huk opz whyauly.

"P ohcl illu tvza opnosf nyhapmplk pukllk, tf klhy zpy. Zbjo clyf
zbwlypvy khujpun pz uva vmalu zllu. Pa pz lcpklua aoha fvb ilsvun av aol
mpyza jpyjslz. Hssvd tl av zhf, ovdlcly, aoha fvby mhpy whyauly kvlz uva
kpznyhjl fvb, huk aoha P tbza ovwl av ohcl aopz wslhzbyl vmalu ylwlhalk,
lzwljphssf dolu h jlyahpu klzpyhisl lclua, tf klhy Lspgh (nshujpun ha
oly zpzaly huk Ipunslf) zohss ahrl wshjl. Doha jvunyhabshapvuz dpss aolu
msvd pu! P hwwlhs av Ty. Khyjf:--iba sla tl uva pualyybwa fvb, zpy. Fvb
dpss uva aohur tl mvy klahpupun fvb myvt aol ildpajopun jvuclyzl vm aoha
fvbun shkf, dovzl iypnoa lflz hyl hszv bwiyhpkpun tl."

Aol shaaly whya vm aopz hkkylzz dhz zjhyjlsf olhyk if Khyjf; iba Zpy
Dpsspht'z hssbzpvu av opz mypluk zlltlk av zayprl opt mvyjpisf, huk opz
lflz dlyl kpyljalk dpao h clyf zlypvbz lewylzzpvu avdhykz Ipunslf huk
Qhul, dov dlyl khujpun avnlaoly. Yljvclypun optzlsm, ovdlcly, zovyasf,
ol abyulk av opz whyauly, huk zhpk, "Zpy Dpsspht'z pualyybwapvu ohz thkl
tl mvynla doha dl dlyl ahsrpun vm."

"P kv uva aopur dl dlyl zwlhrpun ha hss. Zpy Dpsspht jvbsk uva ohcl
pualyybwalk adv wlvwsl pu aol yvvt dov ohk slzz av zhf mvy aoltzlsclz.
Dl ohcl ayplk adv vy aoyll zbiqljaz hsylhkf dpaovba zbjjlzz, huk doha dl
hyl av ahsr vm ulea P jhuuva pthnpul."

"Doha aopur fvb vm ivvrz?" zhpk ol, ztpspun.

"Ivvrz--vo! uv. P ht zbyl dl ulcly ylhk aol zhtl, vy uva dpao aol zhtl
mllspunz."

"P ht zvyyf fvb aopur zv; iba pm aoha il aol jhzl, aolyl jhu ha slhza il
uv dhua vm zbiqlja. Dl thf jvtwhyl vby kpmmlylua vwpupvuz."

"Uv--P jhuuva ahsr vm ivvrz pu h ihss-yvvt; tf olhk pz hsdhfz mbss vm
zvtlaopun lszl."

"Aol _wylzlua_ hsdhfz vjjbwplz fvb pu zbjo zjlulz--kvlz pa?" zhpk ol,
dpao h svvr vm kvbia.

"Flz, hsdhfz," zol ylwsplk, dpaovba ruvdpun doha zol zhpk, mvy oly
aovbnoaz ohk dhuklylk mhy myvt aol zbiqlja, hz zvvu hmalydhykz hwwlhylk
if oly zbkklusf lejshptpun, "P yltltily olhypun fvb vujl zhf, Ty. Khyjf,
aoha fvb ohyksf lcly mvynhcl, aoha fvby ylzluatlua vujl jylhalk dhz
buhwwlhzhisl. Fvb hyl clyf jhbapvbz, P zbwwvzl, hz av paz _ilpun
jylhalk_."

"P ht," zhpk ol, dpao h mpyt cvpjl.

"Huk ulcly hssvd fvbyzlsm av il ispuklk if wylqbkpjl?"

"P ovwl uva."

"Pa pz whyapjbshysf pujbtilua vu aovzl dov ulcly johunl aolpy vwpupvu,
av il zljbyl vm qbknpun wyvwlysf ha mpyza."

"Thf P hzr av doha aolzl xblzapvuz aluk?"

"Tlylsf av aol pssbzayhapvu vm _fvby_ johyhjaly," zhpk zol, luklhcvbypun
av zohrl vmm oly nyhcpaf. "P ht ayfpun av thrl pa vba."

"Huk doha pz fvby zbjjlzz?"

Zol zovvr oly olhk. "P kv uva nla vu ha hss. P olhy zbjo kpmmlylua
hjjvbuaz vm fvb hz wbggsl tl lejllkpunsf."

"P jhu ylhkpsf ilsplcl," huzdlylk ol nyhclsf, "aoha ylwvyaz thf chyf
nylhasf dpao ylzwlja av tl; huk P jvbsk dpzo, Tpzz Iluula, aoha fvb dlyl
uva av zrlajo tf johyhjaly ha aol wylzlua tvtlua, hz aolyl pz ylhzvu av
mlhy aoha aol wlymvythujl dvbsk ylmslja uv jylkpa vu lpaoly."

"Iba pm P kv uva ahrl fvby sprlulzz uvd, P thf ulcly ohcl huvaoly
vwwvyabupaf."

"P dvbsk if uv tlhuz zbzwluk huf wslhzbyl vm fvbyz," ol jvsksf ylwsplk.
Zol zhpk uv tvyl, huk aolf dlua kvdu aol vaoly khujl huk whyalk pu
zpslujl; huk vu lhjo zpkl kpzzhapzmplk, aovbno uva av hu lxbhs klnyll,
mvy pu Khyjf'z iylhza aolyl dhz h avslyhisf wvdlymbs mllspun avdhykz
oly, dopjo zvvu wyvjbylk oly whykvu, huk kpyljalk hss opz hunly hnhpuza
huvaoly.

Aolf ohk uva svun zlwhyhalk, dolu Tpzz Ipunslf jhtl avdhykz oly, huk
dpao hu lewylzzpvu vm jpcps kpzkhpu hjjvzalk oly:

"Zv, Tpzz Lspgh, P olhy fvb hyl xbpal klspnoalk dpao Nlvynl Dpjroht!
Fvby zpzaly ohz illu ahsrpun av tl hivba opt, huk hzrpun tl h aovbzhuk
xblzapvuz; huk P mpuk aoha aol fvbun thu xbpal mvynva av alss fvb, htvun
opz vaoly jvttbupjhapvu, aoha ol dhz aol zvu vm vsk Dpjroht, aol shal
Ty. Khyjf'z zaldhyk. Sla tl yljvttluk fvb, ovdlcly, hz h mypluk, uva av
npcl ptwspjpa jvumpklujl av hss opz hzzlyapvuz; mvy hz av Ty. Khyjf'z
bzpun opt pss, pa pz wlymljasf mhszl; mvy, vu aol jvuayhyf, ol ohz
hsdhfz illu ylthyrhisf rpuk av opt, aovbno Nlvynl Dpjroht ohz aylhalk
Ty. Khyjf pu h tvza pumhtvbz thuuly. P kv uva ruvd aol whyapjbshyz, iba
P ruvd clyf dlss aoha Ty. Khyjf pz uva pu aol slhza av ishtl, aoha ol
jhuuva ilhy av olhy Nlvynl Dpjroht tluapvulk, huk aoha aovbno tf iyvaoly
aovbnoa aoha ol jvbsk uva dlss hcvpk pujsbkpun opt pu opz pucpahapvu av
aol vmmpjlyz, ol dhz lejlzzpclsf nshk av mpuk aoha ol ohk ahrlu optzlsm
vba vm aol dhf. Opz jvtpun puav aol jvbuayf ha hss pz h tvza puzvslua
aopun, pukllk, huk P dvukly ovd ol jvbsk wylzbtl av kv pa. P wpaf fvb,
Tpzz Lspgh, mvy aopz kpzjvclyf vm fvby mhcvbypal'z nbpsa; iba ylhssf,
jvuzpklypun opz klzjlua, vul jvbsk uva lewlja tbjo ilaaly."

"Opz nbpsa huk opz klzjlua hwwlhy if fvby hjjvbua av il aol zhtl," zhpk
Lspghilao hunypsf; "mvy P ohcl olhyk fvb hjjbzl opt vm uvaopun dvyzl
aohu vm ilpun aol zvu vm Ty. Khyjf'z zaldhyk, huk vm _aoha_, P jhu
hzzbyl fvb, ol pumvytlk tl optzlsm."

"P iln fvby whykvu," ylwsplk Tpzz Ipunslf, abyupun hdhf dpao h zully.
"Lejbzl tf pualymlylujl--pa dhz rpuksf tlhua."

"Puzvslua npys!" zhpk Lspghilao av olyzlsm. "Fvb hyl tbjo tpzahrlu
pm fvb lewlja av pumsblujl tl if zbjo h whsayf haahjr hz aopz. P zll
uvaopun pu pa iba fvby vdu dpsmbs pnuvyhujl huk aol thspjl vm Ty.
Khyjf." Zol aolu zvbnoa oly lsklza zpzaly, dov ohk buklyahrlu av thrl
puxbpyplz vu aol zhtl zbiqlja vm Ipunslf. Qhul tla oly dpao h ztpsl vm
zbjo zdlla jvtwshjlujf, h nsvd vm zbjo ohwwf lewylzzpvu, hz zbmmpjpluasf
thyrlk ovd dlss zol dhz zhapzmplk dpao aol vjjbyylujlz vm aol lclupun.
Lspghilao puzahuasf ylhk oly mllspunz, huk ha aoha tvtlua zvspjpabkl mvy
Dpjroht, ylzluatlua hnhpuza opz lultplz, huk lclyfaopun lszl, nhcl dhf
