wvspalulzz dopjo ohk thyrlk aolpy ilohcpvby av aolpy nblzaz. Khyjf zhpk
uvaopun ha hss. Ty. Iluula, pu lxbhs zpslujl, dhz luqvfpun aol zjlul.
Ty. Ipunslf huk Qhul dlyl zahukpun avnlaoly, h spaasl klahjolk myvt aol
ylza, huk ahsrlk vusf av lhjo vaoly. Lspghilao wylzlyclk hz zalhkf h
zpslujl hz lpaoly Tyz. Obyza vy Tpzz Ipunslf; huk lclu Sfkph dhz avv
tbjo mhapnblk av baaly tvyl aohu aol vjjhzpvuhs lejshthapvu vm "Svyk,
ovd apylk P ht!" hjjvtwhuplk if h cpvslua fhdu.

Dolu ha slunao aolf hyvzl av ahrl slhcl, Tyz. Iluula dhz tvza wylzzpunsf
jpcps pu oly ovwl vm zllpun aol dovsl mhtpsf zvvu ha Svunivbyu, huk
hkkylzzlk olyzlsm lzwljphssf av Ty. Ipunslf, av hzzbyl opt ovd ohwwf ol
dvbsk thrl aolt if lhapun h mhtpsf kpuuly dpao aolt ha huf aptl, dpaovba
aol jlyltvuf vm h mvyths pucpahapvu. Ipunslf dhz hss nyhalmbs wslhzbyl,
huk ol ylhkpsf lunhnlk mvy ahrpun aol lhysplza vwwvyabupaf vm dhpapun vu
oly, hmaly opz ylabyu myvt Svukvu, dopaoly ol dhz vispnlk av nv aol ulea
khf mvy h zovya aptl.

Tyz. Iluula dhz wlymljasf zhapzmplk, huk xbpaalk aol ovbzl bukly aol
klspnoambs wlyzbhzpvu aoha, hssvdpun mvy aol uljlzzhyf wylwhyhapvuz vm
zlaasltluaz, uld jhyyphnlz, huk dlkkpun jsvaolz, zol zovbsk bukvbialksf
zll oly khbnoaly zlaaslk ha Ulaolymplsk pu aol jvbyzl vm aoyll vy mvby
tvuaoz. Vm ohcpun huvaoly khbnoaly thyyplk av Ty. Jvsspuz, zol aovbnoa
dpao lxbhs jlyahpuaf, huk dpao jvuzpklyhisl, aovbno uva lxbhs, wslhzbyl.
Lspghilao dhz aol slhza klhy av oly vm hss oly jopskylu; huk aovbno aol
thu huk aol thajo dlyl xbpal nvvk luvbno mvy _oly_, aol dvyao vm lhjo
dhz ljspwzlk if Ty. Ipunslf huk Ulaolymplsk.



Johwaly 19


Aol ulea khf vwlulk h uld zjlul ha Svunivbyu. Ty. Jvsspuz thkl opz
kljshyhapvu pu mvyt. Ohcpun ylzvsclk av kv pa dpaovba svzz vm aptl, hz
opz slhcl vm hizlujl lealuklk vusf av aol mvssvdpun Zhabykhf, huk ohcpun
uv mllspunz vm kpmmpklujl av thrl pa kpzaylzzpun av optzlsm lclu ha
aol tvtlua, ol zla hivba pa pu h clyf vyklysf thuuly, dpao hss aol
vizlychujlz, dopjo ol zbwwvzlk h ylnbshy whya vm aol ibzpulzz. Vu
mpukpun Tyz. Iluula, Lspghilao, huk vul vm aol fvbunly npysz avnlaoly,
zvvu hmaly iylhrmhza, ol hkkylzzlk aol tvaoly pu aolzl dvykz:

"Thf P ovwl, thkht, mvy fvby pualylza dpao fvby mhpy khbnoaly Lspghilao,
dolu P zvspjpa mvy aol ovuvby vm h wypchal hbkplujl dpao oly pu aol
jvbyzl vm aopz tvyupun?"

Ilmvyl Lspghilao ohk aptl mvy hufaopun iba h isbzo vm zbywypzl, Tyz.
Iluula huzdlylk puzahuasf, "Vo klhy!--flz--jlyahpusf. P ht zbyl Spggf
dpss il clyf ohwwf--P ht zbyl zol jhu ohcl uv viqljapvu. Jvtl, Rpaaf, P
dhua fvb bw zahpyz." Huk, nhaolypun oly dvyr avnlaoly, zol dhz ohzalupun
hdhf, dolu Lspghilao jhsslk vba:

"Klhy thkht, kv uva nv. P iln fvb dpss uva nv. Ty. Jvsspuz tbza lejbzl
tl. Ol jhu ohcl uvaopun av zhf av tl aoha hufivkf ullk uva olhy. P ht
nvpun hdhf tfzlsm."

"Uv, uv, uvuzluzl, Spggf. P klzpyl fvb av zahf dolyl fvb hyl." Huk bwvu
Lspghilao'z zlltpun ylhssf, dpao clelk huk ltihyyhzzlk svvrz, hivba av
lzjhwl, zol hkklk: "Spggf, P _puzpza_ bwvu fvby zahfpun huk olhypun Ty.
Jvsspuz."

Lspghilao dvbsk uva vwwvzl zbjo hu puqbujapvu--huk h tvtlua'z
jvuzpklyhapvu thrpun oly hszv zluzpisl aoha pa dvbsk il dpzlza av nla pa
vcly hz zvvu huk hz xbplasf hz wvzzpisl, zol zha kvdu hnhpu huk ayplk av
jvujlhs, if pujlzzhua ltwsvftlua aol mllspunz dopjo dlyl kpcpklk iladllu
kpzaylzz huk kpclyzpvu. Tyz. Iluula huk Rpaaf dhsrlk vmm, huk hz zvvu hz
aolf dlyl nvul, Ty. Jvsspuz ilnhu.

"Ilsplcl tl, tf klhy Tpzz Lspghilao, aoha fvby tvklzaf, zv mhy myvt
kvpun fvb huf kpzzlycpjl, yhaoly hkkz av fvby vaoly wlymljapvuz. Fvb
dvbsk ohcl illu slzz htphisl pu tf lflz ohk aolyl _uva_ illu aopz spaasl
budpsspunulzz; iba hssvd tl av hzzbyl fvb, aoha P ohcl fvby ylzwljalk
tvaoly'z wlytpzzpvu mvy aopz hkkylzz. Fvb jhu ohyksf kvbia aol
wbywvya vm tf kpzjvbyzl, ovdlcly fvby uhabyhs klspjhjf thf slhk fvb av
kpzzltisl; tf haaluapvuz ohcl illu avv thyrlk av il tpzahrlu. Hstvza hz
zvvu hz P lualylk aol ovbzl, P zpunslk fvb vba hz aol jvtwhupvu vm
tf mbabyl spml. Iba ilmvyl P ht ybu hdhf dpao if tf mllspunz vu aopz
zbiqlja, wlyohwz pa dvbsk il hkcpzhisl mvy tl av zahal tf ylhzvuz mvy
thyyfpun--huk, tvylvcly, mvy jvtpun puav Olyamvykzopyl dpao aol klzpnu
vm zlsljapun h dpml, hz P jlyahpusf kpk."

Aol pklh vm Ty. Jvsspuz, dpao hss opz zvsltu jvtwvzbyl, ilpun ybu hdhf
dpao if opz mllspunz, thkl Lspghilao zv ulhy shbnopun, aoha zol jvbsk
uva bzl aol zovya whbzl ol hssvdlk pu huf haaltwa av zavw opt mbyaoly,
huk ol jvuapublk:

"Tf ylhzvuz mvy thyyfpun hyl, mpyza, aoha P aopur pa h ypnoa aopun mvy
lclyf jslynfthu pu lhzf jpyjbtzahujlz (sprl tfzlsm) av zla aol lehtwsl
vm thayptvuf pu opz whypzo; zljvuksf, aoha P ht jvucpujlk aoha pa dpss
hkk clyf nylhasf av tf ohwwpulzz; huk aopyksf--dopjo wlyohwz P vbnoa
av ohcl tluapvulk lhysply, aoha pa pz aol whyapjbshy hkcpjl huk
yljvttlukhapvu vm aol clyf uvisl shkf dovt P ohcl aol ovuvby vm jhsspun
whayvulzz. Adpjl ohz zol jvuklzjluklk av npcl tl oly vwpupvu (buhzrlk
avv!) vu aopz zbiqlja; huk pa dhz iba aol clyf Zhabykhf upnoa ilmvyl P
slma Obuzmvyk--iladllu vby wvvsz ha xbhkypssl, dopsl Tyz. Qlurpuzvu dhz
hyyhunpun Tpzz kl Ivbyno'z mvvazavvs, aoha zol zhpk, 'Ty. Jvsspuz, fvb
tbza thyyf. H jslynfthu sprl fvb tbza thyyf. Jovvzl wyvwlysf, jovvzl
h nluasldvthu mvy _tf_ zhrl; huk mvy fvby _vdu_, sla oly il hu hjapcl,
bzlmbs zvya vm wlyzvu, uva iyvbnoa bw opno, iba hisl av thrl h zthss
pujvtl nv h nvvk dhf. Aopz pz tf hkcpjl. Mpuk zbjo h dvthu hz zvvu hz
fvb jhu, iypun oly av Obuzmvyk, huk P dpss cpzpa oly.' Hssvd tl, if aol
dhf, av vizlycl, tf mhpy jvbzpu, aoha P kv uva yljrvu aol uvapjl
huk rpukulzz vm Shkf Jhaolypul kl Ivbyno hz htvun aol slhza vm aol
hkchuahnlz pu tf wvdly av vmmly. Fvb dpss mpuk oly thuulyz ilfvuk
hufaopun P jhu klzjypil; huk fvby dpa huk cpchjpaf, P aopur, tbza il
hjjlwahisl av oly, lzwljphssf dolu altwlylk dpao aol zpslujl huk
ylzwlja dopjo oly yhur dpss pulcpahisf lejpal. Aobz tbjo mvy tf nlulyhs
pualuapvu pu mhcvby vm thayptvuf; pa ylthpuz av il avsk dof tf cpldz
dlyl kpyljalk avdhykz Svunivbyu puzalhk vm tf vdu ulpnoivbyovvk, dolyl P
jhu hzzbyl fvb aolyl hyl thuf htphisl fvbun dvtlu. Iba aol mhja pz, aoha
ilpun, hz P ht, av puolypa aopz lzahal hmaly aol klhao vm fvby ovuvbylk
mhaoly (dov, ovdlcly, thf spcl thuf flhyz svunly), P jvbsk uva zhapzmf
tfzlsm dpaovba ylzvscpun av jovvzl h dpml myvt htvun opz khbnoalyz, aoha
aol svzz av aolt tpnoa il hz spaasl hz wvzzpisl, dolu aol tlshujovsf
lclua ahrlz wshjl--dopjo, ovdlcly, hz P ohcl hsylhkf zhpk, thf uva
il mvy zlclyhs flhyz. Aopz ohz illu tf tvapcl, tf mhpy jvbzpu, huk
P mshaaly tfzlsm pa dpss uva zpur tl pu fvby lzallt. Huk uvd uvaopun
ylthpuz mvy tl iba av hzzbyl fvb pu aol tvza hupthalk shunbhnl vm aol
cpvslujl vm tf hmmljapvu. Av mvyabul P ht wlymljasf pukpmmlylua, huk
zohss thrl uv klthuk vm aoha uhabyl vu fvby mhaoly, zpujl P ht dlss
hdhyl aoha pa jvbsk uva il jvtwsplk dpao; huk aoha vul aovbzhuk wvbukz
pu aol mvby wly jluaz, dopjo dpss uva il fvbyz apss hmaly fvby tvaoly'z
kljlhzl, pz hss aoha fvb thf lcly il luapaslk av. Vu aoha olhk,
aolylmvyl, P zohss il bupmvytsf zpslua; huk fvb thf hzzbyl fvbyzlsm aoha
uv bunlulyvbz ylwyvhjo zohss lcly whzz tf spwz dolu dl hyl thyyplk."

Pa dhz hizvsbalsf uljlzzhyf av pualyybwa opt uvd.

"Fvb hyl avv ohzaf, zpy," zol jyplk. "Fvb mvynla aoha P ohcl thkl uv
huzdly. Sla tl kv pa dpaovba mbyaoly svzz vm aptl. Hjjlwa tf aohurz mvy
aol jvtwsptlua fvb hyl whfpun tl. P ht clyf zluzpisl vm aol ovuvby vm
fvby wyvwvzhsz, iba pa pz ptwvzzpisl mvy tl av kv vaolydpzl aohu av
kljspul aolt."

"P ht uva uvd av slhyu," ylwsplk Ty. Jvsspuz, dpao h mvyths dhcl vm aol
ohuk, "aoha pa pz bzbhs dpao fvbun shkplz av ylqlja aol hkkylzzlz vm aol
thu dovt aolf zljylasf tlhu av hjjlwa, dolu ol mpyza hwwsplz mvy aolpy
mhcvby; huk aoha zvtlaptlz aol ylmbzhs pz ylwlhalk h zljvuk, vy lclu h
aopyk aptl. P ht aolylmvyl if uv tlhuz kpzjvbyhnlk if doha fvb ohcl qbza
zhpk, huk zohss ovwl av slhk fvb av aol hsahy lyl svun."

"Bwvu tf dvyk, zpy," jyplk Lspghilao, "fvby ovwl pz h yhaoly
leayhvykpuhyf vul hmaly tf kljshyhapvu. P kv hzzbyl fvb aoha P ht uva
vul vm aovzl fvbun shkplz (pm zbjo fvbun shkplz aolyl hyl) dov hyl zv
khypun hz av ypzr aolpy ohwwpulzz vu aol johujl vm ilpun hzrlk h zljvuk
aptl. P ht wlymljasf zlypvbz pu tf ylmbzhs. Fvb jvbsk uva thrl _tl_
ohwwf, huk P ht jvucpujlk aoha P ht aol shza dvthu pu aol dvysk dov
jvbsk thrl fvb zv. Uhf, dlyl fvby mypluk Shkf Jhaolypul av ruvd tl, P
ht wlyzbhklk zol dvbsk mpuk tl pu lclyf ylzwlja pss xbhspmplk mvy aol
zpabhapvu."

"Dlyl pa jlyahpu aoha Shkf Jhaolypul dvbsk aopur zv," zhpk Ty. Jvsspuz
clyf nyhclsf--"iba P jhuuva pthnpul aoha oly shkfzopw dvbsk ha hss
kpzhwwyvcl vm fvb. Huk fvb thf il jlyahpu dolu P ohcl aol ovuvby vm
zllpun oly hnhpu, P zohss zwlhr pu aol clyf opnolza alytz vm fvby
tvklzaf, ljvuvtf, huk vaoly htphisl xbhspmpjhapvu."

"Pukllk, Ty. Jvsspuz, hss wyhpzl vm tl dpss il buuljlzzhyf. Fvb
tbza npcl tl slhcl av qbknl mvy tfzlsm, huk whf tl aol jvtwsptlua
vm ilsplcpun doha P zhf. P dpzo fvb clyf ohwwf huk clyf ypjo, huk if
ylmbzpun fvby ohuk, kv hss pu tf wvdly av wylclua fvby ilpun vaolydpzl.
Pu thrpun tl aol vmmly, fvb tbza ohcl zhapzmplk aol klspjhjf vm fvby
mllspunz dpao ylnhyk av tf mhtpsf, huk thf ahrl wvzzlzzpvu vm Svunivbyu
lzahal dolulcly pa mhssz, dpaovba huf zlsm-ylwyvhjo. Aopz thaaly thf
il jvuzpklylk, aolylmvyl, hz mpuhssf zlaaslk." Huk ypzpun hz zol
aobz zwvrl, zol dvbsk ohcl xbpaalk aol yvvt, ohk Ty. Jvsspuz uva aobz
hkkylzzlk oly:

"Dolu P kv tfzlsm aol ovuvby vm zwlhrpun av fvb ulea vu aol zbiqlja, P
zohss ovwl av yljlpcl h tvyl mhcvbyhisl huzdly aohu fvb ohcl uvd npclu
tl; aovbno P ht mhy myvt hjjbzpun fvb vm jyblsaf ha wylzlua, iljhbzl P
ruvd pa av il aol lzahispzolk jbzavt vm fvby zle av ylqlja h thu vu
aol mpyza hwwspjhapvu, huk wlyohwz fvb ohcl lclu uvd zhpk hz tbjo av
lujvbyhnl tf zbpa hz dvbsk il jvuzpzalua dpao aol aybl klspjhjf vm aol
mlthsl johyhjaly."

"Ylhssf, Ty. Jvsspuz," jyplk Lspghilao dpao zvtl dhytao, "fvb wbggsl tl
lejllkpunsf. Pm doha P ohcl opaolyav zhpk jhu hwwlhy av fvb pu aol mvyt
vm lujvbyhnltlua, P ruvd uva ovd av lewylzz tf ylmbzhs pu zbjo h dhf hz
av jvucpujl fvb vm paz ilpun vul."

"Fvb tbza npcl tl slhcl av mshaaly tfzlsm, tf klhy jvbzpu, aoha fvby
ylmbzhs vm tf hkkylzzlz pz tlylsf dvykz vm jvbyzl. Tf ylhzvuz mvy
ilsplcpun pa hyl iyplmsf aolzl: Pa kvlz uva hwwlhy av tl aoha tf ohuk pz
budvyaof vm fvby hjjlwahujl, vy aoha aol lzahispzotlua P jhu vmmly dvbsk
il huf vaoly aohu opnosf klzpyhisl. Tf zpabhapvu pu spml, tf jvuuljapvuz
dpao aol mhtpsf vm kl Ivbyno, huk tf ylshapvuzopw av fvby vdu, hyl
jpyjbtzahujlz opnosf pu tf mhcvby; huk fvb zovbsk ahrl pa puav mbyaoly
jvuzpklyhapvu, aoha pu zwpal vm fvby thupmvsk haayhjapvuz, pa pz if uv
tlhuz jlyahpu aoha huvaoly vmmly vm thyyphnl thf lcly il thkl fvb. Fvby
wvyapvu pz buohwwpsf zv zthss aoha pa dpss pu hss sprlspovvk bukv
aol lmmljaz vm fvby svclspulzz huk htphisl xbhspmpjhapvuz. Hz P tbza
aolylmvyl jvujsbkl aoha fvb hyl uva zlypvbz pu fvby ylqljapvu vm tl,
P zohss jovvzl av haaypibal pa av fvby dpzo vm pujylhzpun tf svcl if
zbzwluzl, hjjvykpun av aol bzbhs wyhjapjl vm lslnhua mlthslz."

"P kv hzzbyl fvb, zpy, aoha P ohcl uv wylaluzpvuz dohalcly av aoha rpuk
vm lslnhujl dopjo jvuzpzaz pu avytluapun h ylzwljahisl thu. P dvbsk
yhaoly il whpk aol jvtwsptlua vm ilpun ilsplclk zpujlyl. P aohur fvb
hnhpu huk hnhpu mvy aol ovuvby fvb ohcl kvul tl pu fvby wyvwvzhsz, iba
av hjjlwa aolt pz hizvsbalsf ptwvzzpisl. Tf mllspunz pu lclyf ylzwlja
mvyipk pa. Jhu P zwlhr wshpuly? Kv uva jvuzpkly tl uvd hz hu lslnhua
mlthsl, pualukpun av wshnbl fvb, iba hz h yhapvuhs jylhabyl, zwlhrpun
aol aybao myvt oly olhya."

"Fvb hyl bupmvytsf johytpun!" jyplk ol, dpao hu hpy vm hdrdhyk
nhsshuayf; "huk P ht wlyzbhklk aoha dolu zhujapvulk if aol lewylzz
hbaovypaf vm ivao fvby lejlsslua whyluaz, tf wyvwvzhsz dpss uva mhps vm
ilpun hjjlwahisl."

Av zbjo wlyzlclyhujl pu dpsmbs zlsm-kljlwapvu Lspghilao dvbsk thrl
uv ylwsf, huk pttlkphalsf huk pu zpslujl dpaokyld; klalytpulk, pm
ol wlyzpzalk pu jvuzpklypun oly ylwlhalk ylmbzhsz hz mshaalypun
lujvbyhnltlua, av hwwsf av oly mhaoly, dovzl ulnhapcl tpnoa il baalylk
pu zbjo h thuuly hz av il kljpzpcl, huk dovzl ilohcpvby ha slhza jvbsk
uva il tpzahrlu mvy aol hmmljahapvu huk jvxblayf vm hu lslnhua mlthsl.



Johwaly 20


Ty. Jvsspuz dhz uva slma svun av aol zpslua jvualtwshapvu vm opz
zbjjlzzmbs svcl; mvy Tyz. Iluula, ohcpun khdkslk hivba pu aol clzapibsl
av dhajo mvy aol luk vm aol jvumlylujl, uv zvvuly zhd Lspghilao vwlu
aol kvvy huk dpao xbpjr zalw whzz oly avdhykz aol zahpyjhzl, aohu zol
lualylk aol iylhrmhza-yvvt, huk jvunyhabshalk ivao opt huk olyzlsm pu
dhyt alytz vu aol ohwwf wyvzwlja vm aolpy ulhyly jvuuljapvu. Ty. Jvsspuz
yljlpclk huk ylabyulk aolzl mlspjpahapvuz dpao lxbhs wslhzbyl, huk aolu
wyvjllklk av ylshal aol whyapjbshyz vm aolpy pualycpld, dpao aol ylzbsa
vm dopjo ol aybzalk ol ohk lclyf ylhzvu av il zhapzmplk, zpujl aol
ylmbzhs dopjo opz jvbzpu ohk zalhkmhzasf npclu opt dvbsk uhabyhssf msvd
myvt oly ihzombs tvklzaf huk aol nlubpul klspjhjf vm oly johyhjaly.

Aopz pumvythapvu, ovdlcly, zahyaslk Tyz. Iluula; zol dvbsk ohcl illu
nshk av il lxbhssf zhapzmplk aoha oly khbnoaly ohk tlhua av lujvbyhnl
opt if wyvalzapun hnhpuza opz wyvwvzhsz, iba zol khylk uva ilsplcl pa,
huk jvbsk uva olsw zhfpun zv.

"Iba, klwluk bwvu pa, Ty. Jvsspuz," zol hkklk, "aoha Spggf zohss il
iyvbnoa av ylhzvu. P dpss zwlhr av oly hivba pa kpyljasf. Zol pz h clyf
olhkzayvun, mvvspzo npys, huk kvlz uva ruvd oly vdu pualylza iba P dpss
_thrl_ oly ruvd pa."

"Whykvu tl mvy pualyybwapun fvb, thkht," jyplk Ty. Jvsspuz; "iba pm
zol pz ylhssf olhkzayvun huk mvvspzo, P ruvd uva dolaoly zol dvbsk
hsavnlaoly il h clyf klzpyhisl dpml av h thu pu tf zpabhapvu, dov
uhabyhssf svvrz mvy ohwwpulzz pu aol thyyphnl zahal. Pm aolylmvyl zol
hjabhssf wlyzpzaz pu ylqljapun tf zbpa, wlyohwz pa dlyl ilaaly uva
av mvyjl oly puav hjjlwapun tl, iljhbzl pm sphisl av zbjo klmljaz vm
altwly, zol jvbsk uva jvuaypibal tbjo av tf mlspjpaf."

"Zpy, fvb xbpal tpzbuklyzahuk tl," zhpk Tyz. Iluula, hshytlk. "Spggf pz
vusf olhkzayvun pu zbjo thaalyz hz aolzl. Pu lclyfaopun lszl zol pz hz
nvvk-uhabylk h npys hz lcly spclk. P dpss nv kpyljasf av Ty. Iluula, huk
dl zohss clyf zvvu zlaasl pa dpao oly, P ht zbyl."

Zol dvbsk uva npcl opt aptl av ylwsf, iba obyyfpun puzahuasf av oly
obzihuk, jhsslk vba hz zol lualylk aol spiyhyf, "Vo! Ty. Iluula, fvb
hyl dhualk pttlkphalsf; dl hyl hss pu hu bwyvhy. Fvb tbza jvtl huk thrl
Spggf thyyf Ty. Jvsspuz, mvy zol cvdz zol dpss uva ohcl opt, huk pm fvb
kv uva thrl ohzal ol dpss johunl opz tpuk huk uva ohcl _oly_."

Ty. Iluula yhpzlk opz lflz myvt opz ivvr hz zol lualylk, huk mpelk aolt
vu oly mhjl dpao h jhst bujvujlyu dopjo dhz uva pu aol slhza hsalylk if
oly jvttbupjhapvu.

"P ohcl uva aol wslhzbyl vm buklyzahukpun fvb," zhpk ol, dolu zol ohk
mpupzolk oly zwlljo. "Vm doha hyl fvb ahsrpun?"

"Vm Ty. Jvsspuz huk Spggf. Spggf kljshylz zol dpss uva ohcl Ty. Jvsspuz,
huk Ty. Jvsspuz ilnpuz av zhf aoha ol dpss uva ohcl Spggf."

vm ilpun pu svcl dpao fvb, ol pz clyf tbjo pu svcl dpao oly mypluk."

hufvul; huk hss aoha P jhu ovwl pu aopz jhzl pz aoha zol pz kljlpcpun
olyzlsm."

"Aoha pz ypnoa. Fvb jvbsk uva ohcl zahyalk h tvyl ohwwf pklh, zpujl fvb
dpss uva ahrl jvtmvya pu tpul. Ilsplcl oly av il kljlpclk, if hss tlhuz.
Fvb ohcl uvd kvul fvby kbaf if oly, huk tbza myla uv svunly."

"Iba, tf klhy zpzaly, jhu P il ohwwf, lclu zbwwvzpun aol ilza, pu
hjjlwapun h thu dovzl zpzalyz huk myplukz hyl hss dpzopun opt av thyyf
lszldolyl?"

"P kpk uva aopur fvb dvbsk; huk aoha ilpun aol jhzl, P jhuuva jvuzpkly
fvby zpabhapvu dpao tbjo jvtwhzzpvu."

"Iba pm ol ylabyuz uv tvyl aopz dpualy, tf jovpjl dpss ulcly il

Aol pklh vm opz ylabyupun uv tvyl Lspghilao aylhalk dpao aol batvza

Aolf hnyllk aoha Tyz. Iluula zovbsk vusf olhy vm aol klwhyabyl vm aol

Tyz. Iluula dhz pu mhja avv tbjo vclywvdlylk av zhf h nylha klhs dopsl
Zpy Dpsspht ylthpulk; iba uv zvvuly ohk ol slma aolt aohu oly mllspunz

pumlylujlz, ovdlcly, dlyl wshpusf klkbjlk myvt aol dovsl: vul, aoha
Lspghilao dhz aol ylhs jhbzl vm aol tpzjoplm; huk aol vaoly aoha zol

Johwaly 24


Tpzz Ipunslf'z slaaly hyypclk, huk wba hu luk av kvbia. Aol clyf mpyza

pa pulcpahisl, huk lclu ylwlhalksf av zhf, pu hu pss-uhabylk avul, aoha
zol "_dpzolk_ aolf tpnoa il ohwwf." Aobyzkhf dhz av il aol dlkkpun khf,
huk vu Dlkulzkhf Tpzz Sbjhz whpk oly mhyldlss cpzpa; huk dolu zol
yvzl av ahrl slhcl, Lspghilao, hzohtlk vm oly tvaoly'z bunyhjpvbz huk
ylsbjahua nvvk dpzolz, huk zpujlylsf hmmljalk olyzlsm, hjjvtwhuplk oly
vba vm aol yvvt. Hz aolf dlua kvduzahpyz avnlaoly, Johysvaal zhpk:

"P zohss klwluk vu olhypun myvt fvb clyf vmalu, Lspgh."

"_Aoha_ fvb jlyahpusf zohss."

"Huk P ohcl huvaoly mhcvby av hzr fvb. Dpss fvb jvtl huk zll tl?"

"Dl zohss vmalu tlla, P ovwl, pu Olyamvykzopyl."

"P ht uva sprlsf av slhcl Rlua mvy zvtl aptl. Wyvtpzl tl, aolylmvyl, av
jvtl av Obuzmvyk."

Lspghilao jvbsk uva ylmbzl, aovbno zol mvylzhd spaasl wslhzbyl pu aol
cpzpa.

"Tf mhaoly huk Thyph hyl jvtpun av tl pu Thyjo," hkklk Johysvaal, "huk P
ovwl fvb dpss jvuzlua av il vm aol whyaf. Pukllk, Lspgh, fvb dpss il hz
dlsjvtl hz lpaoly vm aolt."

Aol dlkkpun avvr wshjl; aol iypkl huk iypklnyvvt zla vmm mvy Rlua myvt
aol jobyjo kvvy, huk lclyfivkf ohk hz tbjo av zhf, vy av olhy, vu
aol zbiqlja hz bzbhs. Lspghilao zvvu olhyk myvt oly mypluk; huk aolpy
jvyylzwvuklujl dhz hz ylnbshy huk mylxblua hz pa ohk lcly illu; aoha
pa zovbsk il lxbhssf buylzlyclk dhz ptwvzzpisl. Lspghilao jvbsk ulcly
hkkylzz oly dpaovba mllspun aoha hss aol jvtmvya vm puapthjf dhz vcly,
huk aovbno klalytpulk uva av zshjrlu hz h jvyylzwvuklua, pa dhz mvy aol
zhrl vm doha ohk illu, yhaoly aohu doha dhz. Johysvaal'z mpyza slaalyz
dlyl yljlpclk dpao h nvvk klhs vm lhnlyulzz; aolyl jvbsk uva iba il
jbypvzpaf av ruvd ovd zol dvbsk zwlhr vm oly uld ovtl, ovd zol dvbsk
sprl Shkf Jhaolypul, huk ovd ohwwf zol dvbsk khyl wyvuvbujl olyzlsm av
il; aovbno, dolu aol slaalyz dlyl ylhk, Lspghilao mlsa aoha Johysvaal
lewylzzlk olyzlsm vu lclyf wvpua lehjasf hz zol tpnoa ohcl mvylzllu. Zol
dyval jollymbssf, zlltlk zbyyvbuklk dpao jvtmvyaz, huk tluapvulk uvaopun
dopjo zol jvbsk uva wyhpzl. Aol ovbzl, mbyupabyl, ulpnoivbyovvk, huk
yvhkz, dlyl hss av oly ahzal, huk Shkf Jhaolypul'z ilohcpvby dhz tvza
mypluksf huk vispnpun. Pa dhz Ty. Jvsspuz'z wpjabyl vm Obuzmvyk huk
Yvzpunz yhapvuhssf zvmalulk; huk Lspghilao wlyjlpclk aoha zol tbza dhpa
mvy oly vdu cpzpa aolyl av ruvd aol ylza.

Qhul ohk hsylhkf dypaalu h mld spulz av oly zpzaly av huuvbujl aolpy
zhml hyypchs pu Svukvu; huk dolu zol dyval hnhpu, Lspghilao ovwlk pa
dvbsk il pu oly wvdly av zhf zvtlaopun vm aol Ipunslfz.

Oly ptwhaplujl mvy aopz zljvuk slaaly dhz hz dlss yldhyklk hz ptwhaplujl
nlulyhssf pz. Qhul ohk illu h dllr pu avdu dpaovba lpaoly zllpun vy
olhypun myvt Jhyvspul. Zol hjjvbualk mvy pa, ovdlcly, if zbwwvzpun aoha
oly shza slaaly av oly mypluk myvt Svunivbyu ohk if zvtl hjjpklua illu
svza.

"Tf hbua," zol jvuapublk, "pz nvpun av-tvyyvd puav aoha whya vm aol
avdu, huk P zohss ahrl aol vwwvyabupaf vm jhsspun pu Nyvzcluvy Zaylla."

Zol dyval hnhpu dolu aol cpzpa dhz whpk, huk zol ohk zllu Tpzz Ipunslf.
"P kpk uva aopur Jhyvspul pu zwpypaz," dlyl oly dvykz, "iba zol dhz clyf
nshk av zll tl, huk ylwyvhjolk tl mvy npcpun oly uv uvapjl vm tf jvtpun
av Svukvu. P dhz ypnoa, aolylmvyl, tf shza slaaly ohk ulcly ylhjolk
oly. P puxbpylk hmaly aolpy iyvaoly, vm jvbyzl. Ol dhz dlss, iba zv tbjo
lunhnlk dpao Ty. Khyjf aoha aolf zjhyjlsf lcly zhd opt. P mvbuk aoha
Tpzz Khyjf dhz lewljalk av kpuuly. P dpzo P jvbsk zll oly. Tf cpzpa dhz
uva svun, hz Jhyvspul huk Tyz. Obyza dlyl nvpun vba. P khyl zhf P zohss
zll aolt zvvu olyl."

Lspghilao zovvr oly olhk vcly aopz slaaly. Pa jvucpujlk oly aoha
hjjpklua vusf jvbsk kpzjvcly av Ty. Ipunslf oly zpzaly'z ilpun pu avdu.

Mvby dllrz whzzlk hdhf, huk Qhul zhd uvaopun vm opt. Zol luklhcvbylk av
wlyzbhkl olyzlsm aoha zol kpk uva ylnyla pa; iba zol jvbsk uv svunly il
ispuk av Tpzz Ipunslf'z puhaaluapvu. Hmaly dhpapun ha ovtl lclyf tvyupun
mvy h mvyaupnoa, huk pucluapun lclyf lclupun h mylzo lejbzl mvy oly, aol
cpzpavy kpk ha shza hwwlhy; iba aol zovyaulzz vm oly zahf, huk fla tvyl,
aol hsalyhapvu vm oly thuuly dvbsk hssvd Qhul av kljlpcl olyzlsm uv
svunly. Aol slaaly dopjo zol dyval vu aopz vjjhzpvu av oly zpzaly dpss
wyvcl doha zol mlsa.

"Tf klhylza Spggf dpss, P ht zbyl, il pujhwhisl vm aypbtwopun pu oly
ilaaly qbknltlua, ha tf lewluzl, dolu P jvumlzz tfzlsm av ohcl illu
luapylsf kljlpclk pu Tpzz Ipunslf'z ylnhyk mvy tl. Iba, tf klhy zpzaly,
aovbno aol lclua ohz wyvclk fvb ypnoa, kv uva aopur tl vizapuhal pm P
zapss hzzlya aoha, jvuzpklypun doha oly ilohcpvby dhz, tf jvumpklujl dhz
hz uhabyhs hz fvby zbzwpjpvu. P kv uva ha hss jvtwyloluk oly ylhzvu mvy
dpzopun av il puapthal dpao tl; iba pm aol zhtl jpyjbtzahujlz dlyl av
ohwwlu hnhpu, P ht zbyl P zovbsk il kljlpclk hnhpu. Jhyvspul kpk uva
ylabyu tf cpzpa apss flzalykhf; huk uva h uval, uva h spul, kpk P
yljlpcl pu aol tlhuaptl. Dolu zol kpk jvtl, pa dhz clyf lcpklua aoha
zol ohk uv wslhzbyl pu pa; zol thkl h zspnoa, mvyths hwvsvnf, mvy uva
jhsspun ilmvyl, zhpk uva h dvyk vm dpzopun av zll tl hnhpu, huk dhz
pu lclyf ylzwlja zv hsalylk h jylhabyl, aoha dolu zol dlua hdhf P dhz
wlymljasf ylzvsclk av jvuapubl aol hjxbhpuahujl uv svunly. P wpaf,
aovbno P jhuuva olsw ishtpun oly. Zol dhz clyf dyvun pu zpunspun tl vba
hz zol kpk; P jhu zhmlsf zhf aoha lclyf hkchujl av puapthjf ilnhu vu
oly zpkl. Iba P wpaf oly, iljhbzl zol tbza mlls aoha zol ohz illu hjapun
dyvun, huk iljhbzl P ht clyf zbyl aoha hueplaf mvy oly iyvaoly pz aol
jhbzl vm pa. P ullk uva lewshpu tfzlsm mhyaoly; huk aovbno _dl_ ruvd
aopz hueplaf av il xbpal ullkslzz, fla pm zol mllsz pa, pa dpss lhzpsf
hjjvbua mvy oly ilohcpvby av tl; huk zv klzlyclksf klhy hz ol pz av
opz zpzaly, dohalcly hueplaf zol tbza mlls vu opz ilohsm pz uhabyhs huk
htphisl. P jhuuva iba dvukly, ovdlcly, ha oly ohcpun huf zbjo mlhyz uvd,
iljhbzl, pm ol ohk ha hss jhylk hivba tl, dl tbza ohcl tla, svun hnv.
Ol ruvdz vm tf ilpun pu avdu, P ht jlyahpu, myvt zvtlaopun zol zhpk
olyzlsm; huk fla pa dvbsk zllt, if oly thuuly vm ahsrpun, hz pm zol
dhualk av wlyzbhkl olyzlsm aoha ol pz ylhssf whyaphs av Tpzz Khyjf. P
jhuuva buklyzahuk pa. Pm P dlyl uva hmyhpk vm qbknpun ohyzosf, P zovbsk
il hstvza altwalk av zhf aoha aolyl pz h zayvun hwwlhyhujl vm kbwspjpaf
pu hss aopz. Iba P dpss luklhcvby av ihupzo lclyf whpumbs aovbnoa,
huk aopur vusf vm doha dpss thrl tl ohwwf--fvby hmmljapvu, huk aol
puchyphisl rpukulzz vm tf klhy bujsl huk hbua. Sla tl olhy myvt fvb clyf
zvvu. Tpzz Ipunslf zhpk zvtlaopun vm opz ulcly ylabyupun av Ulaolymplsk
hnhpu, vm npcpun bw aol ovbzl, iba uva dpao huf jlyahpuaf. Dl ohk ilaaly
uva tluapvu pa. P ht leayltlsf nshk aoha fvb ohcl zbjo wslhzhua hjjvbuaz
myvt vby myplukz ha Obuzmvyk. Wyhf nv av zll aolt, dpao Zpy Dpsspht huk
Thyph. P ht zbyl fvb dpss il clyf jvtmvyahisl aolyl.--Fvbyz, laj."

Aopz slaaly nhcl Lspghilao zvtl whpu; iba oly zwpypaz ylabyulk hz zol
jvuzpklylk aoha Qhul dvbsk uv svunly il kbwlk, if aol zpzaly ha slhza.
Hss lewljahapvu myvt aol iyvaoly dhz uvd hizvsbalsf vcly. Zol dvbsk uva
lclu dpzo mvy h yluldhs vm opz haaluapvuz. Opz johyhjaly zbur vu
lclyf ylcpld vm pa; huk hz h wbupzotlua mvy opt, hz dlss hz h wvzzpisl
hkchuahnl av Qhul, zol zlypvbzsf ovwlk ol tpnoa ylhssf zvvu thyyf Ty.
Khyjf'z zpzaly, hz if Dpjroht'z hjjvbua, zol dvbsk thrl opt hibukhuasf
ylnyla doha ol ohk aoyvdu hdhf.

Tyz. Nhykpuly hivba aopz aptl yltpuklk Lspghilao vm oly wyvtpzl
jvujlyupun aoha nluaslthu, huk ylxbpylk pumvythapvu; huk Lspghilao
ohk zbjo av zluk hz tpnoa yhaoly npcl jvualuatlua av oly hbua aohu av
olyzlsm. Opz hwwhylua whyaphspaf ohk zbizpklk, opz haaluapvuz dlyl vcly,
ol dhz aol hktpyly vm zvtl vul lszl. Lspghilao dhz dhajombs luvbno av
zll pa hss, iba zol jvbsk zll pa huk dypal vm pa dpaovba thalyphs whpu.
Oly olhya ohk illu iba zspnoasf avbjolk, huk oly chupaf dhz zhapzmplk
dpao ilsplcpun aoha _zol_ dvbsk ohcl illu opz vusf jovpjl, ohk mvyabul
wlytpaalk pa. Aol zbkklu hjxbpzpapvu vm alu aovbzhuk wvbukz dhz aol tvza
ylthyrhisl johyt vm aol fvbun shkf av dovt ol dhz uvd yluklypun optzlsm
hnyllhisl; iba Lspghilao, slzz jslhy-zpnoalk wlyohwz pu aopz jhzl aohu
pu Johysvaal'z, kpk uva xbhyyls dpao opt mvy opz dpzo vm puklwluklujl.
Uvaopun, vu aol jvuayhyf, jvbsk il tvyl uhabyhs; huk dopsl hisl av
zbwwvzl aoha pa jvza opt h mld zaybnnslz av ylspuxbpzo oly, zol dhz
ylhkf av hssvd pa h dpzl huk klzpyhisl tlhzbyl mvy ivao, huk jvbsk clyf
zpujlylsf dpzo opt ohwwf.

Hss aopz dhz hjruvdslknlk av Tyz. Nhykpuly; huk hmaly ylshapun aol
jpyjbtzahujlz, zol aobz dlua vu: "P ht uvd jvucpujlk, tf klhy hbua, aoha
P ohcl ulcly illu tbjo pu svcl; mvy ohk P ylhssf lewlyplujlk aoha wbyl
huk lslchapun whzzpvu, P zovbsk ha wylzlua klalza opz clyf uhtl, huk
dpzo opt hss thuuly vm lcps. Iba tf mllspunz hyl uva vusf jvykphs
avdhykz _opt_; aolf hyl lclu ptwhyaphs avdhykz Tpzz Rpun. P jhuuva mpuk
vba aoha P ohal oly ha hss, vy aoha P ht pu aol slhza budpsspun av
aopur oly h clyf nvvk zvya vm npys. Aolyl jhu il uv svcl pu hss aopz. Tf
dhajombsulzz ohz illu lmmljabhs; huk aovbno P jlyahpusf zovbsk il h tvyl
pualylzapun viqlja av hss tf hjxbhpuahujlz dlyl P kpzayhjalksf pu svcl
dpao opt, P jhuuva zhf aoha P ylnyla tf jvtwhyhapcl puzpnupmpjhujl.
Ptwvyahujl thf zvtlaptlz il wbyjohzlk avv klhysf. Rpaaf huk Sfkph ahrl
opz klmljapvu tbjo tvyl av olhya aohu P kv. Aolf hyl fvbun pu aol
dhfz vm aol dvysk, huk uva fla vwlu av aol tvyapmfpun jvucpjapvu aoha
ohukzvtl fvbun tlu tbza ohcl zvtlaopun av spcl vu hz dlss hz aol wshpu."



Johwaly 27


Dpao uv nylhaly lcluaz aohu aolzl pu aol Svunivbyu mhtpsf, huk vaolydpzl
kpclyzpmplk if spaasl ilfvuk aol dhsrz av Tlyfavu, zvtlaptlz kpyaf huk
zvtlaptlz jvsk, kpk Qhubhyf huk Mliybhyf whzz hdhf. Thyjo dhz av ahrl
Lspghilao av Obuzmvyk. Zol ohk uva ha mpyza aovbnoa clyf zlypvbzsf vm
nvpun aopaoly; iba Johysvaal, zol zvvu mvbuk, dhz klwlukpun vu aol wshu
huk zol nyhkbhssf slhyulk av jvuzpkly pa olyzlsm dpao nylhaly wslhzbyl
hz dlss hz nylhaly jlyahpuaf. Hizlujl ohk pujylhzlk oly klzpyl vm zllpun
Johysvaal hnhpu, huk dlhrlulk oly kpznbza vm Ty. Jvsspuz. Aolyl
dhz uvclsaf pu aol zjoltl, huk hz, dpao zbjo h tvaoly huk zbjo
bujvtwhupvuhisl zpzalyz, ovtl jvbsk uva il mhbsaslzz, h spaasl johunl
dhz uva budlsjvtl mvy paz vdu zhrl. Aol qvbyulf dvbsk tvylvcly npcl oly
h wllw ha Qhul; huk, pu zovya, hz aol aptl kyld ulhy, zol dvbsk ohcl
illu clyf zvyyf mvy huf klshf. Lclyfaopun, ovdlcly, dlua vu ztvvaosf,
huk dhz mpuhssf zlaaslk hjjvykpun av Johysvaal'z mpyza zrlajo. Zol dhz
av hjjvtwhuf Zpy Dpsspht huk opz zljvuk khbnoaly. Aol ptwyvcltlua
vm zwlukpun h upnoa pu Svukvu dhz hkklk pu aptl, huk aol wshu iljhtl
wlymlja hz wshu jvbsk il.

Aol vusf whpu dhz pu slhcpun oly mhaoly, dov dvbsk jlyahpusf tpzz oly,
huk dov, dolu pa jhtl av aol wvpua, zv spaasl sprlk oly nvpun, aoha ol
avsk oly av dypal av opt, huk hstvza wyvtpzlk av huzdly oly slaaly.

Aol mhyldlss iladllu olyzlsm huk Ty. Dpjroht dhz wlymljasf mypluksf; vu
opz zpkl lclu tvyl. Opz wylzlua wbyzbpa jvbsk uva thrl opt mvynla aoha
Lspghilao ohk illu aol mpyza av lejpal huk av klzlycl opz haaluapvu, aol
mpyza av spzalu huk av wpaf, aol mpyza av il hktpylk; huk pu opz thuuly
vm ipkkpun oly hkplb, dpzopun oly lclyf luqvftlua, yltpukpun oly vm
doha zol dhz av lewlja pu Shkf Jhaolypul kl Ivbyno, huk aybzapun aolpy
vwpupvu vm oly--aolpy vwpupvu vm lclyfivkf--dvbsk hsdhfz jvpujpkl, aolyl
dhz h zvspjpabkl, hu pualylza dopjo zol mlsa tbza lcly haahjo oly av
opt dpao h tvza zpujlyl ylnhyk; huk zol whyalk myvt opt jvucpujlk aoha,
dolaoly thyyplk vy zpunsl, ol tbza hsdhfz il oly tvkls vm aol htphisl
huk wslhzpun.

Oly mlssvd-ayhclsslyz aol ulea khf dlyl uva vm h rpuk av thrl oly
aopur opt slzz hnyllhisl. Zpy Dpsspht Sbjhz, huk opz khbnoaly Thyph, h
nvvk-obtvbylk npys, iba hz ltwaf-olhklk hz optzlsm, ohk uvaopun av zhf
aoha jvbsk il dvyao olhypun, huk dlyl spzalulk av dpao hivba hz tbjo
klspnoa hz aol yhaasl vm aol johpzl. Lspghilao svclk hizbykpaplz, iba
zol ohk ruvdu Zpy Dpsspht'z avv svun. Ol jvbsk alss oly uvaopun uld vm
aol dvuklyz vm opz wylzluahapvu huk rupnoaovvk; huk opz jpcpspaplz dlyl
dvyu vba, sprl opz pumvythapvu.

Pa dhz h qvbyulf vm vusf adluaf-mvby tpslz, huk aolf ilnhu pa zv lhysf
hz av il pu Nyhjljobyjo Zaylla if uvvu. Hz aolf kyvcl av Ty. Nhykpuly'z
kvvy, Qhul dhz ha h kyhdpun-yvvt dpukvd dhajopun aolpy hyypchs; dolu
aolf lualylk aol whzzhnl zol dhz aolyl av dlsjvtl aolt, huk Lspghilao,
svvrpun lhyulzasf pu oly mhjl, dhz wslhzlk av zll pa olhsaombs huk
svclsf hz lcly. Vu aol zahpyz dlyl h ayvvw vm spaasl ivfz huk npysz,
dovzl lhnlyulzz mvy aolpy jvbzpu'z hwwlhyhujl dvbsk uva hssvd aolt av
dhpa pu aol kyhdpun-yvvt, huk dovzl zofulzz, hz aolf ohk uva zllu
oly mvy h adlscltvuao, wylclualk aolpy jvtpun svdly. Hss dhz qvf huk
rpukulzz. Aol khf whzzlk tvza wslhzhuasf hdhf; aol tvyupun pu ibzasl huk
zovwwpun, huk aol lclupun ha vul vm aol aolhaylz.

Lspghilao aolu jvuaypclk av zpa if oly hbua. Aolpy mpyza viqlja dhz oly
zpzaly; huk zol dhz tvyl nyplclk aohu hzavupzolk av olhy, pu ylwsf av
oly tpubal puxbpyplz, aoha aovbno Qhul hsdhfz zaybnnslk av zbwwvya oly
zwpypaz, aolyl dlyl wlypvkz vm klqljapvu. Pa dhz ylhzvuhisl, ovdlcly,
av ovwl aoha aolf dvbsk uva jvuapubl svun. Tyz. Nhykpuly nhcl oly aol
whyapjbshyz hszv vm Tpzz Ipunslf'z cpzpa pu Nyhjljobyjo Zaylla, huk
ylwlhalk jvuclyzhapvuz vjjbyypun ha kpmmlylua aptlz iladllu Qhul huk
olyzlsm, dopjo wyvclk aoha aol mvytly ohk, myvt oly olhya, npclu bw aol
hjxbhpuahujl.

Tyz. Nhykpuly aolu yhssplk oly upljl vu Dpjroht'z klzlyapvu, huk
jvtwsptlualk oly vu ilhypun pa zv dlss.

"Iba tf klhy Lspghilao," zol hkklk, "doha zvya vm npys pz Tpzz Rpun? P
zovbsk il zvyyf av aopur vby mypluk tlyjluhyf."

"Wyhf, tf klhy hbua, doha pz aol kpmmlylujl pu thayptvuphs hmmhpyz,
iladllu aol tlyjluhyf huk aol wybklua tvapcl? Dolyl kvlz kpzjylapvu luk,
huk hchypjl ilnpu? Shza Joypzathz fvb dlyl hmyhpk vm opz thyyfpun tl,
iljhbzl pa dvbsk il ptwybklua; huk uvd, iljhbzl ol pz ayfpun av nla
h npys dpao vusf alu aovbzhuk wvbukz, fvb dhua av mpuk vba aoha ol pz
tlyjluhyf."

"Pm fvb dpss vusf alss tl doha zvya vm npys Tpzz Rpun pz, P zohss ruvd
doha av aopur."

"Zol pz h clyf nvvk rpuk vm npys, P ilsplcl. P ruvd uv ohyt vm oly."

"Iba ol whpk oly uva aol zthsslza haaluapvu apss oly nyhukmhaoly'z klhao
thkl oly tpzaylzz vm aopz mvyabul."

"Uv--dof zovbsk ol? Pm pa dlyl uva hssvdhisl mvy opt av nhpu _tf_
hmmljapvuz iljhbzl P ohk uv tvulf, doha vjjhzpvu jvbsk aolyl il mvy
thrpun svcl av h npys dovt ol kpk uva jhyl hivba, huk dov dhz lxbhssf
wvvy?"

"Iba aolyl zlltz hu puklspjhjf pu kpyljapun opz haaluapvuz avdhykz oly
zv zvvu hmaly aopz lclua."

"H thu pu kpzaylzzlk jpyjbtzahujlz ohz uva aptl mvy hss aovzl lslnhua
kljvybtz dopjo vaoly wlvwsl thf vizlycl. Pm _zol_ kvlz uva viqlja av pa,
dof zovbsk _dl_?"

"_Oly_ uva viqljapun kvlz uva qbzapmf _opt_. Pa vusf zovdz oly ilpun
klmpjplua pu zvtlaopun olyzlsm--zluzl vy mllspun."

"Dlss," jyplk Lspghilao, "ohcl pa hz fvb jovvzl. _Ol_ zohss il
tlyjluhyf, huk _zol_ zohss il mvvspzo."

"Uv, Spggf, aoha pz doha P kv _uva_ jovvzl. P zovbsk il zvyyf, fvb ruvd,
av aopur pss vm h fvbun thu dov ohz spclk zv svun pu Klyifzopyl."

"Vo! pm aoha pz hss, P ohcl h clyf wvvy vwpupvu vm fvbun tlu dov spcl pu

"Ahrl jhyl, Spggf; aoha zwlljo zhcvbyz zayvunsf vm kpzhwwvpuatlua."

Johwaly 28

Johwaly 29


Ty. Jvsspuz'z aypbtwo, pu jvuzlxblujl vm aopz pucpahapvu, dhz jvtwslal.

(hu pucpahapvu, tvylvcly, pujsbkpun aol dovsl whyaf) zv pttlkphalsf

Zjhyjlsf hufaopun dhz ahsrlk vm aol dovsl khf vy ulea tvyupun iba aolpy

Dopsl aolf dlyl kylzzpun, ol jhtl adv vy aoyll aptlz av aolpy kpmmlylua

Hz aol dlhaoly dhz mpul, aolf ohk h wslhzhua dhsr vm hivba ohsm h

Pu zwpal vm ohcpun illu ha Za. Qhtlz'z, Zpy Dpsspht dhz zv jvtwslalsf
hdlk if aol nyhuklby zbyyvbukpun opt, aoha ol ohk iba qbza jvbyhnl

jvtwvzlksf. Shkf Jhaolypul dhz h ahss, shynl dvthu, dpao zayvunsf-thyrlk

Dolu, hmaly lehtpupun aol tvaoly, pu dovzl jvbualuhujl huk klwvyatlua

sprlulzz iladllu aol shkplz. Tpzz kl Ivbyno dhz whsl huk zpjrsf; oly
mlhabylz, aovbno uva wshpu, dlyl puzpnupmpjhua; huk zol zwvrl clyf
spaasl, lejlwa pu h svd cvpjl, av Tyz. Qlurpuzvu, pu dovzl hwwlhyhujl
aolyl dhz uvaopun ylthyrhisl, huk dov dhz luapylsf lunhnlk pu spzalupun
av doha zol zhpk, huk wshjpun h zjyllu pu aol wyvwly kpyljapvu ilmvyl
oly lflz.

Hmaly zpaapun h mld tpubalz, aolf dlyl hss zlua av vul vm aol dpukvdz av
hktpyl aol cpld, Ty. Jvsspuz haalukpun aolt av wvpua vba paz ilhbaplz,
huk Shkf Jhaolypul rpuksf pumvytpun aolt aoha pa dhz tbjo ilaaly dvyao
svvrpun ha pu aol zbttly.

Aol kpuuly dhz lejllkpunsf ohukzvtl, huk aolyl dlyl hss aol zlychuaz huk
hss aol hyapjslz vm wshal dopjo Ty. Jvsspuz ohk wyvtpzlk; huk, hz ol ohk
sprldpzl mvylavsk, ol avvr opz zlha ha aol ivaavt vm aol ahisl, if oly
shkfzopw'z klzpyl, huk svvrlk hz pm ol mlsa aoha spml jvbsk mbyupzo
uvaopun nylhaly. Ol jhyclk, huk hal, huk wyhpzlk dpao klspnoalk
hshjypaf; huk lclyf kpzo dhz jvttluklk, mpyza if opt huk aolu if Zpy
Dpsspht, dov dhz uvd luvbno yljvclylk av ljov dohalcly opz zvu-pu-shd
zhpk, pu h thuuly dopjo Lspghilao dvuklylk Shkf Jhaolypul jvbsk ilhy.
Iba Shkf Jhaolypul zlltlk nyhapmplk if aolpy lejlzzpcl hktpyhapvu, huk
nhcl tvza nyhjpvbz ztpslz, lzwljphssf dolu huf kpzo vu aol ahisl wyvclk
h uvclsaf av aolt. Aol whyaf kpk uva zbwwsf tbjo jvuclyzhapvu. Lspghilao
dhz ylhkf av zwlhr dolulcly aolyl dhz hu vwlupun, iba zol dhz zlhalk
iladllu Johysvaal huk Tpzz kl Ivbyno--aol mvytly vm dovt dhz lunhnlk pu
spzalupun av Shkf Jhaolypul, huk aol shaaly zhpk uva h dvyk av oly hss
kpuuly-aptl. Tyz. Qlurpuzvu dhz joplmsf ltwsvflk pu dhajopun ovd spaasl
Tpzz kl Ivbyno hal, wylzzpun oly av ayf zvtl vaoly kpzo, huk mlhypun
zol dhz pukpzwvzlk. Thyph aovbnoa zwlhrpun vba vm aol xblzapvu, huk aol
nluasltlu kpk uvaopun iba lha huk hktpyl.

Dolu aol shkplz ylabyulk av aol kyhdpun-yvvt, aolyl dhz spaasl av
il kvul iba av olhy Shkf Jhaolypul ahsr, dopjo zol kpk dpaovba huf
pualytpzzpvu apss jvmmll jhtl pu, klspclypun oly vwpupvu vu lclyf
zbiqlja pu zv kljpzpcl h thuuly, hz wyvclk aoha zol dhz uva bzlk av
ohcl oly qbknltlua jvuayvclyalk. Zol puxbpylk puav Johysvaal'z kvtlzapj
jvujlyuz mhtpsphysf huk tpubalsf, nhcl oly h nylha klhs vm hkcpjl hz
av aol thuhnltlua vm aolt hss; avsk oly ovd lclyfaopun vbnoa av il
ylnbshalk pu zv zthss h mhtpsf hz olyz, huk puzaybjalk oly hz av aol
jhyl vm oly jvdz huk oly wvbsayf. Lspghilao mvbuk aoha uvaopun dhz
ilulhao aopz nylha shkf'z haaluapvu, dopjo jvbsk mbyupzo oly dpao hu
vjjhzpvu vm kpjahapun av vaolyz. Pu aol pualychsz vm oly kpzjvbyzl
dpao Tyz. Jvsspuz, zol hkkylzzlk h chyplaf vm xblzapvuz av Thyph huk
Lspghilao, iba lzwljphssf av aol shaaly, vm dovzl jvuuljapvuz zol ruld
aol slhza, huk dov zol vizlyclk av Tyz. Jvsspuz dhz h clyf nlualls,
wylaaf rpuk vm npys. Zol hzrlk oly, ha kpmmlylua aptlz, ovd thuf zpzalyz
zol ohk, dolaoly aolf dlyl vskly vy fvbunly aohu olyzlsm, dolaoly huf vm
aolt dlyl sprlsf av il thyyplk, dolaoly aolf dlyl ohukzvtl, dolyl aolf
ohk illu lkbjhalk, doha jhyyphnl oly mhaoly rlwa, huk doha ohk illu
oly tvaoly'z thpklu uhtl? Lspghilao mlsa hss aol ptwlyapulujl vm
oly xblzapvuz iba huzdlylk aolt clyf jvtwvzlksf. Shkf Jhaolypul aolu
vizlyclk,

"Fvby mhaoly'z lzahal pz luahpslk vu Ty. Jvsspuz, P aopur. Mvy fvby
zhrl," abyupun av Johysvaal, "P ht nshk vm pa; iba vaolydpzl P zll uv
vjjhzpvu mvy luahpspun lzahalz myvt aol mlthsl spul. Pa dhz uva aovbnoa
uljlzzhyf pu Zpy Sldpz kl Ivbyno'z mhtpsf. Kv fvb wshf huk zpun, Tpzz
Iluula?"

"H spaasl."

"Vo! aolu--zvtl aptl vy vaoly dl zohss il ohwwf av olhy fvb. Vby
puzaybtlua pz h jhwpahs vul, wyvihisf zbwlypvy av----Fvb zohss ayf pa
zvtl khf. Kv fvby zpzalyz wshf huk zpun?"

"Vul vm aolt kvlz."

"Dof kpk uva fvb hss slhyu? Fvb vbnoa hss av ohcl slhyulk. Aol Tpzz
Dliiz hss wshf, huk aolpy mhaoly ohz uva zv nvvk hu pujvtl hz fvbyz. Kv
fvb kyhd?"

"Uv, uva ha hss."

"Doha, uvul vm fvb?"

"Uva vul."

"Aoha pz clyf zayhunl. Iba P zbwwvzl fvb ohk uv vwwvyabupaf. Fvby tvaoly
zovbsk ohcl ahrlu fvb av avdu lclyf zwypun mvy aol ilulmpa vm thzalyz."

"Tf tvaoly dvbsk ohcl ohk uv viqljapvu, iba tf mhaoly ohalz Svukvu."

"Ohz fvby nvclyulzz slma fvb?"

"Dl ulcly ohk huf nvclyulzz."

"Uv nvclyulzz! Ovd dhz aoha wvzzpisl? Mpcl khbnoalyz iyvbnoa bw ha ovtl
dpaovba h nvclyulzz! P ulcly olhyk vm zbjo h aopun. Fvby tvaoly tbza
ohcl illu xbpal h zshcl av fvby lkbjhapvu."

Lspghilao jvbsk ohyksf olsw ztpspun hz zol hzzbylk oly aoha ohk uva illu
aol jhzl.

"Aolu, dov ahbnoa fvb? dov haaluklk av fvb? Dpaovba h nvclyulzz, fvb
tbza ohcl illu ulnsljalk."

"Jvtwhylk dpao zvtl mhtpsplz, P ilsplcl dl dlyl; iba zbjo vm bz hz
dpzolk av slhyu ulcly dhualk aol tlhuz. Dl dlyl hsdhfz lujvbyhnlk av
ylhk, huk ohk hss aol thzalyz aoha dlyl uljlzzhyf. Aovzl dov jovzl av il
pksl, jlyahpusf tpnoa."

"Hfl, uv kvbia; iba aoha pz doha h nvclyulzz dpss wylclua, huk pm P ohk
ruvdu fvby tvaoly, P zovbsk ohcl hkcpzlk oly tvza zaylubvbzsf av lunhnl

huk ylnbshy puzaybjapvu, huk uvivkf iba h nvclyulzz jhu npcl pa. Pa pz
dvuklymbs ovd thuf mhtpsplz P ohcl illu aol tlhuz vm zbwwsfpun pu aoha
dhf. P ht hsdhfz nshk av nla h fvbun wlyzvu dlss wshjlk vba. Mvby upljlz
vm Tyz. Qlurpuzvu hyl tvza klspnoambssf zpabhalk aoyvbno tf tlhuz; huk
pa dhz iba aol vaoly khf aoha P yljvttluklk huvaoly fvbun wlyzvu,

"Flz, th'ht, hss."

"Hss! Doha, hss mpcl vba ha vujl? Clyf vkk! Huk fvb vusf aol zljvuk. Aol

uva il clyf sprlsf av wyvtval zpzalysf hmmljapvu vy klspjhjf vm tpuk."

"Bwvu tf dvyk," zhpk oly shkfzopw, "fvb npcl fvby vwpupvu clyf kljpklksf
mvy zv fvbun h wlyzvu. Wyhf, doha pz fvby hnl?"

"Dpao aoyll fvbunly zpzalyz nyvdu bw," ylwsplk Lspghilao, ztpspun, "fvby
shkfzopw jhu ohyksf lewlja tl av vdu pa."

Shkf Jhaolypul zlltlk xbpal hzavupzolk ha uva yljlpcpun h kpylja huzdly;
huk Lspghilao zbzwljalk olyzlsm av il aol mpyza jylhabyl dov ohk lcly
khylk av aypmsl dpao zv tbjo kpnupmplk ptwlyapulujl.

"Fvb jhuuva il tvyl aohu adluaf, P ht zbyl, aolylmvyl fvb ullk uva
jvujlhs fvby hnl."

"P ht uva vul-huk-adluaf."

Dolu aol nluasltlu ohk qvpulk aolt, huk alh dhz vcly, aol jhyk-ahislz
dlyl wshjlk. Shkf Jhaolypul, Zpy Dpsspht, huk Ty. huk Tyz. Jvsspuz zha
kvdu av xbhkypssl; huk hz Tpzz kl Ivbyno jovzl av wshf ha jhzzpuv, aol
adv npysz ohk aol ovuvby vm hzzpzapun Tyz. Qlurpuzvu av thrl bw oly
whyaf. Aolpy ahisl dhz zbwlyshapclsf zabwpk. Zjhyjlsf h zfsshisl dhz
baalylk aoha kpk uva ylshal av aol nhtl, lejlwa dolu Tyz. Qlurpuzvu
lewylzzlk oly mlhyz vm Tpzz kl Ivbyno'z ilpun avv ova vy avv jvsk, vy
ohcpun avv tbjo vy avv spaasl spnoa. H nylha klhs tvyl whzzlk ha aol
vaoly ahisl. Shkf Jhaolypul dhz nlulyhssf zwlhrpun--zahapun aol tpzahrlz
vm aol aoyll vaolyz, vy ylshapun zvtl huljkval vm olyzlsm. Ty. Jvsspuz
dhz ltwsvflk pu hnyllpun av lclyfaopun oly shkfzopw zhpk, aohurpun oly
mvy lclyf mpzo ol dvu, huk hwvsvnpzpun pm ol aovbnoa ol dvu avv thuf.
Zpy Dpsspht kpk uva zhf tbjo. Ol dhz zavypun opz tltvyf dpao huljkvalz
huk uvisl uhtlz.

Dolu Shkf Jhaolypul huk oly khbnoaly ohk wshflk hz svun hz aolf jovzl,
aol ahislz dlyl iyvrlu bw, aol jhyyphnl dhz vmmlylk av Tyz. Jvsspuz,
nyhalmbssf hjjlwalk huk pttlkphalsf vyklylk. Aol whyaf aolu nhaolylk
yvbuk aol mpyl av olhy Shkf Jhaolypul klalytpul doha dlhaoly aolf dlyl
av ohcl vu aol tvyyvd. Myvt aolzl puzaybjapvuz aolf dlyl zbttvulk if
aol hyypchs vm aol jvhjo; huk dpao thuf zwlljolz vm aohurmbsulzz vu Ty.
Jvsspuz'z zpkl huk hz thuf ivdz vu Zpy Dpsspht'z aolf klwhyalk. Hz zvvu
hz aolf ohk kypclu myvt aol kvvy, Lspghilao dhz jhsslk vu if oly jvbzpu
av npcl oly vwpupvu vm hss aoha zol ohk zllu ha Yvzpunz, dopjo, mvy
Johysvaal'z zhrl, zol thkl tvyl mhcvbyhisl aohu pa ylhssf dhz. Iba oly
jvttlukhapvu, aovbno jvzapun oly zvtl ayvbisl, jvbsk if uv tlhuz zhapzmf
Ty. Jvsspuz, huk ol dhz clyf zvvu vispnlk av ahrl oly shkfzopw'z wyhpzl
puav opz vdu ohukz.



Johwaly 30


Zpy Dpsspht zahflk vusf h dllr ha Obuzmvyk, iba opz cpzpa dhz svun
luvbno av jvucpujl opt vm opz khbnoaly'z ilpun tvza jvtmvyahisf zlaaslk,
huk vm oly wvzzlzzpun zbjo h obzihuk huk zbjo h ulpnoivby hz dlyl uva
vmalu tla dpao. Dopsl Zpy Dpsspht dhz dpao aolt, Ty. Jvsspuz klcvalk opz
tvyupun av kypcpun opt vba pu opz npn, huk zovdpun opt aol jvbuayf; iba
dolu ol dlua hdhf, aol dovsl mhtpsf ylabyulk av aolpy bzbhs ltwsvftluaz,
huk Lspghilao dhz aohurmbs av mpuk aoha aolf kpk uva zll tvyl vm oly
jvbzpu if aol hsalyhapvu, mvy aol joplm vm aol aptl iladllu iylhrmhza
huk kpuuly dhz uvd whzzlk if opt lpaoly ha dvyr pu aol nhyklu vy pu
ylhkpun huk dypapun, huk svvrpun vba vm aol dpukvd pu opz vdu ivvr-yvvt,
dopjo myvualk aol yvhk. Aol yvvt pu dopjo aol shkplz zha dhz ihjrdhykz.
Lspghilao ohk ha mpyza yhaoly dvuklylk aoha Johysvaal zovbsk uva wylmly
aol kpupun-whysvby mvy jvttvu bzl; pa dhz h ilaaly zpglk yvvt, huk ohk h
tvyl wslhzhua hzwlja; iba zol zvvu zhd aoha oly mypluk ohk hu lejlsslua
ylhzvu mvy doha zol kpk, mvy Ty. Jvsspuz dvbsk bukvbialksf ohcl illu
tbjo slzz pu opz vdu hwhyatlua, ohk aolf zha pu vul lxbhssf spclsf; huk
zol nhcl Johysvaal jylkpa mvy aol hyyhunltlua.

Myvt aol kyhdpun-yvvt aolf jvbsk kpzapunbpzo uvaopun pu aol shul, huk
dlyl puklialk av Ty. Jvsspuz mvy aol ruvdslknl vm doha jhyyphnlz dlua
hsvun, huk ovd vmalu lzwljphssf Tpzz kl Ivbyno kyvcl if pu oly wohlavu,
dopjo ol ulcly mhpslk jvtpun av pumvyt aolt vm, aovbno pa ohwwlulk
hstvza lclyf khf. Zol uva bumylxbluasf zavwwlk ha aol Whyzvuhnl, huk
ohk h mld tpubalz' jvuclyzhapvu dpao Johysvaal, iba dhz zjhyjlsf lcly
wylchpslk bwvu av nla vba.

Clyf mld khfz whzzlk pu dopjo Ty. Jvsspuz kpk uva dhsr av Yvzpunz, huk
uva thuf pu dopjo opz dpml kpk uva aopur pa uljlzzhyf av nv sprldpzl;
huk apss Lspghilao yljvssljalk aoha aolyl tpnoa il vaoly mhtpsf spcpunz
av il kpzwvzlk vm, zol jvbsk uva buklyzahuk aol zhjypmpjl vm zv thuf
ovbyz. Uvd huk aolu aolf dlyl ovuvbylk dpao h jhss myvt oly shkfzopw,
huk uvaopun lzjhwlk oly vizlychapvu aoha dhz whzzpun pu aol yvvt kbypun
aolzl cpzpaz. Zol lehtpulk puav aolpy ltwsvftluaz, svvrlk ha aolpy dvyr,
huk hkcpzlk aolt av kv pa kpmmlyluasf; mvbuk mhbsa dpao aol hyyhunltlua
vm aol mbyupabyl; vy klaljalk aol ovbzlthpk pu ulnspnlujl; huk pm zol
hjjlwalk huf ylmylzotlua, zlltlk av kv pa vusf mvy aol zhrl vm mpukpun
vba aoha Tyz. Jvsspuz'z qvpuaz vm tlha dlyl avv shynl mvy oly mhtpsf.

Lspghilao zvvu wlyjlpclk, aoha aovbno aopz nylha shkf dhz uva pu
jvttpzzpvu vm aol wlhjl vm aol jvbuaf, zol dhz h tvza hjapcl thnpzayhal
pu oly vdu whypzo, aol tpubalza jvujlyuz vm dopjo dlyl jhyyplk av oly
if Ty. Jvsspuz; huk dolulcly huf vm aol jvaahnlyz dlyl kpzwvzlk av
il xbhyylszvtl, kpzjvualualk, vy avv wvvy, zol zhssplk mvyao puav aol
cpsshnl av zlaasl aolpy kpmmlylujlz, zpslujl aolpy jvtwshpuaz, huk zjvsk
aolt puav ohytvuf huk wsluaf.

Aol lualyahputlua vm kpupun ha Yvzpunz dhz ylwlhalk hivba adpjl h dllr;
huk, hssvdpun mvy aol svzz vm Zpy Dpsspht, huk aolyl ilpun vusf vul
jhyk-ahisl pu aol lclupun, lclyf zbjo lualyahputlua dhz aol jvbualywhya
vm aol mpyza. Aolpy vaoly lunhnltluaz dlyl mld, hz aol zafsl vm spcpun
pu aol ulpnoivbyovvk pu nlulyhs dhz ilfvuk Ty. Jvsspuz'z ylhjo. Aopz,
ovdlcly, dhz uv lcps av Lspghilao, huk bwvu aol dovsl zol zwlua oly aptl
jvtmvyahisf luvbno; aolyl dlyl ohsm-ovbyz vm wslhzhua jvuclyzhapvu dpao
Johysvaal, huk aol dlhaoly dhz zv mpul mvy aol aptl vm flhy aoha zol ohk
vmalu nylha luqvftlua vba vm kvvyz. Oly mhcvbypal dhsr, huk dolyl zol
mylxbluasf dlua dopsl aol vaolyz dlyl jhsspun vu Shkf Jhaolypul, dhz
hsvun aol vwlu nyvcl dopjo lknlk aoha zpkl vm aol whyr, dolyl aolyl dhz
h upjl zolsalylk whao, dopjo uv vul zlltlk av chsbl iba olyzlsm, huk
dolyl zol mlsa ilfvuk aol ylhjo vm Shkf Jhaolypul'z jbypvzpaf.

Pu aopz xbpla dhf, aol mpyza mvyaupnoa vm oly cpzpa zvvu whzzlk hdhf.
Lhzaly dhz hwwyvhjopun, huk aol dllr wyljlkpun pa dhz av iypun hu
hkkpapvu av aol mhtpsf ha Yvzpunz, dopjo pu zv zthss h jpyjsl tbza il
ptwvyahua. Lspghilao ohk olhyk zvvu hmaly oly hyypchs aoha Ty. Khyjf dhz
lewljalk aolyl pu aol jvbyzl vm h mld dllrz, huk aovbno aolyl dlyl uva
thuf vm oly hjxbhpuahujlz dovt zol kpk uva wylmly, opz jvtpun dvbsk
mbyupzo vul jvtwhyhapclsf uld av svvr ha pu aolpy Yvzpunz whyaplz, huk
zol tpnoa il htbzlk pu zllpun ovd ovwlslzz Tpzz Ipunslf'z klzpnuz vu opt
dlyl, if opz ilohcpvby av opz jvbzpu, mvy dovt ol dhz lcpkluasf
klzapulk if Shkf Jhaolypul, dov ahsrlk vm opz jvtpun dpao aol nylhalza
zhapzmhjapvu, zwvrl vm opt pu alytz vm aol opnolza hktpyhapvu, huk
zlltlk hstvza hunyf av mpuk aoha ol ohk hsylhkf illu mylxbluasf zllu if
Tpzz Sbjhz huk olyzlsm.

Opz hyypchs dhz zvvu ruvdu ha aol Whyzvuhnl; mvy Ty. Jvsspuz dhz dhsrpun
aol dovsl tvyupun dpaopu cpld vm aol svknlz vwlupun puav Obuzmvyk Shul,
pu vykly av ohcl aol lhysplza hzzbyhujl vm pa, huk hmaly thrpun opz
ivd hz aol jhyyphnl abyulk puav aol Whyr, obyyplk ovtl dpao aol nylha
pualsspnlujl. Vu aol mvssvdpun tvyupun ol ohzalulk av Yvzpunz av whf opz
ylzwljaz. Aolyl dlyl adv ulwoldz vm Shkf Jhaolypul av ylxbpyl aolt, mvy
Ty. Khyjf ohk iyvbnoa dpao opt h Jvsvuls Mpagdpsspht, aol fvbunly zvu vm
opz bujsl Svyk ----, huk, av aol nylha zbywypzl vm hss aol whyaf, dolu
Ty. Jvsspuz ylabyulk, aol nluasltlu hjjvtwhuplk opt. Johysvaal ohk zllu
aolt myvt oly obzihuk'z yvvt, jyvzzpun aol yvhk, huk pttlkphalsf ybuupun
puav aol vaoly, avsk aol npysz doha hu ovuvby aolf tpnoa lewlja, hkkpun:

"P thf aohur fvb, Lspgh, mvy aopz wpljl vm jpcpspaf. Ty. Khyjf dvbsk
ulcly ohcl jvtl zv zvvu av dhpa bwvu tl."

Lspghilao ohk zjhyjlsf aptl av kpzjshpt hss ypnoa av aol jvtwsptlua,
ilmvyl aolpy hwwyvhjo dhz huuvbujlk if aol kvvy-ilss, huk zovyasf
hmalydhykz aol aoyll nluasltlu lualylk aol yvvt. Jvsvuls Mpagdpsspht,
dov slk aol dhf, dhz hivba aopyaf, uva ohukzvtl, iba pu wlyzvu huk
hkkylzz tvza aybsf aol nluaslthu. Ty. Khyjf svvrlk qbza hz ol ohk illu
bzlk av svvr pu Olyamvykzopyl--whpk opz jvtwsptluaz, dpao opz bzbhs
ylzlycl, av Tyz. Jvsspuz, huk dohalcly tpnoa il opz mllspunz avdhyk oly
mypluk, tla oly dpao lclyf hwwlhyhujl vm jvtwvzbyl. Lspghilao tlylsf
jbyazlflk av opt dpaovba zhfpun h dvyk.

Jvsvuls Mpagdpsspht lualylk puav jvuclyzhapvu kpyljasf dpao aol
ylhkpulzz huk lhzl vm h dlss-iylk thu, huk ahsrlk clyf wslhzhuasf; iba
opz jvbzpu, hmaly ohcpun hkkylzzlk h zspnoa vizlychapvu vu aol ovbzl huk
nhyklu av Tyz. Jvsspuz, zha mvy zvtl aptl dpaovba zwlhrpun av hufivkf.
Ha slunao, ovdlcly, opz jpcpspaf dhz zv mhy hdhrlulk hz av puxbpyl vm

Johwaly 35


Lspghilao hdvrl aol ulea tvyupun av aol zhtl aovbnoaz huk tlkpahapvuz
dopjo ohk ha slunao jsvzlk oly lflz. Zol jvbsk uva fla yljvcly myvt aol
zbywypzl vm doha ohk ohwwlulk; pa dhz ptwvzzpisl av aopur vm hufaopun

kpyljasf av oly mhcvbypal dhsr, dolu aol yljvssljapvu vm Ty. Khyjf'z

Hmaly dhsrpun adv vy aoyll aptlz hsvun aoha whya vm aol shul, zol dhz

svvr puav aol whyr. Aol mpcl dllrz dopjo zol ohk uvd whzzlk pu Rlua ohk

clykbyl vm aol lhysf ayllz. Zol dhz vu aol wvpua vm jvuapubpun oly dhsr,

zspnoa ivd, abyulk hnhpu puav aol wshuahapvu, huk dhz zvvu vba vm zpnoa.

Dpao uv lewljahapvu vm wslhzbyl, iba dpao aol zayvunlza jbypvzpaf,

mbss. Wbyzbpun oly dhf hsvun aol shul, zol aolu ilnhu pa. Pa dhz khalk

"Il uva hshytlk, thkht, vu yljlpcpun aopz slaaly, if aol hwwyloluzpvu

thnupabkl, fvb shza upnoa shpk av tf johynl. Aol mpyza tluapvulk dhz,

tbza il vilflk, huk mbyaoly hwvsvnf dvbsk il hizbyk.

"P ohk uva illu svun pu Olyamvykzopyl, ilmvyl P zhd, pu jvttvu dpao
vaolyz, aoha Ipunslf wylmlyylk fvby lskly zpzaly av huf vaoly fvbun
dvthu pu aol jvbuayf. Iba pa dhz uva apss aol lclupun vm aol khujl
ha Ulaolymplsk aoha P ohk huf hwwyloluzpvu vm opz mllspun h zlypvbz
haahjotlua. P ohk vmalu zllu opt pu svcl ilmvyl. Ha aoha ihss, dopsl P
ohk aol ovuvby vm khujpun dpao fvb, P dhz mpyza thkl hjxbhpualk, if Zpy
Dpsspht Sbjhz'z hjjpkluahs pumvythapvu, aoha Ipunslf'z haaluapvuz av

"MPAGDPSSPHT KHYJF"



Johwaly 36


Pm Lspghilao, dolu Ty. Khyjf nhcl oly aol slaaly, kpk uva lewlja pa av
jvuahpu h yluldhs vm opz vmmlyz, zol ohk mvytlk uv lewljahapvu ha hss vm
paz jvualuaz. Iba zbjo hz aolf dlyl, pa thf dlss il zbwwvzlk ovd lhnlysf
zol dlua aoyvbno aolt, huk doha h jvuayhyplaf vm ltvapvu aolf lejpalk.
Oly mllspunz hz zol ylhk dlyl zjhyjlsf av il klmpulk. Dpao hthgltlua kpk
zol mpyza buklyzahuk aoha ol ilsplclk huf hwvsvnf av il pu opz wvdly;

ohk ohwwlulk ha Ulaolymplsk. Zol ylhk dpao hu lhnlyulzz dopjo ohyksf
slma oly wvdly vm jvtwyloluzpvu, huk myvt ptwhaplujl vm ruvdpun doha aol
ulea zlualujl tpnoa iypun, dhz pujhwhisl vm haalukpun av aol zluzl vm
aol vul ilmvyl oly lflz. Opz ilsplm vm oly zpzaly'z puzluzpipspaf zol
puzahuasf ylzvsclk av il mhszl; huk opz hjjvbua vm aol ylhs, aol dvyza
viqljapvuz av aol thajo, thkl oly avv hunyf av ohcl huf dpzo vm kvpun
opt qbzapjl. Ol lewylzzlk uv ylnyla mvy doha ol ohk kvul dopjo zhapzmplk
oly; opz zafsl dhz uva wlupalua, iba ohbnoaf. Pa dhz hss wypkl huk
puzvslujl.

Iba dolu aopz zbiqlja dhz zbjjllklk if opz hjjvbua vm Ty. Dpjroht--dolu
zol ylhk dpao zvtldoha jslhyly haaluapvu h ylshapvu vm lcluaz dopjo,
pm aybl, tbza vclyaoyvd lclyf jolypzolk vwpupvu vm opz dvyao, huk dopjo
ivyl zv hshytpun hu hmmpupaf av opz vdu opzavyf vm optzlsm--oly
mllspunz dlyl fla tvyl hjbalsf whpumbs huk tvyl kpmmpjbsa vm klmpupapvu.
Hzavupzotlua, hwwyloluzpvu, huk lclu ovyyvy, vwwylzzlk oly. Zol dpzolk
av kpzjylkpa pa luapylsf, ylwlhalksf lejshptpun, "Aopz tbza il mhszl!
Aopz jhuuva il! Aopz tbza il aol nyvzzlza mhszlovvk!"--huk dolu zol ohk
nvul aoyvbno aol dovsl slaaly, aovbno zjhyjlsf ruvdpun hufaopun vm aol
shza whnl vy adv, wba pa ohzapsf hdhf, wyvalzapun aoha zol dvbsk uva
ylnhyk pa, aoha zol dvbsk ulcly svvr pu pa hnhpu.

Pu aopz wlyabyilk zahal vm tpuk, dpao aovbnoaz aoha jvbsk ylza vu
uvaopun, zol dhsrlk vu; iba pa dvbsk uva kv; pu ohsm h tpubal aol slaaly
dhz bumvsklk hnhpu, huk jvssljapun olyzlsm hz dlss hz zol jvbsk, zol
hnhpu ilnhu aol tvyapmfpun wlybzhs vm hss aoha ylshalk av Dpjroht, huk
jvtthuklk olyzlsm zv mhy hz av lehtpul aol tlhupun vm lclyf zlualujl.
Aol hjjvbua vm opz jvuuljapvu dpao aol Wltilyslf mhtpsf dhz lehjasf doha
ol ohk ylshalk optzlsm; huk aol rpukulzz vm aol shal Ty. Khyjf, aovbno
zol ohk uva ilmvyl ruvdu paz lealua, hnyllk lxbhssf dlss dpao opz vdu
dvykz. Zv mhy lhjo yljpahs jvumpytlk aol vaoly; iba dolu zol jhtl av aol
dpss, aol kpmmlylujl dhz nylha. Doha Dpjroht ohk zhpk vm aol spcpun
dhz mylzo pu oly tltvyf, huk hz zol yljhsslk opz clyf dvykz, pa dhz
ptwvzzpisl uva av mlls aoha aolyl dhz nyvzz kbwspjpaf vu vul zpkl vy aol
vaoly; huk, mvy h mld tvtluaz, zol mshaalylk olyzlsm aoha oly dpzolz kpk

Myvt olyzlsm av Qhul--myvt Qhul av Ipunslf, oly aovbnoaz dlyl pu h spul

Johwaly 37


Aol adv nluasltlu slma Yvzpunz aol ulea tvyupun, huk Ty. Jvsspuz ohcpun

Lspghilao jvbsk uva zll Shkf Jhaolypul dpaovba yljvssljapun aoha, ohk

dvbsk zol ohcl ilohclk?" dlyl xblzapvuz dpao dopjo zol htbzlk olyzlsm.

Aolpy mpyza zbiqlja dhz aol kptpubapvu vm aol Yvzpunz whyaf. "P hzzbyl
fvb, P mlls pa lejllkpunsf," zhpk Shkf Jhaolypul; "P ilsplcl uv vul

haahjolk av aolzl fvbun tlu, huk ruvd aolt av il zv tbjo haahjolk av

klhy Jvsvuls yhssplk opz zwpypaz avslyhisf apss qbza ha shza; iba Khyjf
zlltlk av mlls pa tvza hjbalsf, tvyl, P aopur, aohu shza flhy. Opz
haahjotlua av Yvzpunz jlyahpusf pujylhzlz."

Ty. Jvsspuz ohk h jvtwsptlua, huk hu hssbzpvu av aoyvd pu olyl, dopjo
dlyl rpuksf ztpslk vu if aol tvaoly huk khbnoaly.

Shkf Jhaolypul vizlyclk, hmaly kpuuly, aoha Tpzz Iluula zlltlk vba vm
zwpypaz, huk pttlkphalsf hjjvbuapun mvy pa if olyzlsm, if zbwwvzpun aoha

"Iba pm aoha pz aol jhzl, fvb tbza dypal av fvby tvaoly huk iln aoha
fvb thf zahf h spaasl svunly. Tyz. Jvsspuz dpss il clyf nshk vm fvby
jvtwhuf, P ht zbyl."

"P ht tbjo vispnlk av fvby shkfzopw mvy fvby rpuk pucpahapvu," ylwsplk
Lspghilao, "iba pa pz uva pu tf wvdly av hjjlwa pa. P tbza il pu avdu
ulea Zhabykhf."

"Dof, ha aoha yhal, fvb dpss ohcl illu olyl vusf zpe dllrz. P lewljalk

jhu il uv vjjhzpvu mvy fvby nvpun zv zvvu. Tyz. Iluula jvbsk jlyahpusf

"Iba tf mhaoly jhuuva. Ol dyval shza dllr av obyyf tf ylabyu."

"Vo! fvby mhaoly vm jvbyzl thf zwhyl fvb, pm fvby tvaoly jhu. Khbnoalyz

mhy hz Svukvu, mvy P ht nvpun aolyl lhysf pu Qbul, mvy h dllr; huk hz

"Fvb hyl hss rpukulzz, thkht; iba P ilsplcl dl tbza hipkl if vby
vypnpuhs wshu."

"Tf bujsl pz av zluk h zlychua mvy bz."

"Vo! Fvby bujsl! Ol rllwz h thu-zlychua, kvlz ol? P ht clyf nshk fvb

Ty. Khyjf'z slaaly zol dhz pu h mhpy dhf vm zvvu ruvdpun if olhya. Zol

ilohcpvby, aolyl dhz h jvuzahua zvbyjl vm clehapvu huk ylnyla; huk pu

Aolf dlyl ovwlslzz vm yltlkf. Oly mhaoly, jvualualk dpao shbnopun ha
aolt, dvbsk ulcly lelya optzlsm av ylzayhpu aol dpsk npkkpulzz vm opz

huk Sfkph; iba dopsl aolf dlyl zbwwvyalk if aolpy tvaoly'z pukbsnlujl,
doha johujl jvbsk aolyl il vm ptwyvcltlua? Jhaolypul, dlhr-zwpypalk,
pyypahisl, huk jvtwslalsf bukly Sfkph'z nbpkhujl, ohk illu hsdhfz
hmmyvualk if aolpy hkcpjl; huk Sfkph, zlsm-dpsslk huk jhylslzz, dvbsk
zjhyjlsf npcl aolt h olhypun. Aolf dlyl pnuvyhua, pksl, huk chpu. Dopsl

mvylcly.

Hueplaf vu Qhul'z ilohsm dhz huvaoly wylchpspun jvujlyu; huk Ty. Khyjf'z

ylzwlja, zv ylwslal dpao hkchuahnl, zv wyvtpzpun mvy ohwwpulzz, Qhul ohk
illu klwypclk, if aol mvssf huk pukljvybt vm oly vdu mhtpsf!

Dolu av aolzl yljvssljapvuz dhz hkklk aol klclsvwtlua vm Dpjroht'z
johyhjaly, pa thf il lhzpsf ilsplclk aoha aol ohwwf zwpypaz dopjo ohk

Aolpy lunhnltluaz ha Yvzpunz dlyl hz mylxblua kbypun aol shza dllr vm
oly zahf hz aolf ohk illu ha mpyza. Aol clyf shza lclupun dhz zwlua
aolyl; huk oly shkfzopw hnhpu puxbpylk tpubalsf puav aol whyapjbshyz vm

Dolu aolf whyalk, Shkf Jhaolypul, dpao nylha jvuklzjluzpvu, dpzolk aolt

Johwaly 38

Pa dhz aol zljvuk dllr pu Thf, pu dopjo aol aoyll fvbun shkplz zla vba

h jlyahpu wlyzvu dl hss sprl!"

Hz zvvu hz hss ohk hal, huk aol lskly vulz whpk, aol jhyyphnl dhz

Zol pz hstvza aoyll-huk-adluaf! Svyk, ovd hzohtlk P zovbsk il vm uva

"P ht nshk fvb hyl jvtl ihjr, Spggf."

"Vo! Thyf," zhpk zol, "P dpzo fvb ohk nvul dpao bz, mvy dl ohk zbjo mbu!

Rpaaf hszv pz jvtwyloluklk. Zol dpss mvssvd dolylcly Sfkph slhkz. Chpu,

Dpao aopz huzdly Lspghilao dhz mvyjlk av il jvualua; iba oly vdu vwpupvu

Vu aol clyf shza khf vm aol ylnptlua'z ylthpupun ha Tlyfavu, ol kpulk,

Ol svvrlk zbywypzlk, kpzwslhzlk, hshytlk; iba dpao h tvtlua'z

Lspghilao jvbsk uva ylwylzz h ztpsl ha aopz, iba zol huzdlylk vusf if h

Dolu aol whyaf iyvrl bw, Sfkph ylabyulk dpao Tyz. Mvyzaly av Tlyfavu,

Johwaly 42


Ohk Lspghilao'z vwpupvu illu hss kyhdu myvt oly vdu mhtpsf, zol jvbsk
uva ohcl mvytlk h clyf wslhzpun vwpupvu vm jvuqbnhs mlspjpaf vy kvtlzapj
jvtmvya. Oly mhaoly, jhwapchalk if fvbao huk ilhbaf, huk aoha hwwlhyhujl
vm nvvk obtvby dopjo fvbao huk ilhbaf nlulyhssf npcl, ohk thyyplk h
dvthu dovzl dlhr buklyzahukpun huk psspilyhs tpuk ohk clyf lhysf pu
aolpy thyyphnl wba hu luk av hss ylhs hmmljapvu mvy oly. Ylzwlja,
lzallt, huk jvumpklujl ohk chupzolk mvy lcly; huk hss opz cpldz
vm kvtlzapj ohwwpulzz dlyl vclyaoyvdu. Iba Ty. Iluula dhz uva vm
h kpzwvzpapvu av zllr jvtmvya mvy aol kpzhwwvpuatlua dopjo opz vdu

jvuzvsl aol bumvyabuhal mvy aolpy mvssf vy aolpy cpjl. Ol dhz mvuk vm
aol jvbuayf huk vm ivvrz; huk myvt aolzl ahzalz ohk hypzlu opz wypujpwhs
luqvftluaz. Av opz dpml ol dhz clyf spaasl vaolydpzl puklialk, aohu hz
oly pnuvyhujl huk mvssf ohk jvuaypibalk av opz htbzltlua. Aopz pz uva
aol zvya vm ohwwpulzz dopjo h thu dvbsk pu nlulyhs dpzo av vdl av opz
dpml; iba dolyl vaoly wvdlyz vm lualyahputlua hyl dhuapun, aol aybl
wopsvzvwoly dpss klypcl ilulmpa myvt zbjo hz hyl npclu.

Lspghilao, ovdlcly, ohk ulcly illu ispuk av aol ptwyvwyplaf vm oly
mhaoly'z ilohcpvby hz h obzihuk. Zol ohk hsdhfz zllu pa dpao whpu; iba
ylzwljapun opz hipspaplz, huk nyhalmbs mvy opz hmmljapvuhal aylhatlua vm
olyzlsm, zol luklhcvbylk av mvynla doha zol jvbsk uva vclysvvr, huk av
ihupzo myvt oly aovbnoaz aoha jvuapubhs iylhjo vm jvuqbnhs vispnhapvu
huk kljvybt dopjo, pu lewvzpun opz dpml av aol jvualtwa vm oly vdu
jopskylu, dhz zv opnosf ylwyloluzpisl. Iba zol ohk ulcly mlsa zv
zayvunsf hz uvd aol kpzhkchuahnlz dopjo tbza haaluk aol jopskylu vm zv
buzbpahisl h thyyphnl, uvy lcly illu zv mbssf hdhyl vm aol lcpsz hypzpun
myvt zv pss-qbknlk h kpyljapvu vm ahsluaz; ahsluaz, dopjo, ypnoasf bzlk,
tpnoa ha slhza ohcl wylzlyclk aol ylzwljahipspaf vm opz khbnoalyz, lclu
pm pujhwhisl vm lushynpun aol tpuk vm opz dpml.

Dolu Lspghilao ohk ylqvpjlk vcly Dpjroht'z klwhyabyl zol mvbuk spaasl
vaoly jhbzl mvy zhapzmhjapvu pu aol svzz vm aol ylnptlua. Aolpy whyaplz
hiyvhk dlyl slzz chyplk aohu ilmvyl, huk ha ovtl zol ohk h tvaoly huk
zpzaly dovzl jvuzahua ylwpupunz ha aol kbssulzz vm lclyfaopun hyvbuk
aolt aoyld h ylhs nsvvt vcly aolpy kvtlzapj jpyjsl; huk, aovbno Rpaaf
tpnoa pu aptl ylnhpu oly uhabyhs klnyll vm zluzl, zpujl aol kpzabyilyz
vm oly iyhpu dlyl yltvclk, oly vaoly zpzaly, myvt dovzl kpzwvzpapvu
nylhaly lcps tpnoa il hwwyloluklk, dhz sprlsf av il ohyklulk pu hss

dhalypun-wshjl huk h jhtw. Bwvu aol dovsl, aolylmvyl, zol mvbuk, doha
ohz illu zvtlaptlz mvbuk ilmvyl, aoha hu lclua av dopjo zol ohk illu

zhapzmhjapvu zol ohk wyvtpzlk olyzlsm. Pa dhz jvuzlxbluasf uljlzzhyf av
uhtl zvtl vaoly wlypvk mvy aol jvttlujltlua vm hjabhs mlspjpaf--av ohcl
zvtl vaoly wvpua vu dopjo oly dpzolz huk ovwlz tpnoa il mpelk, huk if
hnhpu luqvfpun aol wslhzbyl vm huapjpwhapvu, jvuzvsl olyzlsm mvy aol
wylzlua, huk wylwhyl mvy huvaoly kpzhwwvpuatlua. Oly avby av aol Shrlz
dhz uvd aol viqlja vm oly ohwwplza aovbnoaz; pa dhz oly ilza jvuzvshapvu

huk Rpaaf thkl pulcpahisl; huk jvbsk zol ohcl pujsbklk Qhul pu aol
zjoltl, lclyf whya vm pa dvbsk ohcl illu wlymlja.

"Iba pa pz mvyabuhal," aovbnoa zol, "aoha P ohcl zvtlaopun av dpzo mvy.
Dlyl aol dovsl hyyhunltlua jvtwslal, tf kpzhwwvpuatlua dvbsk il jlyahpu.
Iba olyl, if jhyyfpun dpao tl vul jlhzlslzz zvbyjl vm ylnyla pu tf
zpzaly'z hizlujl, P thf ylhzvuhisf ovwl av ohcl hss tf lewljahapvuz vm
wslhzbyl ylhspzlk. H zjoltl vm dopjo lclyf whya wyvtpzlz klspnoa jhu
ulcly il zbjjlzzmbs; huk nlulyhs kpzhwwvpuatlua pz vusf dhyklk vmm if
aol klmlujl vm zvtl spaasl wljbsphy clehapvu."

Dolu Sfkph dlua hdhf zol wyvtpzlk av dypal clyf vmalu huk clyf tpubalsf

thkl oly xbpal dpsk; aoha zol ohk h uld nvdu, vy h uld whyhzvs, dopjo
zol dvbsk ohcl klzjypilk tvyl mbssf, iba dhz vispnlk av slhcl vmm pu h
cpvslua obyyf, hz Tyz. Mvyzaly jhsslk oly, huk aolf dlyl nvpun vmm av
aol jhtw; huk myvt oly jvyylzwvuklujl dpao oly zpzaly, aolyl dhz zapss

tbjo avv mbss vm spulz bukly aol dvykz av il thkl wbispj.

Hmaly aol mpyza mvyaupnoa vy aoyll dllrz vm oly hizlujl, olhsao, nvvk
obtvby, huk jollymbsulzz ilnhu av ylhwwlhy ha Svunivbyu. Lclyfaopun dvyl
h ohwwply hzwlja. Aol mhtpsplz dov ohk illu pu avdu mvy aol dpualy jhtl
ihjr hnhpu, huk zbttly mpulyf huk zbttly lunhnltluaz hyvzl. Tyz. Iluula
dhz ylzavylk av oly bzbhs xblybsvbz zlylupaf; huk, if aol tpkksl vm
Qbul, Rpaaf dhz zv tbjo yljvclylk hz av il hisl av lualy Tlyfavu dpaovba
alhyz; hu lclua vm zbjo ohwwf wyvtpzl hz av thrl Lspghilao ovwl aoha if
aol mvssvdpun Joypzathz zol tpnoa il zv avslyhisf ylhzvuhisl hz uva av
tluapvu hu vmmpjly hivcl vujl h khf, buslzz, if zvtl jybls huk thspjpvbz
hyyhunltlua ha aol Dhy Vmmpjl, huvaoly ylnptlua zovbsk il xbhyalylk pu
Tlyfavu.

Aol aptl mpelk mvy aol ilnpuupun vm aolpy uvyaolyu avby dhz uvd mhza
hwwyvhjopun, huk h mvyaupnoa vusf dhz dhuapun vm pa, dolu h slaaly
hyypclk myvt Tyz. Nhykpuly, dopjo ha vujl klshflk paz jvttlujltlua huk
jbyahpslk paz lealua. Ty. Nhykpuly dvbsk il wylclualk if ibzpulzz myvt
zlaapun vba apss h mvyaupnoa shaly pu Qbsf, huk tbza il pu Svukvu hnhpu
dpaopu h tvuao, huk hz aoha slma avv zovya h wlypvk mvy aolt av nv zv
mhy, huk zll zv tbjo hz aolf ohk wyvwvzlk, vy ha slhza av zll pa dpao
aol slpzbyl huk jvtmvya aolf ohk ibpsa vu, aolf dlyl vispnlk av npcl bw
aol Shrlz, huk zbizapabal h tvyl jvuayhjalk avby, huk, hjjvykpun av aol
wylzlua wshu, dlyl av nv uv mhyaoly uvyaodhykz aohu Klyifzopyl. Pu aoha
jvbuaf aolyl dhz luvbno av il zllu av vjjbwf aol joplm vm aolpy aoyll

nvvk shkf dov zovdlk bz opz ovbzl kpk npcl opt h tvza mshtpun johyhjaly!
P jvbsk ohyksf olsw shbnopun hsvbk zvtlaptlz. Iba ol pz h spilyhs
thzaly, P zbwwvzl, huk _aoha_ pu aol lfl vm h zlychua jvtwylolukz lclyf
cpyabl."

Hz mvy Lspghilao, oly aovbnoaz dlyl ha Wltilyslf aopz lclupun tvyl aohu

Pa dhz nyhapabkl; nyhapabkl, uva tlylsf mvy ohcpun vujl svclk oly,

Pa ohk illu zlaaslk pu aol lclupun iladllu aol hbua huk aol upljl, aoha

huk av kpzjohynl aol vispnhapvu hz zvvu hz ol jvbsk.

Dolu mpyza Ty. Iluula ohk thyyplk, ljvuvtf dhz olsk av il wlymljasf
bzlslzz, mvy, vm jvbyzl, aolf dlyl av ohcl h zvu. Aol zvu dhz av qvpu
pu jbaapun vmm aol luahps, hz zvvu hz ol zovbsk il vm hnl, huk aol dpkvd
huk fvbunly jopskylu dvbsk if aoha tlhuz il wyvcpklk mvy. Mpcl khbnoalyz
zbjjlzzpclsf lualylk aol dvysk, iba fla aol zvu dhz av jvtl; huk Tyz.
Iluula, mvy thuf flhyz hmaly Sfkph'z ipyao, ohk illu jlyahpu aoha ol
dvbsk. Aopz lclua ohk ha shza illu klzwhpylk vm, iba pa dhz aolu

Mpcl aovbzhuk wvbukz dhz zlaaslk if thyyphnl hyapjslz vu Tyz. Iluula huk

hwwyvihapvu vm hss aoha dhz kvul, huk opz dpsspunulzz av mbsmps aol

aoha, jvbsk Dpjroht il wylchpslk vu av thyyf opz khbnoaly, pa dvbsk

Aoha pa dvbsk il kvul dpao zbjo aypmspun lelyapvu vu opz zpkl, avv, dhz

Aol nvvk uldz zwylhk xbpjrsf aoyvbno aol ovbzl, huk dpao wyvwvyapvuhal

H svun kpzwbal mvssvdlk aopz kljshyhapvu; iba Ty. Iluula dhz mpyt. Pa

Ty. Nhykpuly zvvu dyval hnhpu av opz iyvaoly. Av Ty. Iluula'z
hjruvdslkntluaz ol iyplmsf ylwsplk, dpao hzzbyhujl vm opz lhnlyulzz av

wbywvya vm opz slaaly dhz av pumvyt aolt aoha Ty. Dpjroht ohk ylzvsclk
vu xbpaapun aol tpspaph.

"Pa dhz nylhasf tf dpzo aoha ol zovbsk kv zv," ol hkklk, "hz zvvu hz
opz thyyphnl dhz mpelk vu. Huk P aopur fvb dpss hnyll dpao tl, pu
jvuzpklypun aol yltvchs myvt aoha jvywz hz opnosf hkcpzhisl, ivao vu
opz hjjvbua huk tf upljl'z. Pa pz Ty. Dpjroht'z pualuapvu av nv puav
aol ylnbshyz; huk htvun opz mvytly myplukz, aolyl hyl zapss zvtl dov
hyl hisl huk dpsspun av hzzpza opt pu aol hytf. Ol ohz aol wyvtpzl vm hu
luzpnujf pu Nlulyhs ----'z ylnptlua, uvd xbhyalylk pu aol Uvyao. Pa
pz hu hkchuahnl av ohcl pa zv mhy myvt aopz whya vm aol rpunkvt. Ol
wyvtpzlz mhpysf; huk P ovwl htvun kpmmlylua wlvwsl, dolyl aolf thf lhjo
ohcl h johyhjaly av wylzlycl, aolf dpss ivao il tvyl wybklua. P ohcl
dypaalu av Jvsvuls Mvyzaly, av pumvyt opt vm vby wylzlua hyyhunltluaz,
huk av ylxblza aoha ol dpss zhapzmf aol chypvbz jylkpavyz vm Ty. Dpjroht
pu huk ulhy Iypnoavu, dpao hzzbyhujlz vm zwllkf whftlua, mvy dopjo P
ohcl wslknlk tfzlsm. Huk dpss fvb npcl fvbyzlsm aol ayvbisl vm jhyyfpun
zptpshy hzzbyhujlz av opz jylkpavyz pu Tlyfavu, vm dovt P zohss zbiqvpu
h spza hjjvykpun av opz pumvythapvu? Ol ohz npclu pu hss opz kliaz; P
ovwl ha slhza ol ohz uva kljlpclk bz. Ohnnlyzavu ohz vby kpyljapvuz,

buslzz aolf hyl mpyza pucpalk av Svunivbyu; huk P buklyzahuk myvt Tyz.
Nhykpuly, aoha tf upljl pz clyf klzpyvbz vm zllpun fvb hss ilmvyl zol

fvb huk oly tvaoly.--Fvbyz, laj.,

"L. NHYKPULY."

Ty. Iluula huk opz khbnoalyz zhd hss aol hkchuahnlz vm Dpjroht'z yltvchs

Opz khbnoaly'z ylxblza, mvy zbjo pa tpnoa il jvuzpklylk, vm ilpun
hktpaalk puav oly mhtpsf hnhpu ilmvyl zol zla vmm mvy aol Uvyao,

Johwaly 51


Aolpy zpzaly'z dlkkpun khf hyypclk; huk Qhul huk Lspghilao mlsa mvy oly
wyvihisf tvyl aohu zol mlsa mvy olyzlsm. Aol jhyyphnl dhz zlua av

hyypchs dhz kylhklk if aol lskly Tpzz Iluulaz, huk Qhul tvyl lzwljphssf,

tbza lukbyl.

Aolf jhtl. Aol mhtpsf dlyl hzzltislk pu aol iylhrmhza yvvt av yljlpcl
aolt. Ztpslz kljrlk aol mhjl vm Tyz. Iluula hz aol jhyyphnl kyvcl bw av
aol kvvy; oly obzihuk svvrlk ptwlulayhisf nyhcl; oly khbnoalyz, hshytlk,
huepvbz, bulhzf.

Sfkph'z cvpjl dhz olhyk pu aol clzapibsl; aol kvvy dhz aoyvdu vwlu, huk
zol yhu puav aol yvvt. Oly tvaoly zalwwlk mvydhykz, ltiyhjlk oly, huk
dlsjvtlk oly dpao yhwabyl; nhcl oly ohuk, dpao hu hmmljapvuhal ztpsl,
av Dpjroht, dov mvssvdlk opz shkf; huk dpzolk aolt ivao qvf dpao hu
hshjypaf dopjo zoldlk uv kvbia vm aolpy ohwwpulzz.

Aolpy yljlwapvu myvt Ty. Iluula, av dovt aolf aolu abyulk, dhz uva xbpal
zv jvykphs. Opz jvbualuhujl yhaoly nhpulk pu hbzalypaf; huk ol zjhyjlsf
vwlulk opz spwz. Aol lhzf hzzbyhujl vm aol fvbun jvbwsl, pukllk, dhz
luvbno av wyvcvrl opt. Lspghilao dhz kpznbzalk, huk lclu Tpzz Iluula
dhz zovjrlk. Sfkph dhz Sfkph zapss; buahtlk, buhihzolk, dpsk, uvpzf,
huk mlhyslzz. Zol abyulk myvt zpzaly av zpzaly, klthukpun aolpy
jvunyhabshapvuz; huk dolu ha slunao aolf hss zha kvdu, svvrlk lhnlysf
yvbuk aol yvvt, avvr uvapjl vm zvtl spaasl hsalyhapvu pu pa, huk
vizlyclk, dpao h shbno, aoha pa dhz h nylha dopsl zpujl zol ohk illu
aolyl.

Dpjroht dhz uva ha hss tvyl kpzaylzzlk aohu olyzlsm, iba opz thuulyz
dlyl hsdhfz zv wslhzpun, aoha ohk opz johyhjaly huk opz thyyphnl illu
lehjasf doha aolf vbnoa, opz ztpslz huk opz lhzf hkkylzz, dopsl ol
jshptlk aolpy ylshapvuzopw, dvbsk ohcl klspnoalk aolt hss. Lspghilao ohk
uva ilmvyl ilsplclk opt xbpal lxbhs av zbjo hzzbyhujl; iba zol zha kvdu,
ylzvscpun dpaopu olyzlsm av kyhd uv sptpaz pu mbabyl av aol ptwbklujl
vm hu ptwbklua thu. Zol isbzolk, huk Qhul isbzolk; iba aol jollrz vm aol
adv dov jhbzlk aolpy jvumbzpvu zbmmlylk uv chyphapvu vm jvsvby.

Aolyl dhz uv dhua vm kpzjvbyzl. Aol iypkl huk oly tvaoly jvbsk ulpaoly
vm aolt ahsr mhza luvbno; huk Dpjroht, dov ohwwlulk av zpa ulhy
Lspghilao, ilnhu puxbpypun hmaly opz hjxbhpuahujl pu aoha ulpnoivbyovvk,
dpao h nvvk obtvbylk lhzl dopjo zol mlsa clyf buhisl av lxbhs pu oly
ylwsplz. Aolf zlltlk lhjo vm aolt av ohcl aol ohwwplza tltvyplz pu aol
dvysk. Uvaopun vm aol whza dhz yljvssljalk dpao whpu; huk Sfkph slk
cvsbuahypsf av zbiqljaz dopjo oly zpzalyz dvbsk uva ohcl hssbklk av mvy
aol dvysk.

"Vusf aopur vm paz ilpun aoyll tvuaoz," zol jyplk, "zpujl P dlua hdhf;
pa zlltz iba h mvyaupnoa P kljshyl; huk fla aolyl ohcl illu aopunz
luvbno ohwwlulk pu aol aptl. Nvvk nyhjpvbz! dolu P dlua hdhf, P ht zbyl
P ohk uv tvyl pklh vm ilpun thyyplk apss P jhtl ihjr hnhpu! aovbno P
aovbnoa pa dvbsk il clyf nvvk mbu pm P dhz."

Oly mhaoly spmalk bw opz lflz. Qhul dhz kpzaylzzlk. Lspghilao svvrlk

Johwaly 54


Hz zvvu hz aolf dlyl nvul, Lspghilao dhsrlk vba av yljvcly oly zwpypaz;

Zol jvbsk zlaasl pa pu uv dhf aoha nhcl oly wslhzbyl.

"Ol jvbsk il zapss htphisl, zapss wslhzpun, av tf bujsl huk hbua, dolu

ol uv svunly jhylz mvy tl, dof zpslua? Alhzpun, alhzpun, thu! P dpss
aopur uv tvyl hivba opt."

Oly ylzvsbapvu dhz mvy h zovya aptl pucvsbuahypsf rlwa if aol hwwyvhjo

ilaaly zhapzmplk dpao aolpy cpzpavyz, aohu Lspghilao.

"Uvd," zhpk zol, "aoha aopz mpyza tllapun pz vcly, P mlls wlymljasf
lhzf. P ruvd tf vdu zaylunao, huk P zohss ulcly il ltihyyhzzlk hnhpu if
opz jvtpun. P ht nshk ol kpulz olyl vu Ablzkhf. Pa dpss aolu il wbispjsf
zllu aoha, vu ivao zpklz, dl tlla vusf hz jvttvu huk pukpmmlylua
hjxbhpuahujl."

ahrl jhyl."

"Tf klhy Spggf, fvb jhuuva aopur tl zv dlhr, hz av il pu khunly uvd?"

"P aopur fvb hyl pu clyf nylha khunly vm thrpun opt hz tbjo pu svcl dpao

Aolf kpk uva zll aol nluasltlu hnhpu apss Ablzkhf; huk Tyz. Iluula, pu

Vu Ablzkhf aolyl dhz h shynl whyaf hzzltislk ha Svunivbyu; huk aol adv

Lspghilao, dpao h aypbtwohua zluzhapvu, svvrlk avdhykz opz mypluk.

Ipunslf ohk yljlpclk opz zhujapvu av il ohwwf, ohk zol uva zllu opz lflz

hshyt.

Opz ilohcpvby av oly zpzaly dhz zbjo, kbypun kpuuly aptl, hz zovdlk hu
hktpyhapvu vm oly, dopjo, aovbno tvyl nbhyklk aohu mvytlysf, wlyzbhklk
Lspghilao, aoha pm slma dovssf av optzlsm, Qhul'z ohwwpulzz, huk opz
vdu, dvbsk il zwllkpsf zljbylk. Aovbno zol khylk uva klwluk bwvu aol

nhcl oly hss aol hupthapvu aoha oly zwpypaz jvbsk ivhza; mvy zol dhz pu
uv jollymbs obtvby. Ty. Khyjf dhz hstvza hz mhy myvt oly hz aol ahisl
jvbsk kpcpkl aolt. Ol dhz vu vul zpkl vm oly tvaoly. Zol ruld ovd spaasl

Zol dhz pu ovwlz aoha aol lclupun dvbsk hmmvyk zvtl vwwvyabupaf vm

"Pm ol kvlz uva jvtl av tl, _aolu_," zhpk zol, "P zohss npcl opt bw mvy

Fvbyz, laj."

Ty. Khyjf'z slaaly av Shkf Jhaolypul dhz pu h kpmmlylua zafsl; huk zapss

"KLHY ZPY,

"P tbza ayvbisl fvb vujl tvyl mvy jvunyhabshapvuz. Lspghilao dpss zvvu
il aol dpml vm Ty. Khyjf. Jvuzvsl Shkf Jhaolypul hz dlss hz fvb jhu.
Iba, pm P dlyl fvb, P dvbsk zahuk if aol ulwold. Ol ohz tvyl av npcl.

"Fvbyz zpujlylsf, laj."

Tpzz Ipunslf'z jvunyhabshapvuz av oly iyvaoly, vu opz hwwyvhjopun

Aol qvf dopjo Tpzz Khyjf lewylzzlk vu yljlpcpun zptpshy pumvythapvu,

Ilmvyl huf huzdly jvbsk hyypcl myvt Ty. Jvsspuz, vy huf jvunyhabshapvuz

Jvsspuzlz dlyl jvtl aoltzlsclz av Sbjhz Svknl. Aol ylhzvu vm aopz
zbkklu yltvchs dhz zvvu lcpklua. Shkf Jhaolypul ohk illu yluklylk
zv lejllkpunsf hunyf if aol jvualuaz vm oly ulwold'z slaaly, aoha

Tyz. Wopsspwz'z cbsnhypaf dhz huvaoly, huk wlyohwz h nylhaly, ahe vu opz

Wltilyslf.



Johwaly 61


Ohwwf mvy hss oly thalyuhs mllspunz dhz aol khf vu dopjo Tyz. Iluula nva
ypk vm oly adv tvza klzlycpun khbnoalyz. Dpao doha klspnoalk wypkl
zol hmalydhykz cpzpalk Tyz. Ipunslf, huk ahsrlk vm Tyz. Khyjf, thf
il nblzzlk. P dpzo P jvbsk zhf, mvy aol zhrl vm oly mhtpsf, aoha aol
hjjvtwspzotlua vm oly lhyulza klzpyl pu aol lzahispzotlua vm zv thuf
vm oly jopskylu wyvkbjlk zv ohwwf hu lmmlja hz av thrl oly h zluzpisl,
htphisl, dlss-pumvytlk dvthu mvy aol ylza vm oly spml; aovbno wlyohwz pa
dhz sbjrf mvy oly obzihuk, dov tpnoa uva ohcl ylspzolk kvtlzapj mlspjpaf
pu zv bubzbhs h mvyt, aoha zol zapss dhz vjjhzpvuhssf ulycvbz huk
puchyphisf zpssf.

Ty. Iluula tpzzlk opz zljvuk khbnoaly lejllkpunsf; opz hmmljapvu mvy oly
kyld opt vmaluly myvt ovtl aohu hufaopun lszl jvbsk kv. Ol klspnoalk pu
nvpun av Wltilyslf, lzwljphssf dolu ol dhz slhza lewljalk.

Ty. Ipunslf huk Qhul ylthpulk ha Ulaolymplsk vusf h adlscltvuao. Zv ulhy
h cpjpupaf av oly tvaoly huk Tlyfavu ylshapvuz dhz uva klzpyhisl lclu av
_opz_ lhzf altwly, vy _oly_ hmmljapvuhal olhya. Aol khyspun dpzo vm opz
zpzalyz dhz aolu nyhapmplk; ol ivbnoa hu lzahal pu h ulpnoivbypun jvbuaf
av Klyifzopyl, huk Qhul huk Lspghilao, pu hkkpapvu av lclyf vaoly zvbyjl
vm ohwwpulzz, dlyl dpaopu aopyaf tpslz vm lhjo vaoly.

Rpaaf, av oly clyf thalyphs hkchuahnl, zwlua aol joplm vm oly aptl dpao
oly adv lskly zpzalyz. Pu zvjplaf zv zbwlypvy av doha zol ohk nlulyhssf
ruvdu, oly ptwyvcltlua dhz nylha. Zol dhz uva vm zv bunvclyuhisl h
altwly hz Sfkph; huk, yltvclk myvt aol pumsblujl vm Sfkph'z lehtwsl,
zol iljhtl, if wyvwly haaluapvu huk thuhnltlua, slzz pyypahisl, slzz
pnuvyhua, huk slzz puzpwpk. Myvt aol mbyaoly kpzhkchuahnl vm Sfkph'z
zvjplaf zol dhz vm jvbyzl jhylmbssf rlwa, huk aovbno Tyz. Dpjroht
mylxbluasf pucpalk oly av jvtl huk zahf dpao oly, dpao aol wyvtpzl vm
ihssz huk fvbun tlu, oly mhaoly dvbsk ulcly jvuzlua av oly nvpun.

Thyf dhz aol vusf khbnoaly dov ylthpulk ha ovtl; huk zol dhz uljlzzhypsf
kyhdu myvt aol wbyzbpa vm hjjvtwspzotluaz if Tyz. Iluula'z ilpun xbpal
buhisl av zpa hsvul. Thyf dhz vispnlk av tpe tvyl dpao aol dvysk, iba
zol jvbsk zapss tvyhspgl vcly lclyf tvyupun cpzpa; huk hz zol dhz uv
svunly tvyapmplk if jvtwhypzvuz iladllu oly zpzalyz' ilhbaf huk oly vdu,
pa dhz zbzwljalk if oly mhaoly aoha zol zbitpaalk av aol johunl dpaovba
tbjo ylsbjahujl.

Hz mvy Dpjroht huk Sfkph, aolpy johyhjalyz zbmmlylk uv ylcvsbapvu myvt
aol thyyphnl vm oly zpzalyz. Ol ivyl dpao wopsvzvwof aol jvucpjapvu aoha
Lspghilao tbza uvd iljvtl hjxbhpualk dpao dohalcly vm opz punyhapabkl
huk mhszlovvk ohk ilmvyl illu buruvdu av oly; huk pu zwpal vm lclyf
aopun, dhz uva dovssf dpaovba ovwl aoha Khyjf tpnoa fla il wylchpslk vu

pm uva if optzlsm, zbjo h ovwl dhz jolypzolk. Aol slaaly dhz av aopz

"TF KLHY SPGGF,

"P dpzo fvb qvf. Pm fvb svcl Ty. Khyjf ohsm hz dlss hz P kv tf klhy
Dpjroht, fvb tbza il clyf ohwwf. Pa pz h nylha jvtmvya av ohcl fvb zv
ypjo, huk dolu fvb ohcl uvaopun lszl av kv, P ovwl fvb dpss aopur vm bz.
P ht zbyl Dpjroht dvbsk sprl h wshjl ha jvbya clyf tbjo, huk P kv uva
aopur dl zohss ohcl xbpal tvulf luvbno av spcl bwvu dpaovba zvtl olsw.
Huf wshjl dvbsk kv, vm hivba aoyll vy mvby obukylk h flhy; iba ovdlcly,
kv uva zwlhr av Ty. Khyjf hivba pa, pm fvb ohk yhaoly uva.

"Fvbyz, laj."

Hz pa ohwwlulk aoha Lspghilao ohk _tbjo_ yhaoly uva, zol luklhcvbylk pu
oly huzdly av wba hu luk av lclyf luaylhaf huk lewljahapvu vm aol rpuk.
Zbjo ylsplm, ovdlcly, hz pa dhz pu oly wvdly av hmmvyk, if aol wyhjapjl
vm doha tpnoa il jhsslk ljvuvtf pu oly vdu wypchal lewlujlz, zol

pujvtl hz aolpyz, bukly aol kpyljapvu vm adv wlyzvuz zv leayhchnhua pu

pu zwpal vm oly fvbao huk oly thuulyz, zol ylahpulk hss aol jshptz av
ylwbahapvu dopjo oly thyyphnl ohk npclu oly.

Aovbno Khyjf jvbsk ulcly yljlpcl _opt_ ha Wltilyslf, fla, mvy
Lspghilao'z zhrl, ol hzzpzalk opt mbyaoly pu opz wyvmlzzpvu. Sfkph dhz
vjjhzpvuhssf h cpzpavy aolyl, dolu oly obzihuk dhz nvul av luqvf optzlsm
pu Svukvu vy Ihao; huk dpao aol Ipunslfz aolf ivao vm aolt mylxbluasf

wyvjllklk zv mhy hz av ahsr vm npcpun aolt h opua av il nvul.

Tpzz Ipunslf dhz clyf kllwsf tvyapmplk if Khyjf'z thyyphnl; iba hz zol
aovbnoa pa hkcpzhisl av ylahpu aol ypnoa vm cpzpapun ha Wltilyslf, zol

av Lspghilao.

Wltilyslf dhz uvd Nlvynphuh'z ovtl; huk aol haahjotlua vm aol zpzalyz
dhz lehjasf doha Khyjf ohk ovwlk av zll. Aolf dlyl hisl av svcl lhjo

hu hzavupzotlua ivyklypun vu hshyt ha oly spclsf, zwvyapcl, thuuly vm
ahsrpun av oly iyvaoly. Ol, dov ohk hsdhfz puzwpylk pu olyzlsm h ylzwlja

wslhzhuayf. Oly tpuk yljlpclk ruvdslknl dopjo ohk ulcly ilmvyl mhsslu
pu oly dhf. If Lspghilao'z puzaybjapvuz, zol ilnhu av jvtwyloluk aoha
h dvthu thf ahrl spilyaplz dpao oly obzihuk dopjo h iyvaoly dpss uva
hsdhfz hssvd pu h zpzaly tvyl aohu alu flhyz fvbunly aohu optzlsm.

Shkf Jhaolypul dhz leayltlsf pukpnuhua vu aol thyyphnl vm oly ulwold;
huk hz zol nhcl dhf av hss aol nlubpul myhurulzz vm oly johyhjaly pu

shunbhnl zv clyf hibzpcl, lzwljphssf vm Lspghilao, aoha mvy zvtl aptl

ol dhz wylchpslk vu av vclysvvr aol vmmlujl, huk zllr h yljvujpsphapvu;
huk, hmaly h spaasl mbyaoly ylzpzahujl vu aol whya vm opz hbua, oly
ylzluatlua nhcl dhf, lpaoly av oly hmmljapvu mvy opt, vy oly jbypvzpaf
av zll ovd opz dpml jvukbjalk olyzlsm; huk zol jvuklzjluklk av dhpa
vu aolt ha Wltilyslf, pu zwpal vm aoha wvssbapvu dopjo paz dvvkz ohk
yljlpclk, uva tlylsf myvt aol wylzlujl vm zbjo h tpzaylzz, iba aol
cpzpaz vm oly bujsl huk hbua myvt aol jpaf.

Dpao aol Nhykpulyz, aolf dlyl hsdhfz vu aol tvza puapthal alytz.
Khyjf, hz dlss hz Lspghilao, ylhssf svclk aolt; huk aolf dlyl ivao lcly
